// KV allows one write per second per key. Traffic is therefore buffered in memory and
// persisted only after 1 GB or every 15 minutes (and once more when a tunnel closes).
const USER_TRAFFIC_FLUSH_BYTES = 1024 * 1024 * 1024;
const USER_TRAFFIC_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
// Isolate-level usage cache: handshakes read/update usage from RAM instead of hitting KV.
const USAGE_CACHE_TTL_MS = 60 * 1000;
const USAGE_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES = 2000;
const KV_MIN_WRITE_INTERVAL_MS = 1100;
// Worker subrequest budget kept for DoH lookups inside one tunnel (hard platform cap: 50 on Free).
const DNS_DOH_SUBREQUEST_LIMIT = 45;
// Isolate-level DoH answer cache: identical queries are answered locally instead of
// spending a subrequest. Keyed by the query bytes without the 2-byte transaction ID.
const DOH_CACHE_TTL_MS = 4 * 60 * 1000;
const DOH_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES = 1000;
const DOH_CACHE_MAX_QUERY_BYTES = 512;
const dohDnsCache = new Map();
function dohCacheKey(packet, dohUrl) {
  if (!packet || packet.length < 12 || packet.length > DOH_CACHE_MAX_QUERY_BYTES) return null;
  let bin = "";
  for (let i = 2; i < packet.length; i++) bin += String.fromCharCode(packet[i]);
  return String(dohUrl || DEFAULT_DOH_URL) + "|" + bin;
}
function dohCacheGet(key) {
  const hit = dohDnsCache.get(key);
  if (!hit) return null;
  if (hit.expires <= Date.now()) { dohDnsCache.delete(key); return null; }
  return hit.answer.slice();
}
function dohCachePut(key, answer) {
  // Cache only clean, complete answers: NOERROR, at least one answer record, not truncated.
  if (!key || answer.length < 12 || (answer[2] & 0x02) || (answer[3] & 0x0f) !== 0 || ((answer[6] << 8) | answer[7]) === 0) return;
  const now = Date.now();
  dohDnsCache.delete(key);
  if (dohDnsCache.size >= DOH_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES) {
    for (const [k, v] of dohDnsCache) if (v.expires <= now) dohDnsCache.delete(k);
    while (dohDnsCache.size >= DOH_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES) dohDnsCache.delete(dohDnsCache.keys().next().value);
  }
  dohDnsCache.set(key, { answer: answer.slice(), expires: now + DOH_CACHE_TTL_MS });
}

const userActiveConnections = new Map();
const userUsageLocks = new Map();
// Short-lived (60 s) in-memory copy of the raw usage records, keyed by KV key.
// Entry: { raw: <usage object | null>, expires: <ms> }
const usageMemoryCache = new Map();
// Pending/in-flight KV writes per usage key (coalesced: only the latest value is written).
const usageWriteStates = new Map();
// In-flight KV reads per usage key, so a cold/expired cache triggers one read, not one per handshake.
const usageReadInflight = new Map();
// Per-isolate byte ledger prevents concurrent tunnels in the same Worker isolate
// from each spending the same stale KV balance.
const userTrafficState = new Map();
// Keep local close callbacks so credential rotation can terminate old tunnels.
const userConnectionClosers = new Map();
// Local fast revocation protects this isolate while KV changes propagate.
const revokedCredentialKeys = new Set();
const ALLOWED_FINGERPRINTS = new Set(["chrome", "firefox", "edge", "safari", "360", "qq", "ios", "android", "random", "randomized"]);

function credentialRevocationKey(kind, value) {
  return kind + ":" + String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
}
function revokeCredential(kind, value) {
  if (!value) return;
  revokedCredentialKeys.add(credentialRevocationKey(kind, value));
  while (revokedCredentialKeys.size > 5000) revokedCredentialKeys.delete(revokedCredentialKeys.values().next().value);
}
function isCredentialRevoked(kind, value) {
  return !!value && revokedCredentialKeys.has(credentialRevocationKey(kind, value));
}
function getLocalUserTrafficState(userId, usage) {
  const id = String(userId);
  const periodStartedAt = Number(usage?.periodStartedAt) || 0;
  const stored = userTrafficState.get(id);
  if (!stored || stored.periodStartedAt !== periodStartedAt) {
    const fresh = { periodStartedAt, usageBytes: Math.max(0, Number(usage?.usageBytes) || 0) };
    userTrafficState.set(id, fresh);
    return fresh;
  }
  stored.usageBytes = Math.max(stored.usageBytes, Math.max(0, Number(usage?.usageBytes) || 0));
  return stored;
}
function registerUserConnection(userId, closeCallback) {
  const id = String(userId || "default");
  let callbacks = userConnectionClosers.get(id);
  if (!callbacks) userConnectionClosers.set(id, callbacks = new Set());
  callbacks.add(closeCallback);
}
function unregisterUserConnection(userId, closeCallback) {
  const id = String(userId || "default");
  const callbacks = userConnectionClosers.get(id);
  if (!callbacks) return;
  callbacks.delete(closeCallback);
  if (!callbacks.size) userConnectionClosers.delete(id);
}
async function closeUserConnections(userId) {
  const callbacks = [...(userConnectionClosers.get(String(userId || "default")) || [])];
  await Promise.allSettled(callbacks.map(callback => Promise.resolve().then(() => callback())));
}
async function invalidateChangedCredentials(previous, next) {
  if (!previous || !next) return;
  const changedIds = [];
  const invalidate = (oldItem, newItem, connectionId) => {
    let changed = false;
    if (oldItem.uuid && oldItem.uuid !== newItem?.uuid) { revokeCredential("vless", oldItem.uuid); changed = true; }
    if (oldItem.trojanPassword && oldItem.trojanPassword !== newItem?.trojanPassword) {
      revokeCredential("trojanHash", sha224Hex(oldItem.trojanPassword)); changed = true;
    }
    if (oldItem.subToken && oldItem.subToken !== newItem?.subToken) { revokeCredential("sub", oldItem.subToken); changed = true; }
    if (changed) changedIds.push(connectionId);
  };
  invalidate(previous, next, "default");
  const nextUsers = new Map((Array.isArray(next.users) ? next.users : []).map(user => [String(user.id), user]));
  for (const oldUser of Array.isArray(previous.users) ? previous.users : []) {
    invalidate(oldUser, nextUsers.get(String(oldUser.id)), String(oldUser.id));
  }
  await Promise.all(changedIds.map(id => closeUserConnections(id)));
}

const FRAGMENT_PROFILES = {
  hamrah:     { length: "10-30",  interval: "10-20" },
  irancell:   { length: "5-15",   interval: "5-15" },
  rightel:    { length: "20-50",  interval: "10-30" },
  mokhaberat: { length: "50-100", interval: "20-40" },
  gaming:     { length: "1-5",    interval: "1-5" },
  custom:     null
};

function applyFragmentProfile(settings) {
  const p = FRAGMENT_PROFILES[settings.fragmentProfile];
  if (p) {
    settings.fragmentLength = p.length;
    settings.fragmentInterval = p.interval;
    settings.fragmentEnabled = true;
  }
  return settings;
}

function getD1Binding(env) {
  const db = env && env.DB;
  return db && typeof db.prepare === "function" && typeof db.batch === "function" ? db : null;
}

async function ensurePanelD1Schema() {
  if (!PANEL_DB) return;
  if (!panelSchemaPromise) {
    panelSchemaPromise = (async () => {
      await PANEL_DB.batch([
        PANEL_DB.prepare("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS panel_meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL)"),
        PANEL_DB.prepare("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS panel_users (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL, enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, data_json TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL)"),
        PANEL_DB.prepare("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_panel_users_name ON panel_users(name)"),
        PANEL_DB.prepare("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS panel_exit_nodes (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, country TEXT NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, data_json TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL)"),
        PANEL_DB.prepare("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_panel_exit_nodes_country ON panel_exit_nodes(country)"),
        PANEL_DB.prepare("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS panel_tg_proxy (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL)")
      ]);
    })().catch(error => { panelSchemaPromise = null; throw error; });
  }
  await panelSchemaPromise;
}

function parseStoredJson(value, fallback = null) {
  if (typeof value === "string") { try { return JSON.parse(value); } catch { return fallback; } }
  return value && typeof value === "object" ? value : fallback;
}

async function readD1Meta(key) {
  if (!PANEL_DB) return null;
  const row = await PANEL_DB.prepare("SELECT value FROM panel_meta WHERE key = ?1").bind(String(key)).first();
  return row ? parseStoredJson(row.value, null) : null;
}

async function getKvBinding(env) {
  const isKv = value => value && typeof value.get === "function" && typeof value.put === "function";
  for (const name of ["SETTINGS", "PANEL_KV", "KV"]) if (isKv(env[name])) return env[name];
  const candidates = Object.entries(env).filter(([name, value]) => !["ASSETS"].includes(name) && isKv(value));
  return candidates.length === 1 ? candidates[0][1] : null;
}

export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    try {
      const url = new URL(request.url);
      const path = url.pathname;
      PANEL_DB = getD1Binding(env);
      const kv = await getKvBinding(env);

      if (path === "/healthz") {
        return new Response("ok", { headers: securityHeaders({ "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8" }) });
      }
      if (path === "/vless" || path === "/trojan") {
        if (request.headers.get("Upgrade")?.toLowerCase() !== "websocket") return fakeLandingResponse();
        if (!(await checkClientIpRestriction(request, env, kv))) {
          return json({ error: "Access Denied", code: "ERR_IP_RESTRICTED" }, 403);
        }
        return await handleTunnel(request, env, kv, path.slice(1), ctx, url.searchParams.get("exit") || "");
      }
      if (path.startsWith("/ss/")) {
        if (request.headers.get("Upgrade")?.toLowerCase() !== "websocket") return fakeLandingResponse();
        if (!(await checkClientIpRestriction(request, env, kv))) return json({ error: "Access Denied", code: "ERR_IP_RESTRICTED" }, 403);
        let token = "";
        try { token = decodeURIComponent(path.slice(4)); } catch { return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 }); }
        return await handleShadowsocksTunnel(request, env, kv, token, ctx, url.searchParams.get("exit") || "");
      }
      if (path.startsWith("/sub/")) {
        if (!(await checkClientIpRestriction(request, env, kv))) {
          return json({ error: "Access Denied", code: "ERR_IP_RESTRICTED" }, 403);
        }
        return await handleSubscription(request, env, kv, url);
      }
      if (path.startsWith("/telegram/") && request.method === "GET") {
        return await handleTelegramLandingPage(request, env, kv, url);
      }
      // --- TWP-CF (Telegram Web Proxy) routes: data plane is authenticated by bridge/session tokens.
      if (path === "/" && url.searchParams.has("bridge") && request.method === "GET") {
        const twpConfig = await loadTwpConfig(kv);
        if (!twpConfig.enabled || !TWP_RUNTIME.isValidSecret(twpConfig.secret) || !twpConfig.boot) return fakeLandingResponse();
        return await TWP_RUNTIME.handleBridgePage(request, url, twpConfig);
      }
      if (path === "/api/v1/session" && (request.method === "POST" || request.method === "DELETE")) {
        const twpConfig = await loadTwpConfig(kv);
        if (!twpConfig.enabled || !TWP_RUNTIME.isValidSecret(twpConfig.secret) || !twpConfig.boot) return new Response(null, { status: 404, headers: securityHeaders({ "cache-control": "no-store" }) });
        return await TWP_RUNTIME.handleSession(request, twpConfig);
      }
      if (path === "/api/v1/ws" && request.headers.get("Upgrade")?.toLowerCase() === "websocket") {
        const twpConfig = await loadTwpConfig(kv);
        if (!twpConfig.enabled || !TWP_RUNTIME.isValidSecret(twpConfig.secret) || !twpConfig.boot) return new Response(null, { status: 404, headers: securityHeaders({ "cache-control": "no-store" }) });
        return await TWP_RUNTIME.handleWs(request, twpConfig, url);
      }
      // --- End TWP-CF data-plane routes ---
      if (path === "/api/setup-status" && request.method === "GET") {
        const configured = await isSetupDone(kv, env);
        return json({ configured });
      }
      if (path === "/api/setup" && request.method === "POST") {
        return await handleSetup(request, kv, env, url);
      }
      if (path === "/api/login" && request.method === "POST") {
        return await handleLogin(request, kv, env, url);
      }
      if (path.startsWith("/api/")) {
        if (!(await hasSession(request, kv, env))) return json({ error: "Unauthorized", code: "ERR_UNAUTHORIZED" }, 401);
        if (request.method !== "GET" && !sameOrigin(request, url)) return json({ error: "Bad Origin", code: "ERR_BAD_ORIGIN" }, 403);

        if (path === "/api/twp/config") return await handleTwpAdminConfig(request, env, kv, url);
        if (path === "/api/ping-ip" && request.method === "POST") return await handlePingIp(request);
        if (path === "/api/telegram-proxies/test-active" && request.method === "POST") {
          const settings = await loadSettings(kv, env);
          const nodes = getActiveExitNodes(settings).filter(node => node.kind === "socks5");
          const results = await mapWithConcurrency(nodes, 4, async node => ({
            id: node.id, name: node.name, country: node.country, ...await testTelegramSocks5Proxy(node.uri)
          }));
          return json({ results });
        }
        if (path === "/api/telegram-proxies/sync" && request.method === "POST") {
          try {
            const body = await readJsonLimited(request, 8192);
            const result = await fetchAndTestTelegramProxySources(body.sources);
            return json(result);
          } catch (err) {
            return json({ error: String(err?.message || "دریافت یا آزمایش منابع پروکسی تلگرام ناموفق بود").slice(0, 240), code: "ERR_TELEGRAM_PROXY_SYNC" }, 400);
          }
        }
        if (path === "/api/external-ping" && request.method === "POST") return await handleExternalPing(request, kv, env, url);
        if (path === "/api/cleanips/sync" && request.method === "POST") {
          if (!kv) return json({ error: "KV Binding یافت نشد؛ ذخیره و همگام‌سازی Clean IP ممکن نیست.", code: "ERR_KV_MISSING" }, 503);
          try {
            const settings = await loadSettings(kv, env);
            const synced = await fetchCleanIpLists(settings.cleanIpSources || "");
            settings.cleanIPs = synced.cleanIPs;
            await saveSettings(kv, env, settings);
            await attachUserUsage(settings, kv);
            return json({ ok: true, count: synced.count, sources: synced.sources, settings: publicSettings(settings, url) });
          } catch (err) {
            return json({ error: String(err && err.message || "همگام‌سازی Clean IP ناموفق بود").slice(0, 220), code: "ERR_CLEANIP_SYNC" }, 502);
          }
        }
        if (path === "/api/placement/credentials" && request.method === "GET") {
          try {
            return json(await discoverPlacementCredentials(env, kv, url));
          } catch (err) {
            return json({ error: String(err?.message || "دریافت اطلاعات Cloudflare ناموفق بود").slice(0, 220), code: "ERR_CF_DISCOVERY" }, 502);
          }
        }
        if (path === "/api/placement/token" && request.method === "POST") {
          try {
            const body = await readJsonLimited(request, 2048);
            const token = await createPlacementApiToken(env, body.accountId, body.bootstrapToken);
            // The generated scoped token is non-expiring; its secret is returned once and never persisted by this Worker.
            return json({ ok: true, apiToken: token.token, tokenId: token.id, tokenName: token.name, expiresOn: null, permanent: true, accountId: token.accountId });
          } catch (err) {
            return json({ error: String(err?.message || "ساخت API Token ناموفق بود").slice(0, 260), code: "ERR_CF_TOKEN_CREATE" }, 400);
          }
        }
        if (path === "/api/placement/apply" && request.method === "POST") {
          try {
            const body = await readJsonLimited(request, 4096);
            const placement = validateWorkerPlacement(body.workerPlacement || body);
            const credentials = {
              accountId: String(body.accountId || "").trim(),
              workerName: String(body.workerName || "").trim(),
              apiToken: String(body.apiToken || "").trim()
            };
            if (credentials.apiToken.length > 512) throw new Error("API Token ارسالی نامعتبر یا بیش از حد طولانی است.");
            const result = await applyCloudflareWorkerPlacement(env, placement, credentials);
            return json({ ok: true, placement, status: result?.result?.placement || result?.result || null });
          } catch (err) {
            return json({ error: String(err?.message || "اعمال محل اجرای Worker ناموفق بود").slice(0, 260), code: "ERR_PLACEMENT_APPLY" }, 502);
          }
        }
        if (path === "/api/settings" && request.method === "GET") {
          const s = await loadSettings(kv, env);
          await attachUserUsage(s, kv);
          const responseSettings = publicSettings(s, url);
          responseSettings.tcpTestHistory = await loadTcpTestHistory(kv);
          return json(responseSettings);
        }
        if (path === "/api/settings" && request.method === "POST") {
          let next;
          try {
            const body = await readJsonLimited(request, 65536);
            next = validateSettings(body, env);
          } catch (err) {
            return json({ error: err.message, code: "ERR_VALIDATION" }, 400);
          }
          const current = await loadSettings(kv, env);
          next.subToken = current.subToken;
          next.passwordHash = current.passwordHash;
          next.sessionSecret = current.sessionSecret;
          await saveSettings(kv, env, next);
          await invalidateChangedCredentials(current, next);
          await reconcileUserUsageConfigs(kv, current.users, next.users);
          await attachUserUsage(next, kv);
          return json({ ok: true, settings: publicSettings(next, url) });
        }
        if (path === "/api/rotate-token" && request.method === "POST") {
          const previous = await loadSettings(kv, env);
          const s = { ...previous };
          s.subToken = await generateSecureToken();
          // Rotate all root credentials together so no legacy transport credential survives.
          s.uuid = crypto.randomUUID();
          s.trojanPassword = generateSecureRandomString(32);
          await saveSettings(kv, env, s);
          await invalidateChangedCredentials(previous, s);
          await attachUserUsage(s, kv);
          return json({ ok: true, subToken: s.subToken, uuid: s.uuid, trojanPassword: s.trojanPassword, links: publicSettings(s, url).links, settings: publicSettings(s, url) });
        }
        if ((path === "/api/users/reset-usage" || path === "/api/users/rotate-credentials") && request.method === "POST") {
          if (!kv) return json({ error: "KV Binding یافت نشد.", code: "ERR_KV_MISSING" }, 503);
          try {
            const body = await readJsonLimited(request, 2048);
            const id = String(body.id || "").trim();
            if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,64}$/.test(id)) return json({ error: "شناسه کاربر نامعتبر است.", code: "ERR_USER_ID_INVALID" }, 400);
            const result = await withUserUsageLock(id, async () => {
              const s = await loadSettings(kv, env);
              const user = (s.users || []).find(item => String(item.id) === id);
              if (!user) return null;
              const previous = { ...user };
              user.uuid = crypto.randomUUID();
              user.trojanPassword = generateSecureRandomString(32);
              user.subToken = await generateSecureToken();
              await saveSettings(kv, env, s);
              if (previous.uuid) revokeCredential("vless", previous.uuid);
              if (previous.trojanPassword) revokeCredential("trojanHash", sha224Hex(previous.trojanPassword));
              if (previous.subToken) revokeCredential("sub", previous.subToken);
              return { user };
            });
            if (!result) return json({ error: "کاربر پیدا نشد.", code: "ERR_USER_NOT_FOUND" }, 404);
            // Await closing/persisting old sessions before writing the zeroed ledger.
            await closeUserConnections(id);
            const resetUsage = await withUserUsageLock(id, async () => {
              const currentSettings = await loadSettings(kv, env);
              const currentUser = (currentSettings.users || []).find(item => String(item.id) === id);
              if (!currentUser) throw new Error("کاربر پس از بازنشانی پیدا نشد.");
              const usage = await getUserUsage(kv, currentUser, { fresh: true });
              const now = Date.now();
              const nextUsage = { ...usage, usageBytes: 0, requestCount: 0, periodStartedAt: now };
              await saveUserUsage(kv, id, nextUsage);
              userTrafficState.set(id, { periodStartedAt: now, usageBytes: 0 });
              return nextUsage;
            });
            return json({ ok: true, message: "حجم و شمارنده صفر شد؛ توکن، UUID و رمز Trojan جدید شدند و اشتراک و کانفیگ قبلی منسوخ شد. تاریخ انقضا تغییر نکرد.", usage: resetUsage, settings: publicSettings(await loadSettings(kv, env), url) });
          } catch (err) {
            return json({ error: String(err && err.message || "بازنشانی و ابطال اشتراک ناموفق بود").slice(0, 180), code: "ERR_USER_RESET" }, 400);
          }
        }
        if (path === "/api/backup" && request.method === "GET") {
          const s = await loadSettings(kv, env);
          const exportData = { ...s };
          delete exportData.passwordHash;
          delete exportData.sessionSecret;
          delete exportData.subToken;
          delete exportData.uuid;
          delete exportData.trojanPassword;
          delete exportData.chainProxy;
          delete exportData.chainProxies;
          exportData.users = (Array.isArray(s.users) ? s.users : []).map(user => ({
            ...user, uuid: "", trojanPassword: "", subToken: ""
          }));
          exportData.backupNotice = "Credentials are intentionally excluded. User credentials are regenerated on restore; reset the legacy root credentials manually.";
          return new Response(JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2), {
            headers: securityHeaders({
              "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
              "content-disposition": "attachment; filename=cloudflare-worker-backup.json"
            })
          });
        }
        if (path === "/api/restore" && request.method === "POST") {
          try {
            const body = await readJsonLimited(request, 65536);
            const current = await loadSettings(kv, env);
            const validated = validateSettings(body, env);
            validated.passwordHash = current.passwordHash;
            validated.sessionSecret = current.sessionSecret;
            validated.subToken = current.subToken;
            validated.uuid = validated.uuid || current.uuid;
            validated.trojanPassword = validated.trojanPassword || current.trojanPassword;
            await saveSettings(kv, env, validated);
            await invalidateChangedCredentials(current, validated);
            await reconcileUserUsageConfigs(kv, current.users, validated.users);
            await attachUserUsage(validated, kv);
            return json({ ok: true, settings: publicSettings(validated, url) });
          } catch (err) {
            return json({ error: err.message, code: "ERR_RESTORE_FAILED" }, 400);
          }
        }
        if (path === "/api/logout" && request.method === "POST") {
          return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), {
            headers: securityHeaders({
              "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
              "set-cookie": "wp_session=; Path=/; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict; Max-Age=0"
            })
          });
        }
        return json({ error: "not found", code: "ERR_NOT_FOUND" }, 404);
      }

      if (path === "/panel" || path === "/panel/" || path === "/panel/index.html") {
        return new Response(ADMIN_HTML, {
          headers: securityHeaders({ "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store" })
        });
      }
      if (path === "/" || path === "/index.html") return fakeLandingResponse();
      return fakeLandingResponse();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Internal Error:", err);
      return json({ error: "Internal Server Error", code: "ERR_INTERNAL" }, 500);
    }
  },
  async scheduled(controller, env, ctx) {
    PANEL_DB = getD1Binding(env);
    ctx.waitUntil((async () => {
      try {
        const kv = await getKvBinding(env);
        if (!kv) throw new Error("KV Binding یافت نشد؛ همگام‌سازی زمان‌بندی‌شده متوقف شد.");
        const settings = await loadSettings(kv, env);
        const synced = await fetchCleanIpLists(settings.cleanIpSources || "");
        const sample = parseCleanIPs(synced.cleanIPs).slice(0, 1).map(item => item.host);
        const tests = await Promise.all(sample.map(host => checkHostExternalTcp(host, settings.tlsPort || 443, 0, env)));
        settings.cleanIPs = sortCleanIpListByTestResults(synced.cleanIPs, tests);
        await saveSettings(kv, env, settings);
        console.log(`Scheduled Clean IP sync complete: ${synced.count} addresses, ${tests.filter(x => x.status === "ok").length} sampled TCP success(es), ${synced.sources.length} source(s).`);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("Scheduled Clean IP sync failed:", String(err && err.message || err).slice(0, 220));
      }
    })());
  }
};

// -------------------- Telegram SOCKS5 proxy helpers --------------------
const TELEGRAM_PROXY_TEST_TARGET = "149.154.167.51";
const TELEGRAM_PROXY_SOURCE_HOSTS = new Set([
  "raw.githubusercontent.com", "gist.githubusercontent.com", "pastebin.com", "www.pastebin.com", "github.com"
]);
const TELEGRAM_PROXY_SOURCE_MAX_BYTES = 256 * 1024;
const TELEGRAM_PROXY_SOURCE_MAX_COUNT = 5;
const TELEGRAM_PROXY_CANDIDATE_MAX = 40;

function buildTelegramProxyDetails(node, origin = "") {
  if (!node || node.enabled === false || node.kind !== "socks5") return null;
  let proxy;
  try { proxy = parseExitProxyUri(node.uri); } catch { return null; }
  if (proxy.kind !== "socks5") return null;
  const server = String(proxy.hostname || "").replace(/^\[|\]$/g, "");
  if (!server || !Number.isInteger(proxy.port) || proxy.port < 1) return null;
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  params.set("server", server);
  params.set("port", String(proxy.port));
  if (proxy.username) params.set("user", proxy.username);
  if (proxy.password) params.set("pass", proxy.password);
  const query = params.toString();
  return {
    id: String(node.id), name: String(node.name || "Telegram SOCKS5"), country: String(node.country || "UN"),
    server, port: proxy.port, tgUrl: "tg://socks?" + query, webUrl: "https://t.me/socks?" + query,
    workerUrl: String(origin || "").replace(/\/$/, "") + "/telegram/" + encodeURIComponent(String(node.id))
  };
}

function buildTelegramProxyInventory(settings, origin = "") {
  return getActiveExitNodes(settings).map(node => buildTelegramProxyDetails(node, origin)).filter(Boolean);
}

function htmlEscape(value) {
  return String(value == null ? "" : value).replace(/[&<>"']/g, ch => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[ch]));
}

async function handleTelegramLandingPage(request, env, kv, url) {
  if (!(await hasSession(request, kv, env))) {
    return new Response("برای مشاهده لینک اتصال، ابتدا وارد پنل مدیریت شوید.", {
      status: 401, headers: securityHeaders({ "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store" })
    });
  }
  let id = "";
  try { id = decodeURIComponent(url.pathname.slice("/telegram/".length)); } catch {}
  if (!id || id.length > 64 || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(id)) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const settings = await loadSettings(kv, env);
  const node = getActiveExitNodes(settings).find(item => String(item.id) === id);
  const details = node ? buildTelegramProxyDetails(node, url.origin) : null;
  if (!details) return new Response("Telegram SOCKS5 proxy not found", { status: 404 });
  const tg = htmlEscape(details.tgUrl), web = htmlEscape(details.webUrl);
  const html = `<!doctype html><html lang="fa" dir="rtl"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><meta name="referrer" content="no-referrer"><title>اتصال تلگرام - ${htmlEscape(details.name)}</title><style>body{margin:0;background:#080d1a;color:#e8f0ff;font:16px Vazirmatn,system-ui,sans-serif;display:grid;min-height:100vh;place-items:center}.card{width:min(520px,calc(100% - 36px));box-sizing:border-box;padding:28px;border:1px solid #28405d;border-radius:22px;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#151c31,#0b1222);box-shadow:0 20px 60px #0008}h1{font-size:22px;margin:0 0 12px}p{color:#9eafc8;line-height:1.9}.btn{display:block;text-align:center;text-decoration:none;margin-top:12px;padding:13px;border-radius:12px;background:#229ed9;color:white;font-weight:700}.secondary{background:#18283d;border:1px solid #38516e}</style></head><body><main class="card"><h1>اتصال به پروکسی تلگرام</h1><p>${htmlEscape(details.name)} · ${htmlEscape(details.country)}<br><span dir="ltr">${htmlEscape(details.server)}:${details.port}</span></p><a class="btn" href="${tg}">باز کردن مستقیم در Telegram</a><a class="btn secondary" href="${web}">باز کردن صفحه رسمی t.me/socks</a><p>در صورت نصب بودن تلگرام، گزینه اول را انتخاب کنید. این صفحه فقط لینک پراکسی SOCKS5 ذخیره‌شده در پنل را باز می‌کند.</p></main></body></html>`;
  return new Response(html, { headers: securityHeaders({ "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store" }) });
}

function isSafePublicTelegramProxyHost(host) {
  const value = String(host || "").replace(/^\[|\]$/g, "").toLowerCase();
  if (!value || value === "localhost" || value.endsWith(".localhost") || value.endsWith(".local") || value.endsWith(".internal") || value.endsWith(".test") || value.endsWith(".example") || value.endsWith(".invalid") || value.endsWith(".onion")) return false;
  if (isIPv4Text(value)) {
    const reserved = ["0.0.0.0/8", "10.0.0.0/8", "100.64.0.0/10", "127.0.0.0/8", "169.254.0.0/16", "172.16.0.0/12", "192.0.0.0/24", "192.168.0.0/16", "198.18.0.0/15", "198.51.100.0/24", "203.0.113.0/24", "224.0.0.0/4", "240.0.0.0/4"];
    return !reserved.some(cidr => ipInCidr(value, cidr));
  }
  if (isIPv6Text(value)) {
    const reserved = ["::/128", "::1/128", "fc00::/7", "fe80::/10", "ff00::/8", "2001:db8::/32"];
    return !reserved.some(cidr => ipInCidr(value, cidr));
  }
  return isDomainName(value);
}

function normalizeTelegramProxySourceLine(line) {
  let value = String(line || "").trim().replace(/&amp;/gi, "&").replace(/^[\s"'`]+|[\s"'`,;)}\]]+$/g, "");
  if (!value || value.startsWith("#") || value.startsWith("//")) return "";
  const normalized = normalizeTelegramProxy(value);
  if (/^(?:socks5?|tg):\/\//i.test(normalized)) return normalized;
  // Common raw lists also publish host:port or host:port:user:pass records.
  let m = /^\[([^\]]+)\]:(\d{1,5})(?::([^:]*):(.+))?$/.exec(value);
  if (!m) m = /^([^:\s]+):(\d{1,5})(?::([^:]*):(.+))?$/.exec(value);
  if (!m) return "";
  const server = m[1], port = Number(m[2]), user = m[3] || "", pass = m[4] || "";
  if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) return "";
  const host = server.includes(":") ? "[" + server.replace(/^\[|\]$/g, "") + "]" : server;
  const auth = (user || pass) ? encodeURIComponent(user) + ":" + encodeURIComponent(pass) + "@" : "";
  return "socks5://" + auth + host + ":" + port;
}

function extractTelegramProxyUris(sourceText) {
  const text = String(sourceText || "").replace(/&amp;/gi, "&");
  const found = [];
  const add = value => { const normalized = normalizeTelegramProxySourceLine(value); if (normalized) found.push(normalized); };
  // Parse JSON feeds in addition to one-URI-per-line text lists.
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
    const rows = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : (Array.isArray(parsed?.proxies) ? parsed.proxies : (Array.isArray(parsed?.data) ? parsed.data : []));
    for (const row of rows.slice(0, TELEGRAM_PROXY_CANDIDATE_MAX * 2)) {
      if (typeof row === "string") add(row);
      else if (row && typeof row === "object") {
        const direct = row.uri || row.url || row.link || row.proxy || row.socks5;
        if (direct) add(direct);
        else if ((row.server || row.host || row.ip) && row.port) {
          const host = String(row.server || row.host || row.ip);
          const user = String(row.user || row.username || ""), pass = String(row.pass || row.password || "");
          const authority = (user || pass) ? encodeURIComponent(user) + ":" + encodeURIComponent(pass) + "@" : "";
          add("socks5://" + authority + (host.includes(":") && !host.startsWith("[") ? "[" + host + "]" : host) + ":" + Number(row.port));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch {}
  const uriPattern = /(?:tg:\/\/socks\?[^\s"'<>]+|https?:\/\/t\.me\/socks\?[^\s"'<>]+|socks5?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+)/gi;
  for (const match of text.matchAll(uriPattern)) add(match[0]);
  for (const line of text.split(/[\r\n]+/)) add(line.split("#", 1)[0]);
  return [...new Set(found)].slice(0, TELEGRAM_PROXY_CANDIDATE_MAX);
}

async function testTelegramSocks5Proxy(proxyUri) {
  let socket, reader, writer, timer;
  const started = Date.now();
  try {
    const proxy = parseExitProxyUri(proxyUri);
    if (proxy.kind !== "socks5") throw new Error("فقط SOCKS5 برای Telegram پشتیبانی می‌شود.");
    if (!isSafePublicTelegramProxyHost(proxy.hostname)) throw new Error("آدرس پراکسی عمومی معتبر نیست.");
    socket = connect({ hostname: proxy.hostname, port: proxy.port }, { secureTransport: "off", allowHalfOpen: true });
    await Promise.race([socket.opened, new Promise((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("اتصال به پراکسی timeout شد")), 4500); })]);
    if (timer) { clearTimeout(timer); timer = null; }
    reader = socket.readable.getReader(); writer = socket.writable.getWriter();
    const useAuth = !!(proxy.username || proxy.password);
    await writer.write(new Uint8Array(useAuth ? [5, 1, 2] : [5, 1, 0]));
    const buffered = makeBufferedSocketReader(reader);
    const method = await buffered.readExact(2, 4500);
    if (method[0] !== 5 || method[1] === 255) throw new Error("روش احراز هویت SOCKS5 پذیرفته نشد.");
    if (method[1] === 2) {
      const user = enc.encode(proxy.username), pass = enc.encode(proxy.password);
      if (!user.length || user.length > 255 || pass.length > 255) throw new Error("نام کاربری/رمز عبور SOCKS5 نامعتبر است.");
      await writer.write(new Uint8Array([1, user.length, ...user, pass.length, ...pass]));
      const auth = await buffered.readExact(2, 4500);
      if (auth[0] !== 1 || auth[1] !== 0) throw new Error("احراز هویت SOCKS5 ناموفق بود.");
    } else if (method[1] !== 0 || useAuth) throw new Error("پراکسی روش احراز هویت تنظیم‌شده را نپذیرفت.");
    await writer.write(concatChunks([new Uint8Array([5, 1, 0]), encodeSocks5Address(TELEGRAM_PROXY_TEST_TARGET, 443)]));
    const head = await buffered.readExact(4, 4500);
    if (head[0] !== 5 || head[1] !== 0) throw new Error("اتصال SOCKS5 به مقصد تلگرام برقرار نشد (کد " + head[1] + ").");
    if (head[3] === 1) await buffered.readExact(4, 4500);
    else if (head[3] === 3) { const length = (await buffered.readExact(1, 4500))[0]; await buffered.readExact(length, 4500); }
    else if (head[3] === 4) await buffered.readExact(16, 4500);
    else throw new Error("پاسخ SOCKS5 دارای ATYP نامعتبر است.");
    await buffered.readExact(2, 4500);
    return { status: "ok", latency: Date.now() - started, message: "SOCKS5 به مقصد TCP/443 تلگرام متصل شد." };
  } catch (error) {
    return { status: "error", latency: -1, message: String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 160) };
  } finally {
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    try { writer?.releaseLock(); } catch {}
    try { reader?.releaseLock(); } catch {}
    try { await socket?.close?.(); } catch {}
  }
}

async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limit, mapper) {
  const input = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
  const results = new Array(input.length);
  let cursor = 0;
  const workers = Array.from({ length: Math.min(Math.max(1, limit), input.length) }, async () => {
    while (true) {
      const index = cursor++;
      if (index >= input.length) return;
      try { results[index] = await mapper(input[index], index); }
      catch (error) { results[index] = { status: "error", message: String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 160) }; }
    }
  });
  await Promise.all(workers);
  return results;
}

async function fetchAndTestTelegramProxySources(rawSources) {
  const sources = [...new Set((Array.isArray(rawSources) ? rawSources : String(rawSources || "").split(/[\r\n]+/))
    .map(value => String(value || "").trim()).filter(Boolean))];
  if (!sources.length || sources.length > TELEGRAM_PROXY_SOURCE_MAX_COUNT) throw new Error("بین ۱ تا ۵ آدرس منبع HTTPS وارد کنید.");
  const allUris = [];
  for (const source of sources) {
    let url;
    try { url = new URL(source); } catch { throw new Error("آدرس منبع نامعتبر است: " + source.slice(0, 100)); }
    if (url.protocol !== "https:" || url.username || url.password || (url.port && url.port !== "443") || !TELEGRAM_PROXY_SOURCE_HOSTS.has(url.hostname.toLowerCase())) {
      throw new Error("منبع باید HTTPS و از raw.githubusercontent.com، gist.githubusercontent.com یا Pastebin/GitHub باشد.");
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 7000);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(url.toString(), { method: "GET", redirect: "error", signal: controller.signal,
        headers: { "accept": "text/plain, application/json, text/*;q=0.9" } });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("HTTP " + response.status);
      const length = Number(response.headers.get("content-length") || 0);
      if (length > TELEGRAM_PROXY_SOURCE_MAX_BYTES) throw new Error("حجم منبع بیش از ۲۵۶ کیلوبایت است.");
      const text = await response.text();
      if (enc.encode(text).byteLength > TELEGRAM_PROXY_SOURCE_MAX_BYTES) throw new Error("حجم منبع بیش از ۲۵۶ کیلوبایت است.");
      allUris.push(...extractTelegramProxyUris(text));
    } catch (error) {
      throw new Error("دریافت منبع " + url.hostname + " ناموفق بود: " + String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 140));
    } finally { clearTimeout(timeout); }
  }
  const unique = [...new Set(allUris)].slice(0, TELEGRAM_PROXY_CANDIDATE_MAX);
  const parsed = [];
  const seen = new Set();
  for (const uri of unique) {
    try {
      const proxy = parseExitProxyUri(uri);
      if (proxy.kind !== "socks5" || !isSafePublicTelegramProxyHost(proxy.hostname)) continue;
      const key = [proxy.hostname.toLowerCase(), proxy.port, proxy.username, proxy.password].join("|");
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      parsed.push({ uri, host: proxy.hostname, port: proxy.port, name: "Telegram " + proxy.hostname.replace(/^\[|\]$/g, ""), country: "UN" });
    } catch {}
  }
  const tested = await mapWithConcurrency(parsed, 4, async item => ({ ...item, ...(await testTelegramSocks5Proxy(item.uri)) }));
  return { sources: sources.length, found: parsed.length, results: tested };
}

// -------------------- Ping & Clean IP API --------------------
const CLOUDFLARE_EDGE_CIDRS = [
  "103.21.244.0/22", "103.22.200.0/22", "103.31.4.0/22",
  "104.16.0.0/13", "104.24.0.0/14", "108.162.192.0/18",
  "131.0.72.0/22", "141.101.64.0/18", "162.158.0.0/15",
  "172.64.0.0/13", "173.245.48.0/20", "188.114.96.0/20",
  "190.93.240.0/20", "197.234.240.0/22", "198.41.128.0/17",
  "2400:cb00::/32", "2606:4700::/32", "2803:f800::/32",
  "2405:b500::/32", "2405:8100::/32", "2a06:98c0::/29", "2c0f:f248::/32"
];

function ipInCidr(ip, cidr) {
  try {
    const [network, prefixText] = cidr.split("/");
    const prefix = Number(prefixText);
    if (isIPv4Text(ip) && isIPv4Text(network)) {
      if (!Number.isInteger(prefix) || prefix < 0 || prefix > 32) return false;
      const toNumber = value => value.split(".").reduce((n, part) => n * 256 + Number(part), 0);
      const divisor = 2 ** (32 - prefix);
      return Math.floor(toNumber(ip) / divisor) === Math.floor(toNumber(network) / divisor);
    }
    if (isIPv6Text(ip) && isIPv6Text(network)) {
      if (!Number.isInteger(prefix) || prefix < 0 || prefix > 128) return false;
      const a = expandIPv6(ip), b = expandIPv6(network);
      const fullGroups = Math.floor(prefix / 16), remainingBits = prefix % 16;
      for (let i = 0; i < fullGroups; i++) if (a[i] !== b[i]) return false;
      if (remainingBits) {
        const mask = (0xffff << (16 - remainingBits)) & 0xffff;
        if ((a[fullGroups] & mask) !== (b[fullGroups] & mask)) return false;
      }
      return true;
    }
  } catch {}
  return false;
}

function isCloudflareEdgeIp(ip) {
  return CLOUDFLARE_EDGE_CIDRS.some(cidr => ipInCidr(ip, cidr));
}

async function handlePingIp(request) {
  let body;
  try { body = await readJsonLimited(request, 8192); }
  catch (err) { return json({ error: err.message, code: "ERR_VALIDATION" }, 400); }
  const ips = body && body.ips;
  if (!Array.isArray(ips) || ips.length < 1 || ips.length > MAX_CLEAN_IPS) {
    return json({ error: "فهرست آدرس‌ها نامعتبر است (حداکثر ۱۰۰ مورد).", code: "ERR_VALIDATION" }, 400);
  }
  const normalized = [];
  try {
    for (const item of ips) {
      const host = String(item || "").trim();
      if (!host || /[\s\/@?#\\]/.test(host)) throw new Error();
      const hp = parseHostPort(host, 443);
      if (hp.hasPort || (!isIPv4Text(hp.host) && !isIPv6Text(hp.host) && !isDomainName(hp.host))) throw new Error();
      normalized.push(hp.host);
    }
  } catch {
    return json({ error: "یکی از آدرس‌ها نامعتبر است.", code: "ERR_VALIDATION" }, 400);
  }
  const results = new Array(normalized.length);
  let cursor = 0;
  const workerCount = Math.min(6, normalized.length);
  await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, async () => {
    while (true) {
      const index = cursor++;
      if (index >= normalized.length) return;
      const ip = normalized[index];
      if (isCloudflareEdgeIp(ip)) {
        results[index] = { ip, status: "unsupported", latency: -1, code: "ERR_CF_IP_SOCKET_BLOCKED",
          message: "Cloudflare Workers cannot open outbound TCP sockets to Cloudflare edge IP ranges." };
        continue;
      }
      const start = Date.now(); let socket, timer;
      try {
        socket = connect({ hostname: ip, port: 443 }, { secureTransport: "off", allowHalfOpen: true });
        await Promise.race([socket.opened, new Promise((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("timeout")), 2500); })]);
        results[index] = { ip, status: "ok", latency: Date.now() - start };
      } catch (err) {
        const message = String(err && err.message || err || "TCP connection failed").slice(0, 180);
        results[index] = { ip, status: "error", latency: -1, message };
      } finally { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); try { socket?.close(); } catch {} }
    }
  }));
  return json({ results });
}

const MAX_EXTERNAL_PING_TARGETS = 1;
const CHECK_HOST_API = "https://api.check-host.cc";
const CHECK_HOST_WEB = "https://check-host.cc";
// Primary, stable provider: the official check-host.net API (the .cc API stays as automatic fallback).
const CHECK_HOST_NET_API = "https://check-host.net";
const CHECK_HOST_USER_AGENT = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0.0.0 Safari/537.36";

async function fetchJsonWithTimeout(url, timeoutMs = 4500, init = {}) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      ...init,
      headers: { "User-Agent": CHECK_HOST_USER_AGENT, "Accept": "application/json", ...(init.headers || {}) },
      signal: controller.signal
    });
    const text = await response.text();
    let data = null;
    try { data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null; } catch {}
    return { response, data, bodyText: text.slice(0, 240) };
  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}

function sleep(ms) { return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms)); }

function shortExternalError(data, bodyText, fallback) {
  const value = data?.error || data?.message || data?.detail || bodyText || fallback;
  return String(value).replace(/\s+/g, " ").slice(0, 180);
}

// Explicit Iranian probe nodes, sent in the `node` field to target Iran.
const CHECK_HOST_IRAN_NODES = [
  "ir1.node.check-host.net", "ir2.node.check-host.net", "ir3.node.check-host.net",
  "ir4.node.check-host.net", "ir5.node.check-host.net", "ir6.node.check-host.net"
];
// Explicit Iranian nodes sent to check-host.net's /check-tcp endpoint.
const CHECK_HOST_NET_IRAN_NODES = [
  "ir1.node.check-host.net", "ir2.node.check-host.net", "ir3.node.check-host.net", "ir4.node.check-host.net"
];

// True only for Check-Host probe nodes located in Iran: country code "ir",
// a node id such as "ir1.node.check-host.net", or an explicit Iran country name.
function isIranCheckHostNode(locationName, node) {
  const key = String(locationName || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  const code = String(node?.countryCode ?? node?.country_code ?? node?.cc ?? node?.region ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  const country = String(node?.country || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  return code === "ir"
    || /^ir\d*(?:[.\-_:]|$)/.test(key)
    || country === "ir" || country === "iran" || country.startsWith("iran");
}

// A node report is either an array of checks directly (e.g. [[1, 0.045, "Connected"]],
// null while still pending) or an object that carries them in `checks`.
function externalChecksArray(node) {
  if (Array.isArray(node)) {
    // A single un-nested check such as [1, 0.045, "Connected"] is wrapped.
    if (node.length && !Array.isArray(node[0]) && typeof node[0] !== "object") return [node];
    return node;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(node?.checks)) return node.checks;
  if (node?.checks && typeof node.checks === "object") return [node.checks];
  return [];
}

// Check-Host reports `time` in seconds (0.045) in array/classic checks, while the
// `connectiontime` object field is already milliseconds. Normalise both to whole ms.
function checkHostLatencyToMs(value, unit) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return null;
  const n = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0) return null;
  const isSeconds = unit === "s" ? true : (n < 10 && !Number.isInteger(n));
  const ms = isSeconds ? n * 1000 : n;
  return n > 0 ? Math.max(1, Math.round(ms)) : 0;
}

// Supports array checks [code, time, message] and object checks
// {status, connectiontime | time, errortext | error}.
function parseCheckHostCheck(check) {
  let rawStatus, rawLatency = null, unit = "auto", message = "";
  if (Array.isArray(check)) {
    rawStatus = check[0]; rawLatency = check[1]; unit = "s"; message = check[2];
  } else if (check && typeof check === "object") {
    rawStatus = check.status ?? check.code;
    const connectionTime = check.connectiontime ?? check.connection_time ?? check.latency;
    if (connectionTime !== undefined && connectionTime !== null) rawLatency = connectionTime;
    else if (check.time !== undefined && check.time !== null) { rawLatency = check.time; unit = "s"; }
    message = check.errortext ?? check.error ?? check.message ?? "";
    if (rawStatus === undefined || rawStatus === null || String(rawStatus).trim() === "") {
      if (check.error || check.errortext) rawStatus = 0;
      else if (rawLatency !== null && Number.isFinite(Number(rawLatency))) rawStatus = 1;
      else if (check.address) rawStatus = 1;
    }
  } else {
    rawStatus = check;
  }
  const statusText = String(rawStatus ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  const code = statusText === "" ? NaN : Number(statusText);
  if (code === 1 || ["ok", "success", "connected", "open", "true"].includes(statusText)) {
    return { state: "ok", latency: checkHostLatencyToMs(rawLatency, unit), error: "" };
  }
  if (code === 0 || ["error", "failed", "closed", "timeout", "false"].includes(statusText)) {
    return { state: "error", latency: null, error: String(message || "") };
  }
  return { state: "pending", latency: null, error: "" };
}

// Official check-host.net flow: GET /check-tcp -> request_id, then GET /check-result/<id>.
// Result per node: [{ time: 0.045, address: "..." }] | [{ error: "..." }] | null (pending).
// Returns { startFailed: true, error } when the test could not be started (caller falls back to the legacy API).
async function checkHostNetTcp(host, port) {
  const target = (isIPv6Text(host) ? "[" + host + "]" : host) + ":" + Number(port);
  const hostParam = encodeURIComponent(target);
  const isValidStart = r => !!(r?.response?.ok && r?.data && typeof r.data === "object" && r.data.request_id
    && r.data.nodes && typeof r.data.nodes === "object" && Object.keys(r.data.nodes).length > 0);
  const startUrls = [
    `${CHECK_HOST_NET_API}/check-tcp?host=${hostParam}` + CHECK_HOST_NET_IRAN_NODES.map(n => "&node=" + encodeURIComponent(n)).join(""),
    `${CHECK_HOST_NET_API}/check-tcp?host=${hostParam}&max_nodes=10`
  ];
  let started = null, lastError = "";
  for (const url of startUrls) {
    try {
      const attempt = await fetchJsonWithTimeout(url, 7000);
      if (isValidStart(attempt)) { started = attempt; break; }
      const httpStatus = Number(attempt?.response?.status) || 0;
      lastError = `HTTP ${httpStatus || "بدون پاسخ"}: ${shortExternalError(attempt?.data, attempt?.bodyText, "پاسخ معتبر برای شروع آزمون دریافت نشد.")}`;
      if (httpStatus === 429) break;
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = (error && error.name === "AbortError") ? "مهلت ارتباط تمام شد." : String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 120);
    }
  }
  if (!started) return { startFailed: true, error: lastError };

  const requestId = String(started.data.request_id);
  const nodeInfo = started.data.nodes;
  const reportUrl = typeof started.data.permanent_link === "string" && started.data.permanent_link.startsWith(CHECK_HOST_NET_API + "/")
    ? started.data.permanent_link
    : (/^[A-Za-z0-9]{4,40}$/.test(requestId) ? `${CHECK_HOST_NET_API}/check-report/${requestId}` : null);
  const infoFor = name => {
    const v = nodeInfo[name];
    if (Array.isArray(v)) return { countryCode: v[0], country: v[1], city: v[2], isp: v[4] };
    return v && typeof v === "object" ? v : {};
  };
  const allNames = Object.keys(nodeInfo);
  const iranNames = allNames.filter(name => isIranCheckHostNode(name, infoFor(name)));

  let resultMap = null, lastPollError = "";
  for (const delay of [2000, 2000, 2500, 3000]) {
    await sleep(delay);
    let poll;
    try {
      poll = await fetchJsonWithTimeout(`${CHECK_HOST_NET_API}/check-result/${encodeURIComponent(requestId)}`, 3500);
    } catch (error) {
      lastPollError = String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 100);
      continue;
    }
    if (!poll.response.ok || !poll.data || typeof poll.data !== "object" || Array.isArray(poll.data)) {
      lastPollError = `HTTP ${poll.response.status}`;
      continue;
    }
    resultMap = poll.data;
    // Stop once every Iranian node (or, when no Iranian node was assigned, every node) has reported.
    const watch = iranNames.length ? iranNames : allNames;
    if (watch.every(name => externalChecksArray(resultMap[name]).length > 0)) break;
  }

  if (!resultMap) {
    return { host, port, status: "pending", latency: null, successes: 0, nodes: 0, iranNodes: iranNames.length,
      error: lastPollError ? `آزمون ساخته شد اما گزارش هنوز در دسترس نیست (${lastPollError}).` : "آزمون ساخته شد؛ گزارش گره‌ها هنوز آماده نیست.",
      reportUrl, providerMethod: "GET" };
  }

  // Iranian nodes first; with no active Iranian node, report the responding nodes (by name) so the test is never left without a result.
  let usedNames = iranNames;
  let nonIranFallback = false;
  if (!iranNames.length) {
    nonIranFallback = true;
    usedNames = allNames.filter(name => externalChecksArray(resultMap[name]).length > 0);
    if (!usedNames.length) usedNames = allNames;
  }

  const times = [];
  let successfulNodes = 0, failed = 0, pending = 0;
  const nodeResults = usedNames.map(name => {
    const info = infoFor(name);
    const city = [info.city || info.country || "گره نامشخص", name].filter(Boolean).join(" — ");
    const checks = externalChecksArray(resultMap[name]);
    if (!checks.length) { pending++; return { node: name, city, status: "pending", latency: null }; }
    const parsed = parseCheckHostCheck(checks[0]);
    if (parsed.state === "ok") {
      successfulNodes++;
      if (parsed.latency !== null) times.push(parsed.latency);
      return { node: name, city, status: "ok", latency: parsed.latency };
    }
    if (parsed.state === "pending") { pending++; return { node: name, city, status: "pending", latency: null }; }
    failed++;
    return { node: name, city, status: "error", latency: null, error: String(parsed.error || "اتصال TCP برقرار نشد").slice(0, 160) };
  });
  const avg = times.length ? Math.round(times.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / times.length) : null;
  const status = successfulNodes > 0 ? "ok" : pending > 0 ? "pending" : "error";
  const fallbackNote = nonIranFallback
    ? `هیچ گرهٔ فعال ایرانی در این آزمون نبود؛ نتیجه از سایر گره‌ها (${usedNames.slice(0, 6).join("، ")}) گزارش شده است و نمایندهٔ دید داخل ایران نیست.`
    : "";
  let error = null;
  if (status === "error") {
    const reasons = nodeResults.filter(node => node.status === "error" && node.error).slice(0, 3).map(node => node.city + ": " + node.error);
    error = (nonIranFallback ? fallbackNote + " " : "") + (nonIranFallback ? "هیچ‌یک از گره‌های پاسخ‌دهنده" : "هیچ‌یک از گره‌های ایرانی پاسخ‌دهنده")
      + " اتصال TCP برقرار نکردند." + (reasons.length ? " جزئیات: " + reasons.join("؛ ") : "");
  } else if (status === "pending") {
    error = (nonIranFallback ? fallbackNote + " " : "") + "برخی گره‌ها هنوز پاسخ نداده‌اند؛ نامشخص به‌معنای بسته‌بودن پورت نیست.";
  } else if (nonIranFallback) {
    error = fallbackNote;
  }
  return {
    host, port, status, latency: avg, successes: successfulNodes, nodes: usedNames.length, iranNodes: nonIranFallback ? 0 : usedNames.length,
    minLatency: times.length ? Math.min(...times) : null,
    maxLatency: times.length ? Math.max(...times) : null,
    error, reportUrl, failedNodes: failed, pendingNodes: pending, nodeResults, providerMethod: "GET"
  };
}

// Primary: check-host.net. Fallback (only if check-host.net cannot start the test): legacy api.check-host.cc.
async function checkHostExternalTcp(host, port, dispatchDelayMs = 0, env = {}) {
  if (dispatchDelayMs > 0) await sleep(dispatchDelayMs);
  let netResult;
  try {
    netResult = await checkHostNetTcp(host, port);
  } catch (err) {
    netResult = { startFailed: true, error: (err && err.name === "AbortError") ? "مهلت ارتباط تمام شد." : String(err?.message || err).slice(0, 120) };
  }
  if (netResult && !netResult.startFailed) return netResult;
  const legacy = await checkHostLegacyTcp(host, port, env);
  if (legacy && legacy.status === "error" && netResult?.error) {
    legacy.error = `check-host.net: ${netResult.error} | ${legacy.error || ""}`.slice(0, 400);
  }
  return legacy;
}
// Legacy provider (api.check-host.cc). Used only as an automatic fallback when check-host.net cannot start a test.
async function checkHostLegacyTcp(host, port, env = {}) {
  const apiKey = String(env?.CHECK_HOST_API_KEY || "").trim();
  const headers = { "content-type": "application/json" };
  if (apiKey) headers.Authorization = "Bearer " + apiKey;
  const isValidStart = result => !!(result?.response?.ok && result?.data?.success === true && result?.data?.uuid);
  const retryDelay = response => Math.min(15000, Math.max(1000, Number(response?.headers?.get("retry-after") || 5) * 1000));
  try {
    // /usage is explicitly outside Check-Host's rate limiter. Check that one
    // start plus possible fallback and report polling fit the current budget.
    try {
      const budget = await fetchJsonWithTimeout(`${CHECK_HOST_API}/usage`, 2500, {
        headers: apiKey ? { Authorization: "Bearer " + apiKey } : {}
      });
      const remaining = budget?.data?.remaining;
      if (budget.response.ok && budget.data?.success === true && remaining && typeof remaining === "object") {
        const shortWindow = Number(remaining["10s"]);
        const minuteWindow = Number(remaining.min);
        const hourWindow = Number(remaining.hour);
        if ((Number.isFinite(shortWindow) && shortWindow < 6) ||
            (Number.isFinite(minuteWindow) && minuteWindow < 6) ||
            (Number.isFinite(hourWindow) && hourWindow < 6)) {
          const waitMs = Number.isFinite(shortWindow) && shortWindow < 6 ? 11000
            : Number.isFinite(minuteWindow) && minuteWindow < 6 ? 60000 : 3600000;
          return { host, port, status: "rate_limited", latency: null, successes: 0, nodes: 0,
            retryAfterMs: waitMs, error: "سهمیهٔ درخواست Check-Host برای این بازه کافی نیست؛ کمی بعد دوباره تست کنید.",
            reportUrl: null, providerMethod: "preflight" };
        }
      }
    } catch { /* Continue when the read-only usage endpoint is temporarily unavailable. */ }

    // Primary path: official POST API restricted to the Iranian probe nodes
    // (ir1..ir6 via `node`); if the API rejects that body (HTTP 400/422) a plain
    // body is tried once. The report is additionally filtered below, because the
    // result must reflect reachability from inside Iran only.
    let started = null;
    let method = "POST";
    let postTransportError = "";
    const baseBody = { target: host, port: Number(port), timeout: 2500 };
    const postBodies = [
      { ...baseBody, node: CHECK_HOST_IRAN_NODES },
      { ...baseBody }
    ];
    for (const payload of postBodies) {
      try {
        started = await fetchJsonWithTimeout(`${CHECK_HOST_API}/tcp`, 7000, {
          method: "POST", headers, body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        postTransportError = "";
      } catch (error) {
        postTransportError = String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 120);
        started = null;
        break;
      }
      const httpStatus = started?.response?.status;
      if (isValidStart(started) || (httpStatus !== 400 && httpStatus !== 422)) break;
    }

    // Check-Host also publishes an official GET shortcut. Use it if POST was
    // rejected or failed at transport level (except 429, which must not be retried).
    if (!isValidStart(started) && started?.response?.status !== 429) {
      try {
        const getUrl = `${CHECK_HOST_API}/tcp/${encodeURIComponent(host)}/${Number(port)}?timeout=2500`
          + CHECK_HOST_IRAN_NODES.map(n => `&node=${encodeURIComponent(n)}`).join("");
        const fallback = await fetchJsonWithTimeout(getUrl, 7000, {
          headers: apiKey ? { Authorization: "Bearer " + apiKey } : {}
        });
        if (isValidStart(fallback)) { started = fallback; method = "GET"; }
        else if (!started || fallback.response.status === 429) started = fallback;
      } catch (error) {
        if (!started) started = { response: { status: 0, headers: { get: () => "" } }, data: null, bodyText: String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 120) };
      }
    }

    if (!isValidStart(started)) {
      const httpStatus = Number(started?.response?.status) || 0;
      const status = httpStatus === 429 ? "rate_limited" : "error";
      const reason = shortExternalError(started?.data, started?.bodyText,
        httpStatus === 403 ? "دسترسی API رد شد." : postTransportError || "پاسخ معتبر از مسیر POST یا GET برای شروع آزمون دریافت نشد.");
      return {
        host, port, status, latency: null, successes: 0, nodes: 0,
        retryAfterMs: retryDelay(started?.response),
        error: `Check-Host نتوانست آزمون را شروع کند (HTTP ${httpStatus || "بدون پاسخ"}): ${reason}`,
        reportUrl: null, providerMethod: method
      };
    }

    const requestId = String(started.data.uuid);
    const apiReportUrl = typeof started.data.apiURL === "string" && started.data.apiURL.startsWith(CHECK_HOST_API + "/report/")
      ? started.data.apiURL
      : `${CHECK_HOST_API}/report/${encodeURIComponent(requestId)}`;
    const reportUrl = typeof started.data.reportURL === "string" && started.data.reportURL.startsWith(CHECK_HOST_WEB + "/report/")
      ? started.data.reportURL
      : (/^[a-f0-9-]{30,40}$/i.test(requestId) ? `${CHECK_HOST_WEB}/report/${requestId}` : null);

    let reportData = null;
    let lastPollError = "";
    // Probes can take up to ~10 seconds. Poll beyond that, but keep the wait bounded.
    const pollDelays = [1500, 2000, 2500, 3000];
    for (const delay of pollDelays) {
      await sleep(delay);
      let poll;
      try {
        poll = await fetchJsonWithTimeout(apiReportUrl, 3200, {
          headers: apiKey ? { Authorization: "Bearer " + apiKey } : {}
        });
      } catch (error) {
        lastPollError = String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 100);
        continue;
      }
      if (poll.response.status === 429) {
        return { host, port, status: "rate_limited", latency: null, successes: 0, nodes: 0,
          retryAfterMs: retryDelay(poll.response), error: "محدودیت درخواست Check-Host؛ پس از مکث دوباره آزمون کنید.", reportUrl, providerMethod: method };
      }
      if (poll.response.status === 403) {
        return { host, port, status: "error", latency: null, successes: 0, nodes: 0,
          error: `خواندن گزارش Check-Host رد شد (HTTP 403): ${shortExternalError(poll.data, poll.bodyText, "دسترسی رد شد")}`, reportUrl, providerMethod: method };
      }
      if (!poll.response.ok || !poll.data || poll.data.success === false) {
        lastPollError = `HTTP ${poll.response.status}`;
        continue;
      }
      reportData = poll.data;
      const map = reportData.data && typeof reportData.data === "object" ? reportData.data : {};
      // Only Iranian nodes matter: stop polling once every Iranian node has reported.
      const iranNodes = Object.entries(map).filter(([name, node]) => isIranCheckHostNode(name, node)).map(([, node]) => node);
      const allIranNodesReported = iranNodes.length > 0 && iranNodes.every(node => externalChecksArray(node).length > 0);
      if (allIranNodesReported) break;
    }

    const map = reportData?.data && typeof reportData.data === "object" ? reportData.data : {};
    const allEntries = Object.entries(map);
    if (!allEntries.length) {
      return { host, port, status: "pending", latency: null, successes: 0, nodes: 0, iranNodes: 0,
        error: lastPollError ? `آزمون ساخته شد اما گزارش هنوز در دسترس نیست (${lastPollError}).` : "آزمون ساخته شد؛ گزارش گره‌ها هنوز آماده نیست.",
        reportUrl, providerMethod: method };
    }
    // Discard every non-Iranian probe (EU/US/...): they say nothing about
    // reachability for a client located inside Iran.
    const entries = allEntries.filter(([name, node]) => isIranCheckHostNode(name, node));
    if (!entries.length) {
      return { host, port, status: "pending", latency: null, successes: 0, nodes: 0, iranNodes: 0,
        error: "عدم حضور گره فعال در ایران؛ هیچ گرهٔ ایرانی در این آزمون گزارش نداد (گره‌های خارجی عمداً نادیده گرفته شدند). نتیجه‌ای از داخل ایران در دسترس نیست و نباید به‌عنوان موفق یا ناموفق تلقی شود.",
        reportUrl, providerMethod: method, nodeResults: [], failedNodes: 0, pendingNodes: 0 };
    }

    const times = [];
    let successfulNodes = 0, failed = 0, pending = 0;
    const nodeResults = entries.map(([locationName, node]) => {
      const city = [node?.city || node?.country || "گره نامشخص", node?.isp || node?.datacenter || locationName].filter(Boolean).join(" — ");
      const checks = externalChecksArray(node);
      if (!checks.length) { pending++; return { node: locationName, city, status: "pending", latency: null }; }
      const parsed = parseCheckHostCheck(checks[0]);
      if (parsed.state === "ok") {
        successfulNodes++;
        if (parsed.latency !== null) times.push(parsed.latency);
        return { node: locationName, city, status: "ok", latency: parsed.latency };
      }
      if (parsed.state === "pending") { pending++; return { node: locationName, city, status: "pending", latency: null }; }
      failed++;
      return { node: locationName, city, status: "error", latency: null, error: String(parsed.error || "اتصال TCP برقرار نشد").slice(0, 160) };
    });
    const avg = times.length ? Math.round(times.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / times.length) : null;
    // Status is derived strictly from Iranian nodes.
    const status = successfulNodes > 0 ? "ok" : pending > 0 ? "pending" : "error";
    return {
      host, port, status, latency: avg, successes: successfulNodes, nodes: entries.length, iranNodes: entries.length,
      minLatency: times.length ? Math.min(...times) : null,
      maxLatency: times.length ? Math.max(...times) : null,
      error: status === "error" ? (() => {
        const reasons = nodeResults.filter(node => node.status === "error" && node.error).slice(0, 3).map(node => node.city + ": " + node.error);
        return "هیچ‌یک از گره‌های ایرانی پاسخ‌دهنده اتصال TCP برقرار نکردند." + (reasons.length ? " جزئیات: " + reasons.join("؛ ") : "");
      })() : status === "pending" ? "برخی گره‌های ایرانی هنوز پاسخ نداده‌اند؛ نامشخص به‌معنای بسته‌بودن پورت نیست." : null,
      reportUrl, failedNodes: failed, pendingNodes: pending, nodeResults, providerMethod: method
    };
  } catch (err) {
    return { host, port, status: "error", latency: null, successes: 0, nodes: 0,
      error: (err && err.name === "AbortError") ? "مهلت ارتباط با Check-Host تمام شد." : `ارتباط با Check-Host ممکن نشد: ${String(err?.message || err).slice(0, 120)}`, reportUrl: null };
  }
}

function normalizeTcpTestHistoryEntry(item) {
  if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return null;
  const host = String(item.host || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  const port = Number(item.port);
  const status = ["ok", "pending", "error", "rate_limited"].includes(item.status) ? item.status : "error";
  if (!host || !Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) return null;
  const checkedAt = Number(item.checkedAt);
  return {
    host, port, status,
    latency: item.latency == null || !Number.isFinite(Number(item.latency)) ? null : Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(item.latency))),
    successes: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(item.successes) || 0)),
    nodes: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(item.nodes) || 0)),
    failedNodes: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(item.failedNodes) || 0)),
    pendingNodes: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(item.pendingNodes) || 0)),
    error: String(item.error || "").slice(0, 180),
    reportUrl: typeof item.reportUrl === "string" && (item.reportUrl.startsWith("https://check-host.net/") || item.reportUrl.startsWith("https://check-host.cc/")) ? item.reportUrl : null,
    providerMethod: ["POST", "GET", "preflight"].includes(item.providerMethod) ? item.providerMethod : "unknown",
    nodeResults: Array.isArray(item.nodeResults) ? item.nodeResults.slice(0, 80).map(node => ({
      node: String(node.node || "").slice(0, 100), city: String(node.city || "").slice(0, 120),
      status: ["ok", "error", "pending"].includes(node.status) ? node.status : "pending",
      latency: node.latency == null ? null : Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(node.latency) || 0)),
      error: String(node.error || "").slice(0, 160)
    })) : [],
    checkedAt: Number.isFinite(checkedAt) && checkedAt > 0 ? checkedAt : Date.now()
  };
}

async function loadTcpTestHistory(kv) {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (tcpHistoryCache && (now - tcpHistoryCacheAt) < TCP_HISTORY_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
    return tcpHistoryCache.slice();
  }
  if (!kv) return [];
  try {
    const raw = readKvJson(await kv.get(TCP_TEST_HISTORY_KEY, { type: "json" }));
    const result = (Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : []).map(normalizeTcpTestHistoryEntry).filter(Boolean)
      .sort((a, b) => b.checkedAt - a.checkedAt).slice(0, TCP_TEST_HISTORY_LIMIT);
    tcpHistoryCache = result;
    tcpHistoryCacheAt = now;
    return result.slice();
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("TCP test history read failed:", String(err?.message || err).slice(0, 120));
    return [];
  }
}

async function appendTcpTestHistory(kv, results) {
  const stamped = (Array.isArray(results) ? results : []).map(item => normalizeTcpTestHistoryEntry({ ...item, checkedAt: Date.now() })).filter(Boolean);
  if (!kv || !stamped.length) return stamped;
  try {
    const current = await loadTcpTestHistory(kv);
    const combined = [...stamped, ...current].sort((a, b) => b.checkedAt - a.checkedAt).slice(0, TCP_TEST_HISTORY_LIMIT);
    await kv.put(TCP_TEST_HISTORY_KEY, JSON.stringify(combined));
    tcpHistoryCache = null;
    tcpHistoryCacheAt = 0;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("TCP test history write failed:", String(err?.message || err).slice(0, 120));
  }
  return stamped;
}

async function handleExternalPing(request, kv, env, requestUrl) {
  let body;
  try { body = await readJsonLimited(request, 12000); }
  catch (err) { return json({ error: err.message, code: "ERR_VALIDATION" }, 400); }
  if (!body || !Array.isArray(body.targets) || body.targets.length < 1 || body.targets.length > MAX_EXTERNAL_PING_TARGETS) {
    return json({ error: `فهرست آزمون باید بین ۱ تا ${MAX_EXTERNAL_PING_TARGETS} هدف داشته باشد.`, code: "ERR_VALIDATION" }, 400);
  }
  const targets = [], seen = new Set();
  try {
    for (const target of body.targets) {
      const rawHost = String(target && target.host || "").trim();
      const hp = parseHostPort(rawHost, 443);
      if (hp.hasPort) throw new Error("پورت باید در فیلد جداگانه وارد شود");
      const host = hp.host.toLowerCase();
      const port = Number(target?.port);
      if (!isIPv4Text(host) && !isIPv6Text(host) && !isDomainName(host)) throw new Error("آدرس IP یا دامنهٔ نامعتبر است");
      if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) throw new Error("پورت باید عددی بین ۱ تا ۶۵۵۳۵ باشد");
      const key = `${host}|${port}`;
      if (!seen.has(key)) { seen.add(key); targets.push({ host, port }); }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    return json({ error: err.message || "هدف آزمون نامعتبر است", code: "ERR_VALIDATION" }, 400);
  }
  // Up to three starts are staggered to stay below the provider's anonymous
  // burst threshold, while report polling remains parallel to avoid long Worker requests.
  const results = await Promise.all(targets.map((target, index) =>
    checkHostExternalTcp(target.host, target.port, index * 1100, env)
  ));
  const stampedResults = await appendTcpTestHistory(kv, results);
  const byKey = new Map(stampedResults.map(item => [`${item.host}|${item.port}`, item]));
  const resultsWithTime = results.map(item => ({ ...item, checkedAt: byKey.get(`${item.host}|${item.port}`)?.checkedAt || Date.now() }));
  return json({ provider: "Check-Host.net", note: "نتایج در اولویت از گره‌های ایرانی Check-Host محاسبه می‌شوند؛ در صورت نبودن گرهٔ فعال ایرانی، نتیجهٔ سایر گره‌های پاسخ‌دهنده به‌همراه نام گره برگردانده می‌شود. این گره‌ها نمایندهٔ همهٔ اپراتورها یا مسیر دقیق کاربر نیستند.", results: resultsWithTime });
}

// -------------------- Session & Setup Helpers --------------------
async function isSetupDone(kv, env) {
  try {
    const settings = await loadSettings(kv, env);
    return !!settings.passwordHash || !!env.PANEL_PASSWORD;
  } catch { return false; }
}

async function handleSetup(request, kv, env, url) {
  if (!sameOrigin(request, url)) return json({ error: "Bad Origin", code: "ERR_BAD_ORIGIN" }, 403);
  if (!kv) return json({ error: "KV Binding یافت نشد. در تنظیمات Worker یک KV Namespace با نام SETTINGS تعریف کنید.", code: "ERR_KV_MISSING" }, 500);
  if (await isSetupDone(kv, env)) return json({ error: "Setup already completed", code: "ERR_SETUP_DONE" }, 400);
  const body = await readJsonLimited(request, 4096);
  const username = String(body.username || "").trim();
  const password = String(body.password || "").trim();
  if (!username || username.length < 3) return json({ error: "نام کاربری نامعتبر است", code: "ERR_USERNAME_INVALID" }, 400);
  if (!password || password.length < 6) return json({ error: "رمز عبور ضعیف است", code: "ERR_PASSWORD_INVALID" }, 400);

  if (PANEL_DB) {
    await ensurePanelD1Schema();
    const savedCore = await readD1Meta("core");
    if (savedCore?.passwordHash) return json({ error: "Setup completed by another request", code: "ERR_SETUP_DONE" }, 400);
  } else {
    const checkAgain = parseStoredJson(await kv.get(SETTINGS_KEY, { type: "json" }), null);
    if (checkAgain?.passwordHash) return json({ error: "Setup completed by another request", code: "ERR_SETUP_DONE" }, 400);
  }

  const passwordHash = await hashPassword(password);
  const sessionSecret = await generateSecureToken();
  const subToken = await generateSecureToken();
  const uuid = crypto.randomUUID();
  const trojanPassword = generateSecureRandomString(24);
  const settings = { ...DEFAULTS, username, passwordHash, sessionSecret, subToken, uuid, trojanPassword, vlessEnabled: true, trojanEnabled: true };

  await saveSettings(kv, env, settings);
  const timestamp = String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
  const signature = await signSession(timestamp, settings);
  return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), {
    headers: securityHeaders({
      "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
      "cache-control": "no-store",
      "set-cookie": "wp_session=" + timestamp + "." + signature + "; Path=/; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict; Max-Age=86400"
    })
  });
}

const LOGIN_FAILURE_LIMIT = 5;
const LOGIN_FAILURE_WINDOW_SECONDS = 300;
const LOGIN_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS = LOGIN_FAILURE_WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000;
const LOGIN_FAILURE_STORE_KEY = "panel-login-failures-v2";
const LOGIN_FAILURE_STORE_TTL_SECONDS = 600;
const LOGIN_FAILURE_MAX_TRACKED_IPS = 2048;
const loginFailureMemory = new Map();
const loginInflightMemory = new Map();

function recentLocalLoginFailures(memoryKey, now = Date.now()) {
  if (!memoryKey) return [];
  const recent = (loginFailureMemory.get(memoryKey) || []).filter(item => now - item.at < LOGIN_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS);
  if (recent.length) loginFailureMemory.set(memoryKey, recent);
  else loginFailureMemory.delete(memoryKey);
  if (loginFailureMemory.size > 2048) {
    for (const [key, items] of loginFailureMemory) {
      if (!items.some(item => now - item.at < LOGIN_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS)) loginFailureMemory.delete(key);
      if (loginFailureMemory.size <= 1536) break;
    }
    while (loginFailureMemory.size > 2048) {
      const oldestKey = loginFailureMemory.keys().next().value;
      if (oldestKey === undefined) break;
      loginFailureMemory.delete(oldestKey);
    }
  }
  return recent;
}

function releaseLoginAttempt(memoryKey) {
  if (!memoryKey) return;
  const count = loginInflightMemory.get(memoryKey) || 0;
  if (count <= 1) loginInflightMemory.delete(memoryKey);
  else loginInflightMemory.set(memoryKey, count - 1);
}

function loginRetryAfter(timestamps, now = Date.now()) {
  if (!timestamps.length) return LOGIN_FAILURE_WINDOW_SECONDS;
  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil((Math.min(...timestamps) + LOGIN_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS - now) / 1000));
}

async function loginFailureIpHash(request, kv) {
  if (!kv || typeof kv.get !== "function" || typeof kv.put !== "function") return null;
  const ip = normalizeClientIp(request.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP"));
  if (!ip) return null;
  const digest = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", enc.encode(ip)));
  return base64url(digest);
}

async function readLoginFailureStore(kv) {
  if (!kv || typeof kv.get !== "function") return null;
  try {
    let value = await kv.get(LOGIN_FAILURE_STORE_KEY, { type: "json", cacheTtl: 30 });
    if (typeof value === "string") { try { value = JSON.parse(value); } catch { value = null; } }
    if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || !value.ips || typeof value.ips !== "object" || Array.isArray(value.ips)) return { version: 2, ips: {} };
    return { version: 2, ips: value.ips };
  } catch { return null; }
}

function cleanFailureTimestamps(value, now = Date.now()) {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  return value.map(Number).filter(ts => Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= now + 5000 && now - ts < LOGIN_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS && ts >= 0)
    .sort((a, b) => a - b).slice(-LOGIN_FAILURE_LIMIT);
}

function pruneLoginFailureStore(store, now = Date.now()) {
  const ips = store && store.ips && typeof store.ips === "object" && !Array.isArray(store.ips) ? store.ips : {};
  for (const [hash, value] of Object.entries(ips)) {
    const recent = cleanFailureTimestamps(value, now);
    if (recent.length) ips[hash] = recent;
    else delete ips[hash];
  }
  const keys = Object.keys(ips);
  if (keys.length > LOGIN_FAILURE_MAX_TRACKED_IPS) {
    keys.sort((a, b) => Math.max(...(ips[a] || [0])) - Math.max(...(ips[b] || [0])));
    for (const key of keys.slice(0, keys.length - LOGIN_FAILURE_MAX_TRACKED_IPS)) delete ips[key];
  }
  store.version = 2; store.ips = ips; return store;
}

async function checkLoginRateLimit(request, kv) {
  const ip = normalizeClientIp(request.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP"));
  const memoryKey = ip || null;
  const now = Date.now();
  let reserved = false;
  if (memoryKey) {
    const local = recentLocalLoginFailures(memoryKey, now);
    const inflight = loginInflightMemory.get(memoryKey) || 0;
    if (local.length >= LOGIN_FAILURE_LIMIT || local.length + inflight >= LOGIN_FAILURE_LIMIT) {
      const timestamps = local.map(item => item.at);
      return { ipHash: null, store: null, memoryKey, blocked: true, reserved: false,
        retryAfter: timestamps.length ? loginRetryAfter(timestamps, now) : LOGIN_FAILURE_WINDOW_SECONDS };
    }
    loginInflightMemory.set(memoryKey, inflight + 1);
    reserved = true;
  }
  try {
    const ipHash = await loginFailureIpHash(request, kv);
    if (!ipHash) return { ipHash: null, store: null, memoryKey, blocked: false, reserved, retryAfter: 0 };
    const store = await readLoginFailureStore(kv);
    if (!store) return { ipHash: null, store: null, memoryKey, blocked: false, reserved, retryAfter: 0 };
    const shared = cleanFailureTimestamps(store.ips[ipHash], now);
    const local = recentLocalLoginFailures(memoryKey, now).map(item => item.at);
    const timestamps = shared.length >= local.length ? shared : local;
    if (timestamps.length >= LOGIN_FAILURE_LIMIT) {
      if (reserved) releaseLoginAttempt(memoryKey);
      return { ipHash, store, memoryKey, blocked: true, reserved: false, retryAfter: loginRetryAfter(timestamps, now) };
    }
    return { ipHash, store, memoryKey, blocked: false, reserved, retryAfter: 0 };
  } catch { return { ipHash: null, store: null, memoryKey, blocked: false, reserved, retryAfter: 0 }; }
}

async function recordLoginFailure(kv, ipHash, memoryKey, storeSnapshot) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const id = generateSecureRandomString(8);
  if (memoryKey) {
    const recent = recentLocalLoginFailures(memoryKey, now);
    recent.push({ id, at: now });
    loginFailureMemory.set(memoryKey, recent.slice(-LOGIN_FAILURE_LIMIT));
  }
  if (!ipHash || !kv || typeof kv.get !== "function" || typeof kv.put !== "function" || !storeSnapshot) return;
  try {
    const store = pruneLoginFailureStore(storeSnapshot, now);
    const recent = cleanFailureTimestamps(store.ips[ipHash], now);
    recent.push(now);
    store.ips[ipHash] = recent.slice(-LOGIN_FAILURE_LIMIT);
    await kv.put(LOGIN_FAILURE_STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(store), { expirationTtl: LOGIN_FAILURE_STORE_TTL_SECONDS });
  } catch {}
}

async function clearLoginFailures(kv, ipHash, memoryKey, storeSnapshot) {
  if (memoryKey) loginFailureMemory.delete(memoryKey);
