    const line = rawLine.trim();
    if (!line || line.startsWith("#") || line.startsWith("//")) continue;
    let token = line.split(/[\s,;]+/)[0] || "";
    token = token.split("#")[0].trim();
    const v4Port = token.match(/^((?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}):\d{1,5}$/);
    if (v4Port) token = v4Port[1];
    if (!isIPv4Text(token) && !isIPv6Text(token)) continue;
    const key = token.toLowerCase();
    if (!seen.has(key)) { seen.add(key); out.push(token); }
    if (out.length >= MAX_CLEAN_IPS) break;
  }
  return out;
}

async function fetchCleanIpLists(sourceText) {
  const sources = normalizeCleanIpSources(sourceText).split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean);
  if (!sources.length) throw new Error("ابتدا حداقل یک URL فایل متنی خام GitHub را در بخش منابع وارد و ذخیره کنید.");
  const addresses = [], seen = new Set(), sourceErrors = [];
  for (const source of sources) {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 9000);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(source, { signal: controller.signal, headers: { Accept: "text/plain, */*" }, cf: { cacheTtl: 0 } });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("HTTP " + response.status);
      const text = await response.text();
      if (text.length > 512 * 1024) throw new Error("حجم فایل منبع بیش از ۵۱۲ کیلوبایت است.");
      const candidates = extractCleanIpCandidates(text);
      if (!candidates.length) throw new Error("هیچ IP معتبر در فایل پیدا نشد.");
      for (const ip of candidates) {
        const key = ip.toLowerCase();
        if (!seen.has(key)) { seen.add(key); addresses.push(ip); }
        if (addresses.length >= MAX_CLEAN_IPS) break;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      sourceErrors.push(source + " — " + String(err && err.message || err).slice(0, 120));
    } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    if (addresses.length >= MAX_CLEAN_IPS) break;
  }
  if (!addresses.length) throw new Error("هیچ IP معتبری از منابع دریافت نشد. " + sourceErrors.join(" | ").slice(0, 400));
  return { cleanIPs: addresses.join("\n"), count: addresses.length, sources };
}

function sortCleanIpListByTestResults(value, results) {
  const lines = String(value || "").split(/[\r\n,]+/).map(line => line.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const byHost = new Map((results || []).map(result => [String(result.host || "").toLowerCase(), result]));
  return lines.map((line, index) => ({ line, index, result: byHost.get(line.split("#")[0].trim().toLowerCase()) }))
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const score = r => r?.status === "ok" && Number.isFinite(Number(r.latency)) ? 0 : 1;
      const as = score(a.result), bs = score(b.result);
      if (as !== bs) return as - bs;
      if (as === 0 && Number(a.result.latency) !== Number(b.result.latency)) return Number(a.result.latency) - Number(b.result.latency);
      return a.index - b.index;
    })
    .map(item => item.line).join("\n");
}

function validateChainUri(value) {
  let u;
  try { u = new URL(String(value)); } catch { throw new Error("Chain Proxy URI نامعتبر است"); }
  if (!["vless:", "trojan:"].includes(u.protocol) || !u.hostname || !u.username) throw new Error("Chain Proxy باید URI معتبر VLESS/Trojan باشد");
  if (u.searchParams.get("type") && u.searchParams.get("type") !== "ws") throw new Error("Chain Proxy فقط WebSocket را پشتیبانی می‌کند");
  const security = (u.searchParams.get("security") || "tls").toLowerCase();
  if (!["tls", "none"].includes(security)) throw new Error("امنیت Chain Proxy نامعتبر است");
  const port = Number(u.port || (security === "tls" ? 443 : 80));
  if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) throw new Error("پورت Chain Proxy نامعتبر است");
  let credential;
  try { credential = decodeURIComponent(u.username); } catch { throw new Error("اعتبارنامه Chain Proxy نامعتبر است"); }
  if (u.protocol === "vless:" && !UUID_RE.test(credential)) throw new Error("UUID مربوط به Chain Proxy نامعتبر است");
  if (u.protocol === "trojan:" && !credential) throw new Error("رمز Trojan مربوط به Chain Proxy خالی است");
  const path = u.searchParams.get("path") || "/";
  if (!path.startsWith("/") || /[\r\n]/.test(path)) throw new Error("مسیر WebSocket در Chain Proxy نامعتبر است");
  return true;
}

function validateDnsSetting(value, label, optional = false) {
  const raw = String(value == null ? "" : value).trim();
  if (!raw) {
    if (optional) return "";
    throw new Error(label + " نباید خالی باشد");
  }
  if (raw.toLowerCase() === "localhost" || raw.toLowerCase() === "local") return raw.toLowerCase();
  if (/^https:\/\//i.test(raw)) return validateDohUrl(raw);
  try {
    const hp = parseHostPort(raw, 53);
    if (!isIPv4Text(hp.host) && !isIPv6Text(hp.host) && !isDomainName(hp.host)) throw new Error();
    return hp.hasPort ? (uriHost(hp.host) + ":" + hp.port) : hp.host;
  } catch {
    throw new Error(label + " باید localhost، آدرس IP/دامنه یا URL از نوع HTTPS باشد");
  }
}
function safeStoredDns(value, fallback = "") {
  try { return validateDnsSetting(value, "DNS", fallback === ""); } catch { return fallback; }
}
function normalizeLogLevel(value, strict = false) {
  const level = String(value || "warning").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (["debug", "info", "warning", "error", "none"].includes(level)) return level;
  if (strict) throw new Error("Log Level نامعتبر است");
  return "warning";
}
function parseCustomBypass(value, strict = false) {
  const entries = [], seen = new Set();
  const lines = String(value || "").split(/[\r\n,]+/).map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  if (lines.length > 100) throw new Error("حداکثر ۱۰۰ قانون Bypass قابل ثبت است");
  for (const line of lines) {
    if (line.startsWith("#")) continue;
    const m = /^(domain|ip)\s*:\s*(.+)$/i.exec(line);
    if (!m) { if (strict) throw new Error("قانون Bypass نامعتبر: " + line + " (فرمت domain:example.com یا ip:192.0.2.1)"); else continue; }
    const type = m[1].toLowerCase();
    let valuePart = m[2].trim();
    if (type === "domain") {
      valuePart = valuePart.replace(/^\./, "").toLowerCase();
      if (!isDomainName(valuePart) || valuePart.length > 253) { if (strict) throw new Error("دامنهٔ Bypass نامعتبر: " + line); else continue; }
    } else {
      const parts = valuePart.split("/");
      if (parts.length > 2) { if (strict) throw new Error("IP/CIDR در Bypass نامعتبر: " + line); else continue; }
      const base = parts[0].trim();
      const v4 = isIPv4Text(base), v6 = isIPv6Text(base);
      let prefixOkay = parts.length === 1;
      if (parts.length === 2 && /^\d+$/.test(parts[1])) {
        const prefix = Number(parts[1]); prefixOkay = (v4 && prefix >= 0 && prefix <= 32) || (v6 && prefix >= 0 && prefix <= 128);
      }
      if ((!v4 && !v6) || !prefixOkay) { if (strict) throw new Error("IP/CIDR در Bypass نامعتبر: " + line); else continue; }
      valuePart = base.toLowerCase() + (parts.length === 2 ? "/" + Number(parts[1]) : (v4 ? "/32" : "/128"));
    }
    const key = type + ":" + valuePart;
    if (!seen.has(key)) { seen.add(key); entries.push({ type, value: valuePart, source: key }); }
  }
  return entries;
}
function normalizeCustomBypass(value) { return parseCustomBypass(value, true).map(x => x.source).join("\n"); }
function safeCustomBypass(value) { try { return normalizeCustomBypass(value); } catch { return ""; } }
function logLevelForSingBox(value) { const level = normalizeLogLevel(value); return level === "warning" ? "warn" : (level === "none" ? "error" : level); }
function getDnsTlsServerName(host) {
  const h = String(host || "").toLowerCase();
  if (["8.8.8.8", "8.8.4.4", "2001:4860:4860::8888", "2001:4860:4860::8844"].includes(h)) return "dns.google";
  if (["1.1.1.1", "1.0.0.1", "2606:4700:4700::1111", "2606:4700:4700::1001"].includes(h)) return "cloudflare-dns.com";
  return isIPv4Text(h) || isIPv6Text(h) ? "" : h;
}
function toSingBoxDnsServer(rawValue, tag) {
  const raw = String(rawValue || "").trim();
  if (!raw || raw.toLowerCase() === "local" || raw.toLowerCase() === "localhost") return { type: "local", tag };
  if (/^https:\/\//i.test(raw)) {
    const u = new URL(validateDohUrl(raw));
    const server = u.hostname;
    const tls = { enabled: true };
    const serverName = getDnsTlsServerName(server);
    if (serverName) tls.server_name = serverName;
    return { type: "https", tag, server, server_port: Number(u.port || 443), path: (u.pathname || "/dns-query") + (u.search || ""), tls };
  }
  const hp = parseHostPort(raw, 53);
  return { type: "udp", tag, server: hp.host, server_port: hp.port };
}
function buildXrayDnsServers(settings) {
  const servers = [];
  if (settings.fakeDnsEnabled) servers.push("fakedns");
  if (settings.bypassIran && settings.localDns) servers.push({ address: settings.localDns, domains: ["geosite:ir", "regexp:.*\\.ir$"] });
  if (settings.antiSanctionDns) servers.push(settings.antiSanctionDns);
  if (settings.remoteDns || DEFAULT_REMOTE_DNS) servers.push(settings.remoteDns || DEFAULT_REMOTE_DNS);
  if (!settings.bypassIran && settings.localDns && settings.localDns !== "localhost" && settings.localDns !== "local") servers.push(settings.localDns);
  return servers;
}
function validateDohUrl(value) {
  const raw = String(value || "").trim();
  try { const u = new URL(raw); if (u.protocol !== "https:" || !u.hostname || !u.hostname.includes(".")) throw new Error(); return u.href; }
  catch { throw new Error("آدرس DoH نامعتبر"); }
}
function safeDohUrl(value) { try { return validateDohUrl(value || DEFAULT_DOH_URL); } catch { return DEFAULT_DOH_URL; } }

function getEndpoints(settings, host) {
  const domainHost = cleanHost(host);
  const endpoints = domainHost ? [{ address: domainHost, label: "DOMAIN" }] : [];
  const seen = new Set(domainHost ? [domainHost.toLowerCase()] : []);
  for (const item of parseCleanIPs(settings.cleanIPs)) {
    const address = cleanHost(item.host);
    const key = address.toLowerCase();
    if (!address || seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key);
    endpoints.push({ address, label: item.label || address });
  }
  return endpoints;
}
function safeTag(label) { return String(label).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9._-]+/g, "_").slice(0, 32); }
function isHostLike(value) { return typeof value === "string" && value.length <= 253 && !/[\s/@?#]/.test(value); }
function isNumericRange(value, min, max) {
  const parts = String(value).split("-");
  if (parts.length > 2 || parts.some(p => !/^\d+$/.test(p))) return false;
  const first = Number(parts[0]), last = Number(parts.length === 2 ? parts[1] : parts[0]);
  return first >= min && last >= first && last <= max;
}
function parsePort(value, fallback) { const n = Number(value); return Number.isInteger(n) && n > 0 && n <= 65535 ? n : fallback; }

function normalizeExtraPorts(value, label = "") {
  if (value == null) return [];
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(`فهرست پورت‌های اضافی ${label} باید آرایه باشد`);
  if (value.length > 20) throw new Error(`حداکثر ۲۰ پورت اضافی ${label} مجاز است`); const out = []; for (const item of value) { const n = Number(item); if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 1 || n > 65535) throw new Error(`پورت اضافی ${label} باید بین ۱ تا ۶۵۵۳۵ باشد`);
    if (!out.includes(n)) out.push(n);
  }
  return out;
}

function safeStoredPortList(value) {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  return [...new Set(value.map(Number).filter(n => Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 65535))].slice(0, 20);
}

function getConfiguredPorts(settings, tls) {
  const primary = Number(tls ? settings.tlsPort : settings.nonTlsPort);
  const extra = safeStoredPortList(tls ? settings.extraTlsPorts : settings.extraNonTlsPorts);
  return [...new Set([primary, ...extra].filter(n => Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 65535))];
}

async function readJsonLimited(request, maxBytes) {
  const cl = request.headers.get("content-length");
  if (cl && Number(cl) > maxBytes) throw new Error("Payload too large");
  if (!request.body) throw new Error("Empty body");
  const reader = request.body.getReader();
  const chunks = [];
  let total = 0;
  try {
    while (true) {
      const { value, done } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      total += value.byteLength;
      if (total > maxBytes) { try{await reader.cancel();}catch{} throw new Error("Payload too large"); }
      chunks.push(value);
    }
  } finally { try{reader.releaseLock();}catch{} }
  try { return JSON.parse(dec.decode(concatChunks(chunks))); }
  catch { throw new Error("Invalid JSON"); }
}

function sameOrigin(request, url) {
  const origin = request.headers.get("Origin");
  if (origin) {
    try { return new URL(origin).origin === url.origin; } catch { return false; }
  }
  const referer = request.headers.get("Referer");
  if (!referer) return false;
  try { return new URL(referer).origin === url.origin; } catch { return false; }
}

function json(value, status = 200) {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(value), {
    status,
    headers: securityHeaders({ "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store" })
  });
}

function securityHeaders(extra = {}) {
  return {
    "x-content-type-options": "nosniff", "referrer-policy": "no-referrer",
    "permissions-policy": "camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()", ...extra
  };
}

function constantTimeEqual(a, b) {
  const x = enc.encode(String(a)), y = enc.encode(String(b));
  let diff = x.length ^ y.length;
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(x.length, y.length); i++) diff |= (x[i % (x.length||1)] || 0) ^ (y[i % (y.length||1)] || 0);
  return diff === 0;
}

function base64url(bytes) {
  let bin = ""; for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(bin).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/g, "");
}
function base64Utf8(text) { return btoa(Array.from(enc.encode(text), b => String.fromCharCode(b)).join('')); }

// -------------------- Telegram Web Proxy (TWP-CF) runtime --------------------
// Protocol engine adapted from https://github.com/ToiCF/CF-Workers-TGProxy/blob/main/telegram_webproxy.js
// TWP bridge configuration is persisted separately by the panel; sessions/leases and
// relay streams below intentionally remain in Worker-isolate memory only. The injected
// TelegramWebProxy / loopback MessagePort bridge is a client-side WebView contract, not a
// conventional SOCKS5 or raw MTProto proxy listener; clients without this native bridge
// cannot use the resulting tg://webproxy entry as an ordinary mobile proxy.
const TWP_RUNTIME = (() => {
  const CFG = { dnPack: 32 * 1024, upPack: 20 * 1024, dnMs: 1 };
  const txt = new TextEncoder(), dec = new TextDecoder(), nil = new Uint8Array(0), badHead = new Set(['HEAD', 'POST', 'GET ', 'OPTI']);
  const CRYPTO = globalThis.crypto, macKeys = new Map(), leases = new Map();
  const reply = (body = null, status = 404) => new Response(body, { status });
  function u8(v) { if (v instanceof Uint8Array && v.constructor === Uint8Array) return v; if (v instanceof ArrayBuffer) return new Uint8Array(v); if (ArrayBuffer.isView(v)) return new Uint8Array(v.buffer, v.byteOffset, v.byteLength); throw TypeError(); }
  const dup = v => u8(v).slice();
  function cat(...args) { const parts = args.filter(v => v?.byteLength).map(u8); const out = new Uint8Array(parts.reduce((total, part) => total + part.byteLength, 0)); let o = 0; for (const part of parts) { out.set(part, o); o += part.byteLength; } return out; }
  const hex = v => Uint8Array.from({ length: v.length / 2 }, (_, i) => Number.parseInt(v.slice(i * 2, i * 2 + 2), 16));
  function b64(v) { const b = v instanceof Uint8Array ? v : new Uint8Array(v); return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...b)).replaceAll('+', '-').replaceAll('/', '_').replace(/=+$/, ''); }
  const un64 = v => { const t = v.replaceAll('-', '+').replaceAll('_', '/'); return Uint8Array.from(atob(t + '='.repeat((4 - t.length % 4) % 4)), c => c.charCodeAt(0)); };
  const rand = n => CRYPTO.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(n));
  const wake = st => { const { wake: ok } = st; st.wake = null; ok?.(); };
  const isType = t => t === 1 || t === 2 || t === 3 || t === 4 || t === 17;
  function enc(type, sid, data = nil) { const body = u8(data); if (!isType(type)) throw RangeError(); if (!Number.isInteger(sid) || sid < 0 || sid > 0xffffff) throw RangeError(); if (body.byteLength > 1024 * 1024) throw RangeError(); const frame = new Uint8Array(8 + body.byteLength); frame[0] = type; frame[1] = sid >>> 16; frame[2] = sid >>> 8; frame[3] = sid; new DataView(frame.buffer).setUint32(4, body.byteLength, false); frame.set(body, 8); return frame; }
  function getFrame(b, o) { if (b.byteLength - o < 8) return null; const type = b[o]; if (!isType(type)) throw Error(); const view = new DataView(b.buffer, b.byteOffset + o, b.byteLength - o); const sid = (b[o + 1] << 16) | (b[o + 2] << 8) | b[o + 3]; const n = view.getUint32(4, false); if (n > 1024 * 1024) throw Error(); const total = 8 + n; if (b.byteLength - o < total) return null; return { frame: { type, sid, payload: b.subarray(o + 8, o + total) }, consumed: total }; }
  function frames(v) { const src = u8(v), out = []; for (let o = 0; o < src.byteLength;) { const part = getFrame(src, o); if (!part) throw Error(); if (out.length >= 4096) throw Error(); out.push(part.frame); o += part.consumed; } return out; }
  function win(data) { const body = u8(data); if (body.byteLength !== 4) throw Error(); const n = new DataView(body.buffer, body.byteOffset, 4).getUint32(0, false); if (!n) throw Error(); return n; }
  function check(frame) { const { type, payload: data } = frame; if (type === 1 || type === 3) { if (data.byteLength) throw Error(); return 0; } if (type === 2) { if (!data.byteLength) throw Error(); return 0; } if (type === 4) return win(data); throw Error(); }
  const i16 = (b, o) => (b[o] | b[o + 1] << 8) << 16 >> 16;
  function headOf(head) { const tag = new DataView(head.buffer, head.byteOffset, head.byteLength).getUint32(56, true); const mode = tag === 0xefefefef ? 0xef : tag === 0xeeeeeeee ? 0xee : tag === 0xdddddddd ? 0xdd : 0; if (!mode) throw Error(); const dc = i16(head, 60); const target = Math.abs(dc) - 1; if (target < 0 || target >= 5) throw Error(); return { mode, target, media: dc < 0 }; }
  function mkHead(mode, target, media) { const head = new Uint8Array(64); do { CRYPTO.getRandomValues(head); } while (head[0] === 0xef || badHead.has(dec.decode(head.subarray(0, 4))) || head.subarray(4, 8).every(v => v === 0)); head.fill(mode, 56, 60); new DataView(head.buffer).setInt16(60, (target + 1) * (media ? -1 : 1), true); return head; }
  function getKey(v, keepTag = false) { const str = String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!/^(?:[0-9a-f]{32}|(?:dd|ee|ef)[0-9a-f]{32})$/.test(str)) throw Error(); return hex(keepTag && str.length === 34 && str.startsWith('dd') ? str : str.slice(-32)); }
  function hostOf(v) { const host = String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\.$/, ''); const labels = host.split('.'); if (!host.includes('.') || host.length > 253 || host.includes(':') || labels.some(label => !/^[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?$/.test(label))) throw Error(); return host; }
  const macKey = (raw, id) => { let key = macKeys.get(id); if (!key) macKeys.set(id, key = CRYPTO.subtle.importKey('raw', u8(raw), { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign'])); return key; };
  const sign = async (key, v) => new Uint8Array(await CRYPTO.subtle.sign('HMAC', await key, u8(v)));
  async function mac(boot, kind, body) { const prefix = txt.encode(`${kind}\n`); const msg = new Uint8Array(prefix.byteLength + body.byteLength); msg.set(prefix); msg.set(body, prefix.byteLength); return (await sign(macKey(txt.encode(boot), `token:${boot}`), msg)).slice(0, 16); }
  async function mkToken(boot, kind, expAt, nonce) { const body = new Uint8Array(16); const exp = Math.ceil(expAt / 1000); new DataView(body.buffer).setUint32(0, exp, false); body.set(nonce, 4); const token = new Uint8Array(32); token.set(body); token.set(await mac(boot, kind, body), body.byteLength); return b64(token); }
  const mkBoot = async boot => mkToken(boot, 'bootstrap', Date.now() + 2 * 60 * 1000, rand(12));
  async function verify(v, boot, kind) { if (typeof v !== 'string' || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(v)) return false; let token; try { token = un64(v); } catch { return false; } if (token.byteLength !== 32 || b64(token) !== v) return false; const body = token.slice(0, 16), sig = token.slice(16); const exp = new DataView(body.buffer, body.byteOffset, body.byteLength).getUint32(0, false); if (exp <= Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)) return false; const want = await mac(boot, kind, body); let diff = sig.byteLength ^ want.byteLength; for (let i = 0; i < want.length; i++) diff |= sig[i] ^ want[i]; return diff === 0; }
  const mkSess = async boot => mkToken(boot, 'session', Date.now() + 5 * 60 * 1000, rand(12));
  const authBoot = async (v, boot) => v === boot || verify(v, boot, 'bootstrap');
  const mkLease = token => ({ expires: new DataView(un64(token).buffer).getUint32(0) * 1000, active: new Set(), used: new Set(), deleted: false });
  const bearer = v => /^Bearer ([^\s]+)$/.exec(v || '')?.[1] ?? null;
  const binary = req => req.headers.get('Content-Type')?.trim().toLowerCase() === 'application/octet-stream';
  function hello(body) { if (body.byteLength !== 9 || body[0] !== 0x10 || body[1] !== 0 || body[2] !== 0 || body[3] !== 0 || new DataView(body.buffer, body.byteOffset, body.byteLength).getUint32(4, false) !== 1 || body[8] !== 1) throw Error(); }
  const protos = req => (req.headers.get('Sec-WebSocket-Protocol') ?? '').split(',').map(v => v.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const mkCfg = ({ SECRET, BOOT }) => { const secret = String(SECRET || '').trim(), boot = String(BOOT || '').trim(); if (!secret || !boot) throw Error(); return { secret, key: getKey(secret), boot }; };
  async function mkCap(secret, host) { const key = macKey(getKey(secret, true), `bridge:${String(secret).trim().toLowerCase()}`); const sig = await sign(key, txt.encode(`tdesktop-web-proxy-bridge-v1\n${hostOf(host)}`)); return b64(sig); }
  async function bridge(url, secret) { if (url.pathname !== '/' || url.search.length !== 51) return null; const cand = url.search.slice(8); if (url.search !== `?bridge=${cand}` || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(cand)) return null; const host = hostOf(url.hostname); const want = await mkCap(secret, host); if (cand.length !== want.length) return null; let diff = 0; for (let i = 0; i < want.length; i++) diff |= cand.charCodeAt(i) ^ want.charCodeAt(i); return diff === 0 ? host : null; }
  const sha = async v => new Uint8Array(await CRYPTO.subtle.digest('SHA-256', u8(v)));
  function step(ctr, n) { let carry = n; for (let i = ctr.length - 1; i >= 0 && carry; i--) { const next = ctr[i] + (carry & 0xff); ctr[i] = next & 0xff; carry = Math.floor(carry / 256) + Math.floor(next / 256); } if (carry) throw Error(); }
  const mkCtr = (key, ctr) => { let iv = dup(ctr), tail = nil; const ready = CRYPTO.subtle.importKey('raw', u8(key), { name: 'AES-CTR' }, false, ['encrypt']); const crypt = async v => { const b = u8(v); if (!b.byteLength) return new Uint8Array(0); const k = await ready; if (!tail.byteLength && !(b.byteLength % 16)) { const raw = new Uint8Array(await CRYPTO.subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-CTR', counter: iv.slice(), length: 128 }, k, b)); step(iv, b.byteLength / 16); return raw; } if (b.byteLength <= tail.byteLength) { const out = new Uint8Array(b.byteLength); for (let i = 0; i < b.byteLength; i++) out[i] = b[i] ^ tail[i]; tail = tail.slice(b.byteLength); return out; } const skip = tail.byteLength ? 16 - tail.byteLength : 0; const ctrNow = iv.slice(); if (skip) { for (let i = ctrNow.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (ctrNow[i]--) break; } const end = skip + b.byteLength; const block = new Uint8Array(Math.ceil(end / 16) * 16); block.set(b, skip); const raw = new Uint8Array(await CRYPTO.subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-CTR', counter: ctrNow, length: 128 }, k, block)); tail = raw.slice(end); step(iv, block.byteLength / 16 - (skip ? 1 : 0)); return raw.subarray(skip, end); }; return { crypt }; };
  async function cipher(head, secret, reverse) { const mat = dup(u8(head).subarray(8, 56)); if (reverse) mat.reverse(); const key = secret.byteLength ? await sha(cat(mat.subarray(0, 32), secret)) : mat.subarray(0, 32); return mkCtr(key, mat.subarray(32, 48)); }
  async function wireHead(head) { const ctr = await cipher(head, nil, false); const raw = await ctr.crypt(head); raw.set(u8(head).subarray(0, 56)); return { wire: raw, cipher: ctr }; }
  const hosts = (dc, media) => [`kws${dc}${media ? '-1' : ''}.web.telegram.org`, `kws${dc}${media ? '' : '-1'}.web.telegram.org`];
  async function dial({ target, media, signal }) { for (const host of hosts(target + 1, media)) { if (signal?.aborted) throw Error(); const url = `wss://${host}/apiws`; let kws; try { kws = mkWire(new WebSocket(url, 'binary'), 7500, signal); await kws.ready; if (signal?.aborted) throw Error(); return kws; } catch (err) { kws?.close(); if (signal?.aborted) throw err; } } throw Error(); }
  const mkWire = (ws, ms, signal) => { const q = [], wait = []; let h = 0, qBytes = 0, closed = false, dead = false, up = false, fault = null; let pWake = null, pNeed, openTimer, readyOk, readyBad; const ready = new Promise((ok, bad) => { readyOk = ok; readyBad = bad; }); const onAbort = () => { end(Error()); close(); }; const onOpen = () => { if (closed) return; if (ws.protocol !== 'binary') { end(Error()); close(); return; } up = true; clearTimeout(openTimer); readyOk(api); }; const onData = v => { if (closed) return; const b = v instanceof Uint8Array ? v : new Uint8Array(v); if (!b.byteLength) return; const waiter = wait.shift(); if (waiter) waiter.resolve(b); else { q.push(b); qBytes += b.byteLength; if (pWake && (qBytes >= pNeed || b.byteLength >= CFG.dnPack)) pWake(); } }; const end = (err = null) => { if (closed) return; closed = true; fault = err; clearTimeout(openTimer); signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort); if (!up) readyBad(err || Error()); pWake?.(); for (const waiter of wait.splice(0)) err ? waiter.reject(err) : waiter.resolve(null); }; const write = async v => { await ready; if (closed) throw fault || Error(); ws.send(u8(v)); }; const take = async () => { await ready; if (h < q.length) { const msg = q[h]; q[h++] = null; qBytes -= msg.byteLength; trim(); return msg; } if (fault) throw fault; if (closed) return null; return new Promise((ok, bad) => wait.push({ resolve: ok, reject: bad })); }; const pack = async () => { const first = await take(); if (dead) return null; if (!first || first.byteLength >= CFG.dnPack) return first; if (!closed && qBytes < CFG.dnPack - first.byteLength) await new Promise(ok => { pNeed = CFG.dnPack - first.byteLength; const timer = setTimeout(() => pWake?.(), CFG.dnMs); pWake = () => { clearTimeout(timer); pWake = null; ok(); }; }); if (dead) return null; let n = first.byteLength, endAt = h; while (endAt < q.length && n + q[endAt].byteLength <= CFG.dnPack) n += q[endAt++].byteLength; if (endAt === h) return first; const b = new Uint8Array(n); b.set(first); let o = first.byteLength; while (h < endAt) { const part = q[h]; q[h++] = null; b.set(part, o); o += part.byteLength; qBytes -= part.byteLength; } trim(); return b; }; const trim = () => { if (h === q.length) { q.length = 0; h = 0; } else if (h >= 256 && h * 2 >= q.length) { q.splice(0, h); h = 0; } }; const close = () => { if (dead) return; dead = true; if (!closed) end(); q.length = h = qBytes = 0; try { ws.close(1000); } catch {} }; const api = { ready, write, readPack: pack, close, get closed() { return closed; } }; ws.binaryType = 'arraybuffer'; ws.addEventListener('open', onOpen); ws.addEventListener('message', e => onData(e.data)); ws.addEventListener('error', () => end(Error())); ws.addEventListener('close', e => end(e?.code && e.code !== 1000 ? Error() : null)); openTimer = setTimeout(() => { end(Error()); close(); }, ms); signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true }); if (signal?.aborted) onAbort(); return api; };
  const mkPkt = mode => { let tail = nil; const push = v => { const b = u8(v); if (b.byteLength) tail = tail.byteLength ? cat(tail, b) : b; const pkts = []; let o = 0; for (;;) { const avail = tail.byteLength - o; let hLen, pLen; if (mode === 0xef) { if (avail < 1) break; let words = tail[o] & 0x7f; if (words === 0x7f) { if (avail < 4) break; words = tail[o + 1] | (tail[o + 2] << 8) | (tail[o + 3] << 16); hLen = 4; } else hLen = 1; pLen = words * 4; } else { if (avail < 4) break; pLen = new DataView(tail.buffer, tail.byteOffset + o, 4).getUint32(0, true) & 0x7fffffff; hLen = 4; } if (!pLen || pLen > 8 * 1024 * 1024) throw Error(); const total = hLen + pLen; if (avail < total) break; pkts.push(tail.subarray(o, o + total)); o += total; } if (o) tail = o === tail.byteLength ? nil : tail.slice(o); if (tail.byteLength > 8 * 1024 * 1024 + 4) throw Error(); return pkts; }; const clear = () => { tail = nil; }; return { push, clear }; };
  const mkStream = ({ secret, emit, onClose }) => { let hdr = nil, cIn = null, cOut = null, kIn = null, kOut = null, peer = null, pkt = null; let init = false, closed = false, chain = Promise.resolve(); const ac = new AbortController(), uq = new Set(); const push = v => { if (closed) return Promise.reject(Error()); const job = { bytes: u8(v) }; uq.add(job); chain = chain.then(async () => { if (closed) throw Error(); if (!job.bytes) return job.result; const jobs = [job]; let n = job.bytes.byteLength; for (const next of uq) { if (next === job) continue; if (n >= CFG.upPack || n + next.bytes.byteLength > CFG.upPack) break; n += next.bytes.byteLength; jobs.push(next); } const b = jobs.length === 1 ? job.bytes : cat(...jobs.map(x => x.bytes)); for (const item of jobs) { uq.delete(item); item.bytes = null; } const result = await feed(b); for (const item of jobs) item.result = result; return result; }); return chain; }; const feed = async b => { if (closed) throw Error(); if (init && peer.closed) return false; if (!b.byteLength) return; if (!init) { const need = 64 - hdr.byteLength; if (b.byteLength < need) { hdr = cat(hdr, b); return; } hdr = cat(hdr, b.subarray(0, need)); await start(); const rest = b.subarray(need); if (rest.byteLength) return write(rest); return; } return write(b); }; const start = async () => { const raw = hdr; const cr = await cipher(raw, secret, false); const plain = await cr.crypt(raw); if (closed) return; const info = headOf(plain); const head = mkHead(info.mode, info.target, info.media); const [cw, out, kr] = await Promise.all([cipher(raw, secret, true), wireHead(head), cipher(head, nil, true)]); if (closed) return; const kws = await dial({ target: info.target, media: info.media, signal: ac.signal }); if (closed) { kws.close(); return; } peer = kws; cIn = cr; cOut = cw; kOut = out.cipher; kIn = kr; pkt = mkPkt(info.mode); await kws.write(out.wire); if (closed) return; hdr = nil; init = true; pump().catch(() => close()); }; const write = async raw => { if (closed || peer.closed) return false; const plain = await cIn.crypt(raw); if (closed || peer.closed) return false; const pkts = pkt.push(plain); for (let start = 0; start < pkts.length;) { let end = start + 1, n = pkts[start].byteLength; while (end < pkts.length && n + pkts[end].byteLength <= CFG.upPack) n += pkts[end++].byteLength; const first = pkts[start]; const b = end === start + 1 ? first : new Uint8Array(first.buffer, first.byteOffset, n); if (closed || peer.closed) return false; const wire = await kOut.crypt(b); for (let o = 0; start < end; start++) { if (closed || peer.closed) return false; const size = pkts[start].byteLength; try { await peer.write(wire.subarray(o, o + size)); } catch (err) { if (closed || peer.closed) return false; throw err; } o += size; } } }; const pump = async () => { while (!closed) { const b = await peer.readPack(); if (closed) return; if (!b) return close(); const plain = await kIn.crypt(b); if (closed) return; const raw = await cOut.crypt(plain); if (closed) return; await emit(raw); } }; const close = () => { if (closed) return; closed = true; ac.abort(); try { peer?.close(); } catch {} for (const job of uq) job.bytes = null; uq.clear(); hdr = nil; pkt?.clear(); cIn = cOut = kIn = kOut = peer = null; try { onClose(); } catch {} }; return { push, close }; };
  const mkSession = ({ secret, sid, onClose, lease }) => { let sock = null, closed = false, tx = Promise.resolve(), cur = null; const attach = ws => { if (sock || closed) throw Error(); sock = ws; ws.accept({ allowHalfOpen: true }); ws.binaryType = 'arraybuffer'; ws.addEventListener('message', e => { if (closed) return; try { onData(e.data); } catch { close(1008); } }); ws.addEventListener('close', () => close()); ws.addEventListener('error', () => close()); }; const onData = v => { if (closed) return; if (lease?.deleted) return close(1000); if (typeof v === 'string') throw Error(); const b = v instanceof Uint8Array ? v : new Uint8Array(v); if (!b.byteLength || b.byteLength > 2 * 1024 * 1024) throw Error(); for (const frame of frames(b)) { if (closed) return; const delta = check(frame); if (frame.sid !== sid) throw Error(); if (!cur && frame.type !== 1) throw Error(); if (frame.type !== 1 && cur?.closed) continue; if (frame.type === 1) open(); else if (frame.type === 2) write(frame.payload); else if (frame.type === 4) grant(delta); else if (frame.type === 3) end(false); else throw Error(); } }; const send = (type, data = nil) => { const out = enc(type, sid, data); const st = type === 2 || type === 4 ? cur : null; tx = tx.then(() => { if (closed) throw Error(); if ((type === 2 || type === 4) && (!st || st.closed)) return; sock.send(out); }); return tx; }; const open = () => { if (closed) return; if (cur) throw Error(); const st = { in: 4 * 1024 * 1024, out: 4 * 1024 * 1024, wake: null, closed: false, proxy: null, pItems: 0 }; st.proxy = mkStream({ secret, emit: b => emitDn(st, b), onClose: () => { if (!closed && !st.closed) end(true); } }); cur = st; }; const write = data => { if (closed || cur?.closed) return; const st = cur; if (!st || st.closed) throw Error(); const n = data.byteLength; if (n > st.in) throw Error(); if (st.pItems >= 16384) throw Error(); st.in -= n; st.pItems++; st.proxy.push(data).then(accepted => { freeUp(st); if (closed || st.closed || accepted === false) return; st.in += n; const delta = new Uint8Array(4); new DataView(delta.buffer).setUint32(0, n); send(4, delta).catch(() => close()); }, () => { freeUp(st); if (!closed && !st.closed) st.proxy.close(); }).catch(() => close()); }; const freeUp = st => { if (st.closed) return; st.pItems--; }; const emitDn = async (st, b) => { for (let o = 0; o < b.byteLength;) { while (!closed && !st.closed && st.out === 0) await new Promise(ok => { st.wake = ok; }); if (closed || st.closed) throw Error(); const n = Math.min(64 * 1024, b.byteLength - o, st.out); st.out -= n; await send(2, b.subarray(o, o + n)); o += n; } }; const grant = n => { if (closed || cur?.closed) return; const st = cur; if (!st || st.closed) throw Error(); st.out = Math.min(0xffffffff, st.out + n); wake(st); }; const stop = st => { st.closed = true; st.pItems = 0; wake(st); st.proxy.close(); }; const end = notify => { if (closed || cur?.closed) return; const st = cur; if (!st) throw Error(); stop(st); if (notify) send(3).then(() => close(), () => close()); else close(); }; const close = (code = 1000, reason = '') => { if (closed) return; closed = true; if (cur) stop(cur); cur = null; try { sock?.close(code, reason); } catch {} onClose?.(); onClose = null; }; return { attach, close }; };
  function mkPage(host, boot) { const nonce = b64(rand(18)); const body = `<script nonce="__NONCE__">\n(()=>{'use strict'; const origin=__ORIGIN__,boot=__BOOTSTRAP__;\nconst aNonce=/^#android=([A-Za-z0-9_-]{43})$/.exec(location.hash)?.[1]??''; history.replaceState(null,'',location.pathname);\nlet init=false,closed=false,port=null,token='',started=false,delSent=false; let qBytes=0,qItems=0;\nconst lanes=new Map(),used=new Set(),qMax=33554432,iMax=16384,msgMax=2097152; const laneMax=8388608,laneItems=1024;\nconst true; }\nfunction free(lane,v){qBytes-=v.byteLength;qItems--;lane.bytes-=v.byteLength;lane.items--;}\nfunction fail(){ if(closed)return;state('failed');port?.postMessage({t:'close'});close(true);       .replaceAll('__NONCE__', nonce)
      .replace('__ORIGIN__', JSON.stringify(`https://${host}`))
      .replace('__BOOTSTRAP__', JSON.stringify(String(boot)));
    const csp = ["default-src 'none'", "base-uri 'none'", "child-src 'none'", `connect-src 'self' wss://${host}`, "font-src 'none'", "form-action 'none'", 'frame-ancestors http://127.0.0.1:*', "frame-src 'none'", "img-src 'none'", "manifest-src 'none'", "media-src 'none'", "object-src 'none'", `script-src 'nonce-${nonce}'`, "style-src 'none'", "worker-src 'none'", 'sandbox allow-same-origin allow-scripts'].join('; ');
    return { body, csp };
  }
  function runtimeConfig(config) { return mkCfg({ SECRET: config?.secret, BOOT: config?.boot }); }
  async function handleBridgePage(request, url, config) {
    if (request.method !== 'GET' || url.pathname !== '/') return reply();
    let cfg, host = null;
    try { cfg = runtimeConfig(config); host = await bridge(url, cfg.secret); } catch { return reply(); }
    if (!host) return reply();
    const page = mkPage(host, await mkBoot(cfg.boot));
    const permissions = ['accelerometer=()', 'autoplay=()', 'camera=()', 'clipboard-read=()', 'clipboard-write=()', 'display-capture=()', 'encrypted-media=()', 'fullscreen=()', 'geolocation=()', 'gyroscope=()', 'hid=()', 'idle-detection=()', 'magnetometer=()', 'microphone=()', 'midi=()', 'payment=()', 'picture-in-picture=()', 'publickey-credentials-create=()', 'publickey-credentials-get=()', 'screen-wake-lock=()', 'serial=()', 'usb=()', 'web-share=()', 'xr-spatial-tracking=()'].join(', ');
    return new Response(page.body, { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', 'Content-Security-Policy': page.csp, 'Cache-Control': 'no-store', 'Referrer-Policy': 'no-referrer', 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff', 'Permissions-Policy': permissions } });
  }
  async function handleSession(request, config) {
    let cfg; try { cfg = runtimeConfig(config); } catch { return reply(null, 500); }
    const auth = bearer(request.headers.get('Authorization'));
    if (request.method === 'DELETE') {
      if (!auth || !(await verify(auth, cfg.boot, 'session'))) return reply();
      let lease = leases.get(auth); if (!lease) leases.set(auth, lease = mkLease(auth)); lease.deleted = true;
      return reply(null, 204);
    }
    if (request.method !== 'POST' || !auth || !binary(request)) return reply();
    if (!(await authBoot(auth, cfg.boot))) return reply();
    try {
      const body = new Uint8Array(await request.arrayBuffer()); hello(body);
      const token = await mkSess(cfg.boot);
      return new Response(enc(0x11, 0), { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store', 'X-Session-Token': token, 'X-Carrier-Mode': 'websocket-lanes' } });
    } catch { return reply(); }
  }
  async function handleWs(request, config, url) {
    let cfg; try { cfg = runtimeConfig(config); } catch { return reply(null, 500); }
    const headers = request.headers, origin = url.origin;
    if (request.method !== 'GET' || headers.get('Upgrade')?.toLowerCase() !== 'websocket' || (headers.has('Origin') && headers.get('Origin') !== origin)) return reply();
    const offers = protos(request), proto = offers.length === 1 ? offers[0] : null;
    const parts = proto?.split('.') ?? [], [kind, token = '', id] = parts, sid = Number(id);
    if (parts.length !== 3 || kind !== 'tproxy-lane-v1' || !/^[1-9][0-9]{0,7}$/.test(id) || sid > 0xffffff || !(await verify(token, cfg.boot, 'session'))) return reply();
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [key, value] of leases) if (value.expires <= now && !value.active.size) leases.delete(key);
    let lease = leases.get(token);
    if (!lease) { if (leases.size >= 4096) return reply(null, 503); leases.set(token, lease = mkLease(token)); }
    if (lease.deleted || lease.used.has(sid) || lease.active.size >= 128 || lease.used.size >= 4096) return reply(null, 409);
    lease.used.add(sid); lease.active.add(sid);
    const session = mkSession({ secret: cfg.key, sid, lease, onClose: () => lease.active.delete(sid) });
    const pair = new WebSocketPair();
    const client = pair[0], server = pair[1];
    try { session.attach(server); } catch { session.close(); return reply(null, 409); }
    return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client, headers: { 'Sec-WebSocket-Protocol': proto, 'Sec-WebSocket-Extensions': '' } });
  }
  async function makeBridgeToken(secret, host) { return await mkCap(String(secret || '').trim(), hostOf(host)); }
  function isValidSecret(secret) { return /^[a-f0-9]{32}$/i.test(String(secret || '').trim()); }
  return { handleBridgePage, handleSession, handleWs, makeBridgeToken, hostOf, isValidSecret };
})();

// -------------------- TWP-CF panel configuration storage --------------------
const TWP_CONFIG_KEY = 'panel-twp-cf-config-v1';
function randomHex(bytes = 16) { const value = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(bytes)); return Array.from(value, b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join(''); }
async function loadTwpConfig(kv) {
  const fallback = { enabled: false, secret: '', boot: '', updatedAt: 0 };
  if (PANEL_DB) {
    await ensurePanelD1Schema();
    const row = await PANEL_DB.prepare('SELECT value FROM panel_tg_proxy WHERE key = ?1').bind('config').first();
    if (row?.value) return { ...fallback, ...(parseStoredJson(row.value, {}) || {}) };
  }
  if (kv) {
    const stored = await kv.get(TWP_CONFIG_KEY, 'json');
    if (stored && typeof stored === 'object') return { ...fallback, ...stored };
    if (typeof stored === 'string') return { ...fallback, ...(parseStoredJson(stored, {}) || {}) };
  }
  return fallback;
}
async function saveTwpConfig(kv, config) {
  const value = JSON.stringify({ enabled: config.enabled === true, secret: String(config.secret || '').toLowerCase(), boot: String(config.boot || ''), updatedAt: Date.now() });
  if (PANEL_DB) {
    await ensurePanelD1Schema();
    await PANEL_DB.prepare('INSERT INTO panel_tg_proxy (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value, updated_at = excluded.updated_at').bind('config', value, Date.now()).run();
    return;
  }
  if (kv) { await kv.put(TWP_CONFIG_KEY, value); return; }
  throw new Error('برای ذخیره BRIDGE_SECRET باید D1 یا KV متصل باشد.');
}
function getTwpHost(value, fallback) {
  const strip = v => String(v || '').trim().replace(/^https?:\/\//i, '').split('/')[0].replace(/:\d+$/, '').replace(/^\[|\]$/g, '').toLowerCase();
  try { return TWP_RUNTIME.hostOf(strip(value)); } catch { return TWP_RUNTIME.hostOf(strip(fallback)); }
}
async function buildTwpAdminState(config, env, kv, url) {
  const settings = kv ? await loadSettings(kv, env) : {};
  let host;
  try { host = getTwpHost(settings.publicHost || env.PUBLIC_HOST || url.hostname, url.hostname); } catch { host = TWP_RUNTIME.hostOf(url.hostname); }
  const state = { enabled: config.enabled === true, status: config.enabled ? 'active' : (config.secret ? 'stopped' : 'not_created'), secret: config.secret || '', host, links: {} };
  if (config.enabled && TWP_RUNTIME.isValidSecret(config.secret)) {
    const signed = await TWP_RUNTIME.makeBridgeToken(config.secret, host);
    state.links = { tg: `tg://webproxy?server=${encodeURIComponent(host)}&secret=${encodeURIComponent(config.secret)}`, webBridge: `https://${host}/?bridge=${encodeURIComponent(signed)}` };
  }
  return state;
}
async function handleTwpAdminConfig(request, env, kv, url) {
  if (request.method === 'GET') {
    try { return json(await buildTwpAdminState(await loadTwpConfig(kv), env, kv, url)); }
    catch (err) { return json({ error: String(err?.message || 'خواندن تنظیمات TWP-CF ناموفق بود') }, 500); }
  }
  if (request.method !== 'POST') return json({ error: 'Method Not Allowed' }, 405);
  try {
    const body = await readJsonLimited(request, 2048);
    const config = await loadTwpConfig(kv);
    const action = String(body.action || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (action === 'enable') {
      const requestedSecret = String(body.secret || '').trim();
      const nextSecret = requestedSecret || config.secret || randomHex(16);
      if (!TWP_RUNTIME.isValidSecret(nextSecret)) throw new Error('BRIDGE_SECRET باید دقیقاً ۳۲ کاراکتر hex باشد.');
      if (!config.boot || nextSecret.toLowerCase() !== String(config.secret || '').toLowerCase()) config.boot = randomHex(32);
      config.secret = nextSecret.toLowerCase();
      config.enabled = true;
    } else if (action === 'disable') {
      config.enabled = false;
    } else if (action === 'regenerate') {
      config.secret = randomHex(16);
      config.boot = randomHex(32);
      config.enabled = true;
    } else {
      throw new Error('عملیات TWP-CF نامعتبر است.');
    }
    await saveTwpConfig(kv, config);
    return json(await buildTwpAdminState(config, env, kv, url));
  } catch (err) {
    return json({ error: String(err?.message || 'ذخیره تنظیمات TWP-CF ناموفق بود').slice(0, 240), code: 'ERR_TWP_CONFIG' }, 400);
  }
}
// -------------------- End Telegram Web Proxy runtime --------------------

// -------------------- Subscriptions & Uniform Config Generator --------------------
function generateOutboundsData(settings, endpoints) {
  const outbounds = [];
  let index = 1;
  const tlsPorts = getConfiguredPorts(settings, true);
  const nonTlsPorts = getConfiguredPorts(settings, false);
  const exitNodes = getActiveExitNodes(settings);

  endpoints.forEach((e, endpointIndex) => {
    const add = (proto, isTls, port, exitId = "", exitLabel = "") => {
      const num = String(index++).padStart(2, '0');
      const trName = isTls ? "TLS" : "WS";
      const baseTag = `${settings.username} config${num} ${proto}${trName}:${port}${e.label ? " ("+e.label+")" : ""}${exitLabel ? " · "+exitLabel : ""}`;
      outbounds.push({ proto, isTls, port, tag: baseTag, address: e.address, label: e.label, exitId });
    };
    const addAllPaths = (proto, isTls, port) => {
      add(proto, isTls, port);
      // Avoid multiplying every Clean IP by every country. Keep all legacy entries,
      // then add country variants for a bounded number of entry endpoints.
      if (endpointIndex < MAX_EXIT_ENDPOINTS) {
        add(proto, isTls, port, "auto", "AUTO");
        for (const node of exitNodes) add(proto, isTls, port, node.id, node.country + " " + node.name);
      }
    };
    if (settings.vlessEnabled && settings.uuid) {
      tlsPorts.forEach(port => addAllPaths("VLESS", true, port));
      nonTlsPorts.forEach(port => addAllPaths("VLESS", false, port));
    }
    if (settings.trojanEnabled && settings.trojanPassword) {
      tlsPorts.forEach(port => addAllPaths("Trojan", true, port));
      nonTlsPorts.forEach(port => addAllPaths("Trojan", false, port));
    }
  });
  return outbounds;
}

function generateShadowsocksOutboundsData(settings, endpoints) {
  if (settings.shadowsocksEnabled !== true) return [];
  const password = String(settings.shadowsocksPassword || settings.uuid || "");
  const subToken = String(settings.subToken || "");
  if (!password || !subToken) return [];
  const result = [];
  let index = 1;
  const exitNodes = getActiveExitNodes(settings);
  const ports = [...getConfiguredPorts(settings, true).map(port => ({ port, isTls: true })), ...getConfiguredPorts(settings, false).map(port => ({ port, isTls: false }))];
  endpoints.forEach((endpoint, endpointIndex) => {
    for (const port of ports) {
      const add = (exitId = "", label = "") => result.push({
        proto: "Shadowsocks", address: endpoint.address, label: endpoint.label, port: port.port, isTls: port.isTls,
        exitId, tag: `${settings.username} Shadowsocks ${port.isTls ? "TLS" : "WS"}:${port.port} #${String(index++).padStart(2, "0")}${label ? " · " + label : ""}`
      });
      add();
      if (endpointIndex < MAX_EXIT_ENDPOINTS) {
        add("auto", "AUTO");
        for (const node of exitNodes) add(node.id, node.country + " " + node.name);
      }
    }
  });
  return result;
}

async function handleSubscription(request, env, kv, url) {
  if (request.method !== "GET") return new Response("Method not allowed", { status: 405 });
  const segments = url.pathname.split("/").filter(Boolean);
  if (segments.length !== 3 || segments[0] !== "sub") return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const settings = await loadSettings(kv, env);
  const token = String(segments[1] || "");
  const user = findUserByCredential(settings, "sub", token);
  if (!user) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  let scoped = userScopedSettings(settings, user);
  if (user.id !== "default") {
    const usage = await getUserUsage(kv, user);
    const localTraffic = getLocalUserTrafficState(user.id, usage);
    usage.usageBytes = Math.max(Number(usage.usageBytes) || 0, localTraffic.usageBytes);
    if (!isUserSubscriptionAvailable(user, usage)) return new Response("Subscription expired or quota exhausted", { status: 410, headers: securityHeaders({ "cache-control": "no-store" }) });
  }
  const host = cleanHost(settings.publicHost) || url.hostname;
  const safeName = String(scoped.username || "user").replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_").slice(0, 48) || "user";

  if (segments[2] === "xray") {
    const uris = makeUris(scoped, host);
    if (!uris.length) return new Response("No config", { status: 404 });
    return new Response(base64Utf8(uris.join("\n")), { headers: securityHeaders({
      "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store",
      "profile-title": safeName + " Subscription"
    }) });
  }
  if (segments[2] === "singbox") {
    const config = makeSingBoxConfig(scoped, host);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(config, null, 2), { headers: securityHeaders({
      "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store",
      "content-disposition": "attachment; filename=" + safeName + "-sing-box.json"
    }) });
  }
  if (segments[2] === "xray-json") {
    const config = makeXrayConfig(scoped, host);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(config, null, 2), { headers: securityHeaders({
      "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store",
      "content-disposition": "attachment; filename=" + safeName + "-xray.json"
    }) });
  }
  return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
}

function makeSubscriptionLinks(settings, requestUrl, host) {
  const origin = requestUrl.origin;
  const token = encodeURIComponent(settings.subToken || "");
  return {
    xray: origin + "/sub/" + token + "/xray",
    singbox: origin + "/sub/" + token + "/singbox",
    xrayJson: origin + "/sub/" + token + "/xray-json",
    uris: makeUris(settings, host)
  };
}

// Extracts a valid Base64 ECHConfigList from the free-text setting. Accepts a
// plain Base64 line (standard or URL-safe) or a PEM "ECH CONFIGS" block.
// Returns standard padded Base64, or "" when nothing valid was supplied, so
// callers can omit the ECH field instead of emitting something clients cannot decode.
function normalizeEchConfigList(raw) {
  const text = String(raw == null ? "" : raw).trim();
  if (!text) return "";
  let candidates;
  if (/-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*ECH[A-Z ]*-----/i.test(text)) {
    const body = text.replace(/-----BEGIN [^-]+-----/gi, "\n").replace(/-----END [^-]+-----/gi, "\n");
    candidates = [body.replace(/\s+/g, "")];
  } else {
    candidates = text.split(/\r?\n/).map(line => line.replace(/\s+/g, "")).filter(Boolean);
  }
  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9+/_-]+={0,2}$/.test(candidate)) continue;
    let b64 = candidate.replace(/=+$/, "").replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/");
    if (b64.length % 4 === 1) continue;
    b64 += "=".repeat((4 - (b64.length % 4)) % 4);
    let bytes;
    try { bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(b64), ch => ch.charCodeAt(0)); } catch { continue; }
    // ECHConfigList = uint16 length prefix followed by one or more ECHConfig structures.
    if (bytes.length < 8 || ((bytes[0] << 8) | bytes[1]) !== bytes.length - 2) continue;
    return b64;
  }
  return "";
}
function echConfigForOutput(settings) {
  return settings.echEnabled ? normalizeEchConfigList(settings.echConfigList) : "";
}
function echPemLines(b64) {
  const lines = ["-----BEGIN ECH CONFIGS-----"];
  for (let i = 0; i < b64.length; i += 64) lines.push(b64.slice(i, i + 64));
  lines.push("-----END ECH CONFIGS-----");
  return lines;
}

// One source of truth for the WebSocket path so the URI, Xray and Sing-box
// outputs can never disagree. Xray-style early data: "?ed=N" in the path makes
// V2RayN/Nekoray/Xray carry the first payload in Sec-WebSocket-Protocol.
function wsPathFor(settings, proto, withEarlyDataQuery = true, exitId = "") {
  const params = [];
  if (withEarlyDataQuery && settings.earlyData) params.push("ed=" + EARLY_DATA_SIZE);
  if (exitId) params.push("exit=" + encodeURIComponent(exitId));
  return "/" + String(proto).toLowerCase() + (params.length ? "?" + params.join("&") : "");
}
function ensureWsPathExitId(path, exitId) {
  if (!exitId) return String(path || "/");
  const value = String(path || "/");
  const splitAt = value.indexOf("?");
  const pathname = splitAt < 0 ? value : value.slice(0, splitAt);
  const query = splitAt < 0 ? "" : value.slice(splitAt + 1);
  const params = new URLSearchParams(query);
  params.set("exit", String(exitId));
  return pathname + "?" + params.toString();
}

function shadowsocksWsPath(settings, exitId = "") {
  const token = encodeURIComponent(String(settings.subToken || ""));
  return "/ss/" + token + (exitId ? "?exit=" + encodeURIComponent(exitId) : "");
}

function base64Utf8Standard(value) {
  return btoa(Array.from(enc.encode(String(value)), byte => String.fromCharCode(byte)).join(""));
}

function makeShadowsocksUri(settings, item, host, password) {
  const method = SHADOWSOCKS_METHODS.has(settings.shadowsocksMethod) ? settings.shadowsocksMethod : "aes-256-gcm";
  const pluginOptions = ["mux=0", "mode=websocket", "host=" + cleanHost(host), "path=" + shadowsocksWsPath(settings, item.exitId)];
  if (item.isTls) pluginOptions.push("tls");
  const credentials = base64Utf8Standard(method + ":" + password);
  const plugin = encodeURIComponent("v2ray-plugin;" + pluginOptions.join(";"));
  const tag = encodeURIComponent(item.tag || "Shadowsocks");
  return `ss://${credentials}@${uriHost(item.address)}:${item.port}/?plugin=${plugin}#${tag}`;
}

function makeUrisUncached(settings, host) {
  const result = [];
  const publicHost = cleanHost(host);
  const pathOf = base => encodeURIComponent(wsPathFor(settings, base.replace(/^\//, "")));
  const fp = encodeURIComponent(normalizeFingerprint(settings.fingerprint));
  // ECH is emitted only for a valid user-supplied ECHConfigList; never synthesized.
  const echValue = echConfigForOutput(settings);
  const echExtra = echValue ? "&ech=" + encodeURIComponent(echValue) : "";
  const tlsExtra = "&fp=" + fp + "&alpn=" + encodeURIComponent("http/1.1") + echExtra;
  const pass = settings.trojanPassword ? encodeURIComponent(settings.trojanPassword) : "";

  const endpoints = getEndpoints(settings, publicHost);
  const outbounds = generateOutboundsData(settings, endpoints);

  for (const o of outbounds) {
    const addr = uriHost(o.address);
    const safeTagStr = encodeURIComponent(o.tag);
    if (o.proto === "VLESS") {
      if (o.isTls) result.push(`vless://${settings.uuid}@${addr}:${o.port}?encryption=none&security=tls&type=ws&host=${encodeURIComponent(publicHost)}&sni=${encodeURIComponent(publicHost)}${tlsExtra}&path=${encodeURIComponent(wsPathFor(settings, "vless", true, o.exitId))}#${safeTagStr}`);
      else result.push(`vless://${settings.uuid}@${addr}:${o.port}?encryption=none&security=none&type=ws&host=${encodeURIComponent(publicHost)}&path=${encodeURIComponent(wsPathFor(settings, "vless", true, o.exitId))}#${safeTagStr}`);
    } else if (o.proto === "Trojan") {
      const path = encodeURIComponent(wsPathFor(settings, "trojan", true, o.exitId));
      if (o.isTls) result.push(`trojan://${pass}@${addr}:${o.port}?security=tls&type=ws&host=${encodeURIComponent(publicHost)}&sni=${encodeURIComponent(publicHost)}${tlsExtra}&path=${path}#${safeTagStr}`);
      else result.push(`trojan://${pass}@${addr}:${o.port}?security=none&type=ws&host=${encodeURIComponent(publicHost)}&path=${path}#${safeTagStr}`);
    }
  }
  const ssPassword = String(settings.shadowsocksPassword || settings.uuid || "");
  for (const item of generateShadowsocksOutboundsData(settings, endpoints)) result.push(makeShadowsocksUri(settings, item, publicHost, ssPassword));
  return result;
}

function makeUris(settings, host) {
  const exits = getActiveExitNodes(settings).map(node => [node.id, node.name, node.country, node.uri, node.enabled !== false]);
  const cacheKey = JSON.stringify([
    cleanHost(host), settings.username, settings.uuid, settings.trojanPassword, settings.shadowsocksPassword,
    settings.subToken, settings.vlessEnabled, settings.trojanEnabled, settings.shadowsocksEnabled, settings.shadowsocksMethod,
    settings.tlsPort, settings.nonTlsPort, settings.extraTlsPorts, settings.extraNonTlsPorts,
    settings.cleanIPs, settings.earlyData, settings.fingerprint, settings.echEnabled, settings.echConfigList, exits
  ]);
  const now = Date.now();
  const cached = SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE.get(cacheKey);
  if (cached && cached.expiresAt > now) return cached.uris.slice();
  const uris = makeUrisUncached(settings, host);
  SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE.set(cacheKey, { uris, expiresAt: now + SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE_TTL_MS });
  if (SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE.size > SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE_MAX) {
    for (const [key, entry] of SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE) if (entry.expiresAt <= now) SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE.delete(key);
    while (SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE.size > SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE_MAX) SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE.delete(SUBSCRIPTION_URI_CACHE.keys().next().value);
  }
  return uris.slice();
}

function makeXrayConfig(settings, host) {
  const sni = cleanHost(host);
  const endpoints = getEndpoints(settings, sni);
  const outboundsData = generateOutboundsData(settings, endpoints);
  const echConfig = echConfigForOutput(settings);
  const proxies = [];

  for (const o of outboundsData) {
    const stream = {
      network: "ws", security: o.isTls ? "tls" : "none",
      wsSettings: { path: ensureWsPathExitId(wsPathFor(settings, o.proto, true, ""), o.exitId), headers: { Host: sni } }
    };
    if (o.isTls) {
      stream.tlsSettings = { serverName: sni, allowInsecure: false, fingerprint: normalizeFingerprint(settings.fingerprint), alpn: ["http/1.1"] };
      if (echConfig) stream.tlsSettings.echConfigList = echConfig;
    }
    if (settings.fragmentEnabled && o.isTls) stream.sockopt = { dialerProxy: "fragment" };

    if (o.proto === "VLESS") {
      proxies.push({ tag: o.tag, protocol: "vless", settings: { vnext: [{ address: o.address, port: o.port, users: [{ id: settings.uuid, encryption: "none" }] }] }, streamSettings: stream });
    } else {
      proxies.push({ tag: o.tag, protocol: "trojan", settings: { servers: [{ address: o.address, port: o.port, password: settings.trojanPassword }] }, streamSettings: stream });
    }
  }
  const ssPassword = String(settings.shadowsocksPassword || settings.uuid || "");
  for (const item of generateShadowsocksOutboundsData(settings, endpoints)) {
    const path = ensureWsPathExitId(shadowsocksWsPath(settings), item.exitId);
    const streamSettings = {
      network: "ws", security: item.isTls ? "tls" : "none",
      wsSettings: { path: ensureWsPathExitId(path, item.exitId), headers: { Host: sni } }
    };
    if (item.isTls) {
      streamSettings.tlsSettings = { serverName: sni, allowInsecure: false, fingerprint: normalizeFingerprint(settings.fingerprint), alpn: ["http/1.1"] };
      if (echConfig) streamSettings.tlsSettings.echConfigList = echConfig;
    }
    proxies.push({ tag: item.tag, protocol: "shadowsocks", settings: { servers: [{ address: item.address, port: item.port, method: settings.shadowsocksMethod || "aes-256-gcm", password: ssPassword }] }, streamSettings });
  }

  const bypass = settings.bypassIran === true;
  // DNS must never leave through the operator's (filtered) network. Port-53 UDP is
  // handed to Xray's internal DNS module ("dns-out"), which resolves geosite:ir
  // names with the local DNS and everything else over the DoH servers; those DoH
  // requests follow the normal rules below and therefore travel through the tunnel.
  const rules = [{ type: "field", inboundTag: ["socks-in"], network: "udp", port: "53", outboundTag: "dns-out" }];
  if (bypass) {
    rules.push({ type: "field", ip: ["geoip:private"], outboundTag: "direct" });
    rules.push({ type: "field", domain: ["geosite:ir", "regexp:.*\\.ir$"], outboundTag: "direct" });
    rules.push({ type: "field", ip: ["geoip:ir"], outboundTag: "direct" });
  }
  const customBypass = parseCustomBypass(settings.customBypass);
  const customDomains = customBypass.filter(x => x.type === "domain").map(x => "domain:" + x.value);
  const customIps = customBypass.filter(x => x.type === "ip").map(x => x.value);
  if (customDomains.length) rules.push({ type: "field", domain: customDomains, outboundTag: "direct" });
  if (customIps.length) rules.push({ type: "field", ip: customIps, outboundTag: "direct" });

  rules.push({ type: "field", network: "udp", outboundTag: "block" });

  const routing = { domainStrategy: bypass ? "IPIfNonMatch" : "AsIs", rules };
  const outbounds = [
    ...proxies,
    { tag: "direct", protocol: "freedom", settings: {} },
    { tag: "dns-out", protocol: "dns", settings: {} },
    { tag: "block", protocol: "blackhole", settings: { response: { type: "http" } } }
  ];

  if (settings.fragmentEnabled && proxies.some(p => p.streamSettings.security === "tls")) {
    outbounds.push({ tag: "fragment", protocol: "freedom", settings: { fragment: { packets: "tlshello", length: settings.fragmentLength || "100-200", interval: settings.fragmentInterval || "10-20" } } });
  }

  const config = {
    log: { loglevel: normalizeLogLevel(settings.logLevel) },
    dns: { servers: buildXrayDnsServers(settings), queryStrategy: settings.ipv6Enabled === false ? "UseIPv4" : "UseIP" },
    inbounds: [{ tag: "socks-in", listen: "127.0.0.1", port: 10808, protocol: "socks", settings: { auth: "noauth", udp: true }, sniffing: { enabled: true, destOverride: settings.fakeDnsEnabled ? ["http", "tls", "quic", "fakedns"] : ["http", "tls"] } }],
    outbounds, routing
  };
  if (settings.fakeDnsEnabled) {
    config.fakedns = settings.ipv6Enabled === false
      ? { ipPool: "198.18.0.0/15", poolSize: 65535 }
      : [{ ipPool: "198.18.0.0/15", poolSize: 32768 }, { ipPool: "fc00::/18", poolSize: 32768 }];
  }

  if (proxies.length > 1) {
    const outboundTags = proxies.map(proxy => proxy.tag);
    routing.balancers = [{ tag: "proxy-balancer", selector: outboundTags, strategy: { type: "leastPing" } }];
    rules.push({ type: "field", network: "tcp", balancerTag: "proxy-balancer" });
    config.observatory = { subjectSelector: outboundTags, probeUrl: "https://www.gstatic.com/generate_204", probeInterval: "3m" };
  } else if (proxies.length === 1) {
    rules.push({ type: "field", network: "tcp", outboundTag: proxies[0].tag });
  }
  return config;
}

function makeSingBoxConfig(settings, host) {
  const sni = cleanHost(host);
  const endpoints = getEndpoints(settings, sni);
  const outboundsData = generateOutboundsData(settings, endpoints);
  const outbounds = [];
  const tags = [];
  const echConfig = echConfigForOutput(settings);

  const tlsCfg = (isTls) => {
    if (!isTls) return { enabled: false };
    const config = {
      enabled: true, server_name: sni, insecure: false,
      // Sing-box only understands a boolean "fragment" (no length/interval fields);
      // unknown keys would make its strict JSON parser reject the whole profile.
      fragment: settings.fragmentEnabled === true,
      fragment_fallback_delay: "500ms"
    };
    // ECH only with a valid user-supplied ECHConfigList (PEM form is what Sing-box expects).
    if (echConfig) config.ech = { enabled: true, config: echPemLines(echConfig) };
    config.utls = { enabled: true, fingerprint: normalizeFingerprint(settings.fingerprint) };
    return config;
  };

  const transport = (path, exitId = "") => {
    // Exit Node selection is carried in the WS path so the Worker receives ?exit=<id>.
    path = ensureWsPathExitId(path, exitId);
    // Early data travels in the Sec-WebSocket-Protocol header (Xray-compatible),
    // so the path stays clean; max_early_data matches the "?ed=" value in URI/Xray outputs.
    const t = { type: "ws", path, headers: { Host: sni } };
    if (settings.earlyData) { t.max_early_data = EARLY_DATA_SIZE; t.early_data_header_name = "Sec-WebSocket-Protocol"; }
    return t;
  };

  for (const o of outboundsData) {
    tags.push(o.tag);
    if (o.proto === "VLESS") {
      outbounds.push({ type: "vless", tag: o.tag, server: o.address, server_port: o.port, uuid: settings.uuid, tls: tlsCfg(o.isTls), transport: transport(wsPathFor(settings, "vless", false, ""), o.exitId) });
    } else {
      outbounds.push({ type: "trojan", tag: o.tag, server: o.address, server_port: o.port, password: settings.trojanPassword, tls: tlsCfg(o.isTls), transport: transport(wsPathFor(settings, "trojan", false, ""), o.exitId) });
    }
  }
  const ssPassword = String(settings.shadowsocksPassword || settings.uuid || "");
  for (const item of generateShadowsocksOutboundsData(settings, endpoints)) {
    const pluginOpts = [`mode=websocket`, `host=${sni}`, `path=${shadowsocksWsPath(settings, item.exitId)}`];
    if (item.isTls) pluginOpts.push("tls");
    tags.push(item.tag);
    outbounds.push({ type: "shadowsocks", tag: item.tag, server: item.address, server_port: item.port,
      method: settings.shadowsocksMethod || "aes-256-gcm", password: ssPassword,
      plugin: "v2ray-plugin", plugin_opts: pluginOpts.join(";") });
  }

  if (tags.length > 0) {
    outbounds.push({ type: "urltest", tag: "best-ping", outbounds: tags, url: "https://www.gstatic.com/generate_204", interval: "3m", tolerance: 50 });
    outbounds.push({ type: "selector", tag: "proxy", outbounds: ["best-ping", ...tags], default: "best-ping" });
  }
  outbounds.push({ type: "direct", tag: "direct" });
  outbounds.push({ type: "block", tag: "block" });

  const routeRules = [];
  if (settings.bypassIran === true) routeRules.push({ ip_is_private: true, outbound: "direct" }, { domain_suffix: [".ir"], outbound: "direct" });
  const customBypass = parseCustomBypass(settings.customBypass);
  const customDomains = customBypass.filter(x => x.type === "domain").map(x => x.value);
  const customIps = customBypass.filter(x => x.type === "ip").map(x => x.value);
  if (customDomains.length) routeRules.push({ domain_suffix: customDomains, outbound: "direct" });
  if (customIps.length) routeRules.push({ ip_cidr: customIps, outbound: "direct" });
  routeRules.push({ network: ["udp"], outbound: "block" });
  // DNS is hijacked into Sing-box's own resolver instead of being sent direct to the
  // operator network. The hijack rule must be evaluated before every other rule.
  routeRules.unshift({ port: [53], action: "hijack-dns" });

  const dnsServers = [toSingBoxDnsServer(settings.localDns || "localhost", "local"), toSingBoxDnsServer(settings.remoteDns || DEFAULT_REMOTE_DNS, "remote")];
  if (settings.antiSanctionDns) dnsServers.push(toSingBoxDnsServer(settings.antiSanctionDns, "anti-sanction"));
  // Remote DoH servers are reached through the proxy so their queries never leak.
  // (The panel's own domain is resolved via "local" to avoid a bootstrap loop.)
  if (tags.length) for (const server of dnsServers) if (server.tag === "remote" || server.tag === "anti-sanction") if (server.type !== "local") server.detour = "proxy";
  if (settings.fakeDnsEnabled) dnsServers.push({ type: "fakeip", tag: "fakeip", inet4_range: "198.18.0.0/15", ...(settings.ipv6Enabled === false ? {} : { inet6_range: "fc00::/18" }) });
  const dnsRules = [];
  if (sni && !isIPv4Text(sni) && !isIPv6Text(sni)) dnsRules.push({ domain: [sni], server: "local" });
  if (settings.bypassIran) dnsRules.push({ domain_suffix: [".ir"], server: "local" });
  const dnsFinal = settings.fakeDnsEnabled ? "fakeip" : (settings.antiSanctionDns ? "anti-sanction" : "remote");

  return {
    log: { level: logLevelForSingBox(settings.logLevel), timestamp: true, ...(normalizeLogLevel(settings.logLevel) === "none" ? { disabled: true } : {}) },
    dns: { servers: dnsServers, ...(dnsRules.length ? { rules: dnsRules } : {}), final: dnsFinal, strategy: settings.ipv6Enabled === false ? "ipv4_only" : "prefer_ipv4" },
    inbounds: [{ type: "mixed", tag: "mixed-in", listen: "127.0.0.1", listen_port: 2080 }],
    outbounds, route: { final: tags.length ? "proxy" : "direct", auto_detect_interface: true, default_domain_resolver: "local", rules: routeRules }
  };
}

function uriHost(host) { return host.includes(":") && !host.startsWith("[") ? "[" + host + "]" : host; }
function cleanHost(value) { return String(value || "").trim().replace(/^\[|\]$/g, ""); }

// -------------------- Multi-user & Tunnel Handling --------------------
function getCredentialIndex(settings) {
  const users = Array.isArray(settings.users) ? settings.users : [];
  if (credentialIndexCache && credentialIndexCache.users === users && credentialIndexCache.revision === panelSettingsRevision) return credentialIndexCache;
  const vless = new Map(), trojan = new Map(), sub = new Map();
  for (const user of users) {
    if (!user || user.enabled === false) continue;
    if (user.vlessEnabled !== false && user.uuid) vless.set(String(user.uuid).toLowerCase(), user);
    if (user.trojanEnabled !== false && user.trojanPassword) trojan.set(sha224Hex(user.trojanPassword).toLowerCase(), user);
    if (user.subToken) sub.set(String(user.subToken), user);
  }
  credentialIndexCache = { users, revision: panelSettingsRevision, vless, trojan, sub };
  return credentialIndexCache;
}

function findUserByCredential(settings, kind, value) {
  if (!value) return null;
  const normalized = String(value).trim();
  if (kind === "vless" && isCredentialRevoked("vless", normalized)) return null;
  if (kind === "trojanHash" && isCredentialRevoked("trojanHash", normalized)) return null;
  if (kind === "sub" && isCredentialRevoked("sub", normalized)) return null;
  const index = getCredentialIndex(settings);
  const match = kind === "vless" ? index.vless.get(normalized.toLowerCase())
    : kind === "trojanHash" ? index.trojan.get(normalized.toLowerCase())
    : kind === "sub" ? index.sub.get(normalized) : null;
  if (match) return match;
  if (kind === "vless" && settings.vlessEnabled && settings.uuid && constantTimeEqual(String(settings.uuid).toLowerCase(), normalized.toLowerCase())) return { ...settings, id: "default", name: settings.username || "default", vlessEnabled: true, trojanEnabled: false };
  if (kind === "trojanHash" && settings.trojanEnabled && settings.trojanPassword && constantTimeEqual(sha224Hex(settings.trojanPassword), normalized.toLowerCase())) return { ...settings, id: "default", name: settings.username || "default", vlessEnabled: false, trojanEnabled: true };
  if (kind === "sub" && settings.subToken && constantTimeEqual(settings.subToken, normalized)) return { ...settings, id: "default", name: settings.username || "default" };
  return null;
}

function userScopedSettings(settings, user) {
  if (!user || user.id === "default") return settings;
  return {
    ...settings,
    username: user.name || "user",
    uuid: user.vlessEnabled !== false ? user.uuid : "",
    trojanPassword: user.trojanEnabled !== false ? user.trojanPassword : "",
    subToken: user.subToken || "",
    shadowsocksPassword: user.shadowsocksPassword || user.uuid || "",
    vlessEnabled: settings.vlessEnabled !== false && user.vlessEnabled !== false && !!user.uuid,
    trojanEnabled: settings.trojanEnabled !== false && user.trojanEnabled !== false && !!user.trojanPassword,
    users: []
  };
}

function isUserSubscriptionAvailable(user, usage) {
  if (!user || user.enabled === false) return false;
  const now = Date.now();
  if (usage?.expiresAt && now >= usage.expiresAt) return false;
  const resetMs = Math.max(0, Number(user.resetPeriodDays) || 0) * 86400000;
  const periodExpired = resetMs > 0 && usage?.periodStartedAt && now - usage.periodStartedAt >= resetMs;
  const usageBytes = periodExpired ? 0 : Number(usage?.usageBytes || 0);
  const requestCount = periodExpired ? 0 : Number(usage?.requestCount || 0);
  const maxBytes = Number(user.trafficLimitGB) > 0 ? Number(user.trafficLimitGB) * 1024 * 1024 * 1024 : 0;
  const maxRequests = Math.max(0, Number(user.requestLimit) || 0);
  return !(maxBytes > 0 && usageBytes >= maxBytes) && !(maxRequests > 0 && requestCount >= maxRequests);
}

// Shadowsocks AEAD (legacy aes-128-gcm/aes-256-gcm) over the Worker WebSocket
// transport. Master-key derivation follows EVP_BytesToKey(MD5); per-connection
// subkeys follow HKDF-SHA1(salt, master_key, "ss-subkey"). SIP022 2022 ciphers
// are intentionally not advertised because they require BLAKE3 and different keying.
const SS_MD5_SHIFTS = [7,12,17,22,5,9,14,20,4,11,16,23,6,10,15,21];
const SS_MD5_K = Uint32Array.from({ length: 64 }, (_, i) => Math.floor(Math.abs(Math.sin(i + 1)) * 0x100000000) >>> 0);

function md5Bytes(input) {
  const bytes = input instanceof Uint8Array ? input : new Uint8Array(input);
  const paddedLength = Math.ceil((bytes.length + 9) / 64) * 64;
  const padded = new Uint8Array(paddedLength);
  padded.set(bytes); padded[bytes.length] = 0x80;
  const view = new DataView(padded.buffer);
  const bitLength = bytes.length * 8;
  view.setUint32(paddedLength - 8, bitLength >>> 0, true);
  view.setUint32(paddedLength - 4, Math.floor(bitLength / 0x100000000), true);
  let a0 = 0x67452301, b0 = 0xefcdab89, c0 = 0x98badcfe, d0 = 0x10325476;
  const words = new Uint32Array(16);
  const rotate = (x, n) => (x << n) | (x >>> (32 - n));
  for (let offset = 0; offset < paddedLength; offset += 64) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) words[i] = view.getUint32(offset + i * 4, true);
    let a = a0, b = b0, c = c0, d = d0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      let f, g, shift;
      if (i < 16) { f = (b & c) | (~b & d); g = i; shift = SS_MD5_SHIFTS[i % 4]; }
      else if (i < 32) { f = (d & b) | (~d & c); g = (5 * i + 1) % 16; shift = SS_MD5_SHIFTS[4 + (i % 4)]; }
      else if (i < 48) { f = b ^ c ^ d; g = (3 * i + 5) % 16; shift = SS_MD5_SHIFTS[8 + (i % 4)]; }
      else { f = c ^ (b | ~d); g = (7 * i) % 16; shift = SS_MD5_SHIFTS[12 + (i % 4)]; }
      const next = (b + rotate((a + f + SS_MD5_K[i] + words[g]) >>> 0, shift)) >>> 0;
      a = d; d = c; c = b; b = next;
    }
    a0 = (a0 + a) >>> 0; b0 = (b0 + b) >>> 0; c0 = (c0 + c) >>> 0; d0 = (d0 + d) >>> 0;
  }
  const out = new Uint8Array(16), outView = new DataView(out.buffer);
  outView.setUint32(0, a0, true); outView.setUint32(4, b0, true);
  outView.setUint32(8, c0, true); outView.setUint32(12, d0, true);
  return out;
}

function evpBytesToKey(password, keyLength) {
  const pass = enc.encode(String(password));
  const blocks = [];
  let previous = new Uint8Array(0), total = 0;
  while (total < keyLength) {
    previous = md5Bytes(concatChunks([previous, pass]));
    blocks.push(previous); total += previous.length;
  }
  return concatChunks(blocks).slice(0, keyLength);
}

function getShadowsocksMasterKey(password, method) {
  const keyLength = method === "aes-128-gcm" ? 16 : method === "aes-256-gcm" ? 32 : 0;
  if (!keyLength) throw new Error("Unsupported Shadowsocks AEAD method");
  const cacheKey = method + "|" + String(password);
  const cached = SS_MASTER_KEY_CACHE.get(cacheKey);
  if (cached) return cached;
  const key = evpBytesToKey(password, keyLength);
  SS_MASTER_KEY_CACHE.set(cacheKey, key);
  while (SS_MASTER_KEY_CACHE.size > SS_MASTER_KEY_CACHE_MAX) SS_MASTER_KEY_CACHE.delete(SS_MASTER_KEY_CACHE.keys().next().value);
  return key;
}

async function deriveShadowsocksSubkey(password, method, salt) {
  const masterKey = getShadowsocksMasterKey(password, method);
  const hkdfKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", masterKey, "HKDF", false, ["deriveBits"]);
  const bits = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits({
    name: "HKDF", hash: "SHA-1", salt, info: enc.encode("ss-subkey")
  }, hkdfKey, masterKey.length * 8);
  return crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", bits, { name: "AES-GCM" }, false, ["encrypt", "decrypt"]);
}

function incrementShadowsocksNonce(nonce) {
  for (let i = 0; i < nonce.length; i++) {
    nonce[i] = (nonce[i] + 1) & 255;
    if (nonce[i] !== 0) break;
  }
}

async function shadowsocksEncrypt(key, nonce, plaintext) {
  return new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name: "AES-GCM", iv: nonce, tagLength: 128 }, key, plaintext));
}
async function shadowsocksDecrypt(key, nonce, ciphertext) {
  return new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.decrypt({ name: "AES-GCM", iv: nonce, tagLength: 128 }, key, ciphertext));
}

function parseShadowsocksAddress(bytes) {
  if (!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array)) throw new Error("invalid Shadowsocks destination address");
  if (bytes.length < 1) return { incomplete: true };
  let p = 1;
  const type = bytes[0];
  let host = "";
  if (type === 1) {
    if (bytes.length < p + 4) return { incomplete: true };
    host = Array.from(bytes.subarray(p, p + 4)).join(".");
    if (!isIPv4Text(host)) throw new Error("invalid Shadowsocks IPv4 address");
    p += 4;
  } else if (type === 3) {
    if (bytes.length < p + 1) return { incomplete: true };
    const length = bytes[p++];
    if (length < 1) throw new Error("invalid Shadowsocks domain length");
    if (bytes.length < p + length) return { incomplete: true };
    host = dec.decode(bytes.subarray(p, p + length));
    if (!isDomainName(host) && !isIPv4Text(host)) throw new Error("invalid Shadowsocks domain address");
    p += length;
  } else if (type === 4) {
    if (bytes.length < p + 16) return { incomplete: true };
    const groups = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i += 2) groups.push(((bytes[p + i] << 8) | bytes[p + i + 1]).toString(16));
    host = groups.join(":");
    if (!isIPv6Text(host)) throw new Error("invalid Shadowsocks IPv6 address");
    p += 16;
  } else {
    throw new Error("unsupported Shadowsocks address type");
  }
  if (bytes.length < p + 2) return { incomplete: true };
  const port = (bytes[p] << 8) | bytes[p + 1];
  if (!port) throw new Error("invalid Shadowsocks destination port");
  return { host, port, data: bytes.slice(p + 2), headerLength: p + 2 };
}

async function encryptShadowsocksStreamChunk(key, nonce, payload) {
  const length = new Uint8Array([(payload.length >> 8) & 255, payload.length & 255]);
  const encryptedLength = await shadowsocksEncrypt(key, nonce, length);
  incrementShadowsocksNonce(nonce);
  const encryptedPayload = await shadowsocksEncrypt(key, nonce, payload);
  incrementShadowsocksNonce(nonce);
  return concatChunks([encryptedLength, encryptedPayload]);
}

async function sendShadowsocksEncrypted(ws, key, nonce, data) {
  if (!data || !data.length || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;
  const frames = [];
  for (let offset = 0; offset < data.length; offset += 0x3fff) {
    const chunk = data.subarray(offset, Math.min(data.length, offset + 0x3fff));
    frames.push(await encryptShadowsocksStreamChunk(key, nonce, chunk));
  }
  if (frames.length && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) ws.send(concatChunks(frames));
}

async function handleShadowsocksTunnel(request, env, kv, subscriptionToken, ctx, preferredExitId = "") {
  if (request.method !== "GET" || request.headers.get("Upgrade")?.toLowerCase() !== "websocket") return fakeLandingResponse();
  const settings = await loadSettings(kv, env);
