  if (settings.shadowsocksEnabled !== true) return new Response("Shadowsocks disabled", { status: 404 });
  const candidate = findUserByCredential(settings, "sub", subscriptionToken);
  if (!candidate) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const userPassword = String(candidate.shadowsocksPassword || candidate.uuid || settings.shadowsocksPassword || settings.uuid || "");
  const method = SHADOWSOCKS_METHODS.has(String(settings.shadowsocksMethod || "aes-256-gcm")) ? settings.shadowsocksMethod : "aes-256-gcm";
  const keyLength = method === "aes-128-gcm" ? 16 : 32;
  if (!userPassword) return new Response("Shadowsocks credential missing", { status: 503 });

  const pair = new WebSocketPair();
  const client = pair[0], server = pair[1];
  try { server.binaryType = "arraybuffer"; } catch {}
  server.accept({ allowHalfOpen: true });
  let finished = false, initialized = false, sessionStarted = false;
  let activeUser = null, activeSettings = settings, userUsageAtStart = null, userTrafficLedger = null;
  let userSessionBytes = 0, userSessionPersistedBytes = 0, userUsageFlushTimer = null;
  let userTrafficPersistChain = Promise.resolve(), remote = null, remoteWriter = null;
  const userSessionDailyBytes = new Map();
  const inputState = { saltReady: false, cipherKey: null, nonce: new Uint8Array(12), expectedLength: null };
  let pendingShadowsocksAddress = new Uint8Array(0);
  const encryptedInputQueue = createByteQueue(256 * 1024, 16 * 1024);
  let ssUpstreamQueue = null;
  let ssDownstreamSender = null;
  let responseCipherKey = null, responseNonce = new Uint8Array(12);

  const queueUserTrafficFlush = (force = false) => {
    if (!activeUser || !kv || activeSettings.trafficByteTrackingEnabled !== true) return userTrafficPersistChain;
    const pending = Math.max(0, userSessionBytes - userSessionPersistedBytes);
    if (!pending || (!force && pending < USER_TRAFFIC_FLUSH_BYTES)) return userTrafficPersistChain;
    const id = String(activeUser.id || "default");
    const deltas = Object.fromEntries(userSessionDailyBytes.entries());
    userSessionDailyBytes.clear();
    userSessionPersistedBytes += pending;
    userTrafficPersistChain = enqueueTrafficWrite(kv, id, pending, deltas);
    if (ctx && typeof ctx.waitUntil === "function") ctx.waitUntil(userTrafficPersistChain);
    return userTrafficPersistChain;
  };

  const releaseUserSession = () => {
    if (!activeUser) return Promise.resolve();
    if (userUsageFlushTimer) { clearInterval(userUsageFlushTimer); userUsageFlushTimer = null; }
    const id = String(activeUser.id || "default");
    unregisterUserConnection(id, closeAll);
    const count = userActiveConnections.get(id) || 0;
    if (count <= 1) userActiveConnections.delete(id); else userActiveConnections.set(id, count - 1);
    if (userSessionBytes > 0 && kv) return queueUserTrafficFlush(true);
    return Promise.resolve();
  };

  let shutdownPromise = null;
  const closeAll = () => {
    if (finished) return shutdownPromise || userTrafficPersistChain;
    finished = true;
    if (ssDownstreamSender) ssDownstreamSender.cancel();
    shutdownPromise = Promise.resolve().then(async () => {
      if (ssUpstreamQueue) {
        try { await ssUpstreamQueue.drain(); } catch {}
        ssUpstreamQueue.cancel();
      }
      try { if (remoteWriter) await remoteWriter.close(); } catch {}
      try { remote?.close?.(); } catch {}
      return releaseUserSession();
    });
    try { server.close(); } catch {}
    return shutdownPromise;
  };

  const recordTraffic = amount => {
    if (activeSettings.trafficByteTrackingEnabled !== true) return true;
    if (!activeUser || !userUsageAtStart || !userTrafficLedger || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) return true;
    const current = Math.floor(amount);
    const limitBytes = activeUser.id === "default" ? 0 : (Number(activeUser.trafficLimitGB) > 0 ? Number(activeUser.trafficLimitGB) * 1024 ** 3 : 0);
    const remaining = limitBytes > 0 ? Math.max(0, limitBytes - Number(userTrafficLedger.usageBytes || 0)) : current;
    const accepted = Math.min(current, remaining);
    if (accepted > 0) {
      userTrafficLedger.usageBytes += accepted; userSessionBytes += accepted;
      const day = utcUsageDayKey(); userSessionDailyBytes.set(day, (userSessionDailyBytes.get(day) || 0) + accepted);
      void queueUserTrafficFlush(false);
    }
    const exhausted = limitBytes > 0 && (current > remaining || userTrafficLedger.usageBytes >= limitBytes);
    if (exhausted) { userTrafficLedger.usageBytes = Math.min(limitBytes, userTrafficLedger.usageBytes); void closeAll(); return false; }
    return true;
  };

  const startRemotePump = async readable => {
    const reader = createWorkerReadableReader(readable, 64 * 1024);
    ssDownstreamSender = createShadowsocksDownstreamSender(server, responseCipherKey, responseNonce, () => finished);
    try {
      while (!finished) {
        const item = await reader.read();
        if (item.done) break;
        if (!item.value?.byteLength) continue;
        if (!recordTraffic(item.value.byteLength)) return;
        await ssDownstreamSender.enqueue(item.value);
      }
      if (!finished) await ssDownstreamSender.flush();
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn("Shadowsocks remote stream ended:", String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 100));
    } finally { try { reader.releaseLock(); } catch {} }
    if (!finished) await closeAll();
  };

  const processEncryptedInput = async bytes => {
    if (finished || !bytes?.length) return;
    if (!sessionStarted) {
      const session = await beginUserSession(kv, candidate, ctx);
      activeUser = session.user; userUsageAtStart = session.usage; userTrafficLedger = session.trafficState || null;
      activeSettings = userScopedSettings(settings, activeUser);
      if (activeSettings.shadowsocksEnabled !== true) throw new Error("Shadowsocks is disabled for this account");
      if (activeSettings.trafficByteTrackingEnabled === true) userUsageFlushTimer = setInterval(() => { void queueUserTrafficFlush(true); }, USER_TRAFFIC_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS);
      registerUserConnection(activeUser.id || "default", closeAll);
      sessionStarted = true;
    }
    encryptedInputQueue.append(bytes);

    if (!inputState.saltReady) {
      if (encryptedInputQueue.available < keyLength) return;
      const salt = encryptedInputQueue.take(keyLength);
      inputState.cipherKey = await deriveShadowsocksSubkey(userPassword, method, salt);
      inputState.nonce = new Uint8Array(12);
      inputState.saltReady = true;
    }

    while (!finished) {
      if (inputState.expectedLength === null) {
        if (encryptedInputQueue.available < 18) return;
        const encryptedLength = encryptedInputQueue.take(18);
        const plainLength = await shadowsocksDecrypt(inputState.cipherKey, inputState.nonce, encryptedLength);
        incrementShadowsocksNonce(inputState.nonce);
        if (plainLength.length !== 2) throw new Error("invalid Shadowsocks encrypted length");
        inputState.expectedLength = (plainLength[0] << 8) | plainLength[1];
        if (inputState.expectedLength < 1 || inputState.expectedLength > 0x3fff) throw new Error("Shadowsocks chunk size is invalid");
      }
      const frameLength = inputState.expectedLength + 16;
      if (encryptedInputQueue.available < frameLength) return;
      const encryptedPayload = encryptedInputQueue.take(frameLength);
      const payload = await shadowsocksDecrypt(inputState.cipherKey, inputState.nonce, encryptedPayload);
      incrementShadowsocksNonce(inputState.nonce);
      inputState.expectedLength = null;
      if (payload.length === 0 || payload.length > 0x3fff) throw new Error("invalid Shadowsocks payload length");
      if (!recordTraffic(payload.length)) return;

      if (!initialized) {
        pendingShadowsocksAddress = concatChunks([pendingShadowsocksAddress, payload]);
        const destination = parseShadowsocksAddress(pendingShadowsocksAddress);
        if (destination.incomplete) {
          if (pendingShadowsocksAddress.length > 259) throw new Error("Shadowsocks destination header is too large");
          continue;
        }
        pendingShadowsocksAddress = new Uint8Array(0);
        if (!isAllowedDestinationPort(destination.port, env)) throw new Error("port not allowed");
        remote = await openOutbound(activeSettings, destination.host, destination.port, destination.data, -1, preferredExitId);
        if (finished) { try { remote.close(); } catch {} return; }
        remoteWriter = remote.writable.getWriter();
        ssUpstreamQueue = createChunkCoalescer(bytes => remoteWriter.write(bytes), {
          targetBytes: UPSTREAM_BUNDLE_TARGET_BYTES, maxBytes: UPSTREAM_BUNDLE_MAX_BYTES,
          delayMs: UPSTREAM_BUNDLE_DELAY_MS, onError: () => { void closeAll(); }
        });
        const responseSalt = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(keyLength));
        responseCipherKey = await deriveShadowsocksSubkey(userPassword, method, responseSalt);
        responseNonce = new Uint8Array(12);
        server.send(responseSalt);
        initialized = true;
        void startRemotePump(remote.readable);
      } else {
        if (!remoteWriter) throw new Error("Shadowsocks remote stream is not ready");
        await ssUpstreamQueue.enqueue(payload);
      }
    }
  };

  let inboundQueue = Promise.resolve();
  server.addEventListener("message", event => {
    inboundQueue = inboundQueue.then(async () => {
      if (!finished) await processEncryptedInput(await toBytes(event.data));
    }).catch(error => {
      console.warn("Shadowsocks tunnel closed:", String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 140));
      void closeAll();
    });
  });
  server.addEventListener("close", closeAll);
  server.addEventListener("error", closeAll);
  return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
}

async function handleTunnel(request, env, kv, kind, ctx, preferredExitId = "") {
  if (request.headers.get("Upgrade")?.toLowerCase() !== "websocket") return fakeLandingResponse();
  const settings = await loadSettings(kv, env);
  if (kind === "vless" && !settings.vlessEnabled) return new Response("VLESS disabled", { status: 404 });
  if (kind === "trojan" && !settings.trojanEnabled) return new Response("Trojan disabled", { status: 404 });

  const earlyDataHeader = settings.earlyData ? request.headers.get("sec-websocket-protocol") : null;
  const earlyBytes = decodeEarlyData(earlyDataHeader);
  const earlyDataProtocol = getEarlyDataProtocolForResponse(earlyDataHeader, earlyBytes);
  const pair = new WebSocketPair();
  const client = pair[0], server = pair[1];
  try { server.binaryType = "arraybuffer"; } catch {}
  server.accept({ allowHalfOpen: true });

  let initialized = false, finished = false, inboundQueue = Promise.resolve();
  let udp = null, dest = null, remote = null, remoteWriter = null, generation = 0;
  let upstreamQueue = null;
  let receivedAny = false, switching = null;
  let initialWritesBuffer = [], initialWritesSize = 0, bufferOverflow = false;
  let activeUser = null, userUsageAtStart = null, userTrafficLedger = null, userSessionBytes = 0, userSessionPersistedBytes = 0, sessionReleased = false;
  let activeSettings = settings, releasePromise = null, userTrafficPersistChain = Promise.resolve();
  let userUsageFlushTimer = null;
  const userSessionDailyBytes = new Map();

  const queueUserTrafficFlush = (force = false) => {
    if (!activeUser || !kv || activeSettings.trafficByteTrackingEnabled !== true) return userTrafficPersistChain;
    const pending = Math.max(0, userSessionBytes - userSessionPersistedBytes);
    if (!pending || (!force && pending < USER_TRAFFIC_FLUSH_BYTES)) return userTrafficPersistChain;
    const id = String(activeUser.id);
    const dailyDeltas = Object.fromEntries(userSessionDailyBytes.entries());
    userSessionDailyBytes.clear();
    userSessionPersistedBytes += pending;
    // Bytes are handed to the shared per-account queue, which coalesces concurrent
    // tunnels and paces KV writes; failures stay buffered there, so no rollback is needed here.
    userTrafficPersistChain = enqueueTrafficWrite(kv, id, pending, dailyDeltas);
    if (ctx && typeof ctx.waitUntil === "function") ctx.waitUntil(userTrafficPersistChain);
    return userTrafficPersistChain;
  };

  const releaseUserSession = () => {
    if (sessionReleased) return releasePromise || Promise.resolve();
    sessionReleased = true;
    if (userUsageFlushTimer) { clearInterval(userUsageFlushTimer); userUsageFlushTimer = null; }
    if (!activeUser) return Promise.resolve();
    const id = String(activeUser.id || "default");
    unregisterUserConnection(id, closeAll);
    const count = userActiveConnections.get(id) || 0;
    if (count <= 1) userActiveConnections.delete(id); else userActiveConnections.set(id, count - 1);
    if (userSessionBytes > 0 && kv) releasePromise = queueUserTrafficFlush(true);
    return releasePromise || Promise.resolve();
  };

  let shutdownPromise = null;
  const closeAll = () => {
    if (finished) return shutdownPromise || releasePromise || Promise.resolve();
    finished = true;
    initialWritesBuffer = [];
    shutdownPromise = Promise.resolve().then(async () => {
      if (upstreamQueue) {
        try { await upstreamQueue.drain(); } catch {}
        upstreamQueue.cancel();
      }
      try { if (remoteWriter) await remoteWriter.close(); } catch {}
      try { if (remote && remote.close) remote.close(); } catch {}
      return releaseUserSession();
    });
    try { server.close(); } catch {}
    return shutdownPromise;
  };

  const recordTraffic = amount => {
    // Byte tracking is off by default: no byte counting / KV flush, the session itself still counts.
    if (activeSettings.trafficByteTrackingEnabled !== true) return true;
    if (!activeUser || !userUsageAtStart || !userTrafficLedger || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) return true;
    const current = Math.floor(amount);
    const limitBytes = activeUser.id === "default" ? 0 : (Number(activeUser.trafficLimitGB) > 0 ? Number(activeUser.trafficLimitGB) * 1024 * 1024 * 1024 : 0);
    const remaining = limitBytes > 0 ? Math.max(0, limitBytes - Number(userTrafficLedger.usageBytes || 0)) : current;
    const accepted = Math.min(current, remaining);
    if (accepted > 0) {
      userTrafficLedger.usageBytes += accepted;
      userSessionBytes += accepted;
      const day = utcUsageDayKey();
      userSessionDailyBytes.set(day, (userSessionDailyBytes.get(day) || 0) + accepted);
      void queueUserTrafficFlush(false);
    }
    const exhausted = limitBytes > 0 && (current > remaining || userTrafficLedger.usageBytes >= limitBytes);
    if (exhausted) {
      userTrafficLedger.usageBytes = Math.min(limitBytes, userTrafficLedger.usageBytes);
      void closeUserConnections(activeUser.id);
      void closeAll();
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  };

  const rememberWrite = bytes => {
    if (receivedAny || bufferOverflow || !bytes || !bytes.length) return;
    if (initialWritesSize + bytes.length > MAX_INITIAL_BUFFER) { bufferOverflow = true; initialWritesBuffer = []; initialWritesSize = 0; return; }
    initialWritesBuffer.push(bytes); initialWritesSize += bytes.length;
  };

  const routesAfterCurrent = () => remote && Number.isInteger(remote.routeIndex) && remote.routeIndex < buildOutboundRoutes(activeSettings, preferredExitId).length - 1;
  const canFallback = () => !finished && !!dest && !receivedAny && !bufferOverflow && routesAfterCurrent();

  const startFallback = () => {
    if (switching) return switching;
    if (!canFallback()) return Promise.resolve(false);
    const job = (async () => {
      let next = null;
      try {
        const sentCount = initialWritesBuffer.length;
        next = await openOutbound(activeSettings, dest.host, dest.port, concatChunks(initialWritesBuffer), remote.routeIndex, preferredExitId);
        if (finished) { try { next.close(); } catch {} return false; }
        const nextWriter = next.writable.getWriter();
        for (let i = sentCount; i < initialWritesBuffer.length; i++) {
          if (bufferOverflow) throw new Error("overflow");
          await nextWriter.write(initialWritesBuffer[i]);
        }
        const previous = remote, previousWriter = remoteWriter;
        remote = next; remoteWriter = nextWriter; switching = null; generation++;
        try { if (previousWriter) previousWriter.close().catch(() => {}); } catch {}
        try { if (previous && previous.close) previous.close(); } catch {}
        void pumpRemote(next.readable, server, hooks, generation);
        return true;
      } catch { try { if (next && next.close) next.close(); } catch {} return false; }
    })();
    switching = job;
    job.finally(() => { if (switching === job) switching = null; });
    return job;
  };

  const hooks = {
    isStale: gen => gen !== generation,
    onFirstByte: () => { receivedAny = true; initialWritesBuffer = []; initialWritesSize = 0; },
    onRemoteBytes: amount => recordTraffic(amount),
    fallback: async gen => (gen !== generation) || (await startFallback()),
    closeAll
  };

  const writeRemoteRaw = async bytes => {
    if (switching) await switching;
    try { await remoteWriter.write(bytes); }
    catch (err) { if (canFallback() && await startFallback()) return; throw err; }
  };
  upstreamQueue = createChunkCoalescer(writeRemoteRaw, {
    targetBytes: UPSTREAM_BUNDLE_TARGET_BYTES,
    maxBytes: UPSTREAM_BUNDLE_MAX_BYTES,
    delayMs: UPSTREAM_BUNDLE_DELAY_MS,
    onError: () => { void closeAll(); }
  });
  const writeRemote = bytes => {
    rememberWrite(bytes);
    return upstreamQueue.enqueue(bytes);
  };

  const startTcp = async parsed => {
    if (!isAllowedDestinationPort(parsed.port, env)) throw new Error("port not allowed");
    dest = { host: parsed.host, port: parsed.port };
    rememberWrite(parsed.data);
    // Acknowledge the VLESS request right away so the client is not left waiting
    // (and the socket does not stall) while the outbound connection is opened.
    if (kind === "vless" && !finished) server.send(new Uint8Array([parsed.version, 0]));
    const r = await openOutbound(activeSettings, parsed.host, parsed.port, parsed.data, -1, preferredExitId);
    if (finished) { try { r.close(); } catch {} return; }
    remote = r; remoteWriter = r.writable.getWriter();
    generation++;
    void pumpRemote(r.readable, server, hooks, generation);
  };

  const startUdp = parsed => {
    udp = {
      mode: kind, pending: new Uint8Array(0),
      header: kind === "vless" ? new Uint8Array([parsed.version, 0]) : null,
      inflight: 0, onTraffic: recordTraffic
    };
    if (parsed.data.length) {
      if (kind === "trojan") handleTrojanUdpDnsPackets(parsed.data, udp, activeSettings.dohUrl, server);
      else handleUdpDnsPackets(parsed.data, udp, activeSettings.dohUrl, server);
    }
  };

  const processInbound = async bytes => {
    if (finished) return;
    if (!initialized) {
      const parsed = kind === "vless" ? parseVlessRequest(bytes) : parseTrojanRequest(bytes);
      const authSettings = await loadSettings(kv, env);
      const candidate = kind === "vless"
        ? findUserByCredential(authSettings, "vless", parsed.uuid)
        : findUserByCredential(authSettings, "trojanHash", parsed.passwordHash);
      if (!candidate) throw new Error("auth");
      const session = await beginUserSession(kv, candidate, ctx);
      activeUser = session.user;
      userUsageAtStart = session.usage;
      userTrafficLedger = session.trafficState || null;
      activeSettings = userScopedSettings(authSettings, activeUser);
      if (activeSettings.trafficByteTrackingEnabled === true) {
        userUsageFlushTimer = setInterval(() => { void queueUserTrafficFlush(true); }, USER_TRAFFIC_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS);
      }
      registerUserConnection(activeUser.id || "default", closeAll);
      if (kind === "vless" && !activeSettings.vlessEnabled) throw new Error("VLESS is disabled for this user");
      if (kind === "trojan" && !activeSettings.trojanEnabled) throw new Error("Trojan is disabled for this user");
      if (!recordTraffic(bytes.byteLength)) return;
      initialized = true;
      if (parsed.udp) startUdp(parsed); else await startTcp(parsed);
    } else {
      if (!recordTraffic(bytes.byteLength)) return;
      if (udp) {
        if (udp.mode === "trojan") handleTrojanUdpDnsPackets(bytes, udp, activeSettings.dohUrl, server);
        else handleUdpDnsPackets(bytes, udp, activeSettings.dohUrl, server);
      } else {
        if (!remoteWriter) throw new Error("not ready");
        await writeRemote(bytes);
      }
    }
  };

  if (earlyBytes && earlyBytes.length) inboundQueue = inboundQueue.then(() => processInbound(earlyBytes)).catch(closeAll);
  server.addEventListener("message", ev => {
    inboundQueue = inboundQueue.then(async () => { if (!finished) await processInbound(await toBytes(ev.data)); }).catch(closeAll);
  });
  server.addEventListener("close", closeAll);
  server.addEventListener("error", closeAll);
  return new Response(null, {
    status: 101, webSocket: client,
    ...(earlyDataProtocol ? { headers: { "Sec-WebSocket-Protocol": earlyDataProtocol } } : {})
  });
}

function getEarlyDataProtocolForResponse(header, earlyBytes) {
  if (!header || !earlyBytes || !earlyBytes.length || earlyBytes.length > EARLY_DATA_SIZE) return null;
  if (String(header).length > MAX_EARLY_HEADER) return null;
  // The client may list several sub-protocols; the first one carries the early data and
  // is the only one the server may select in its 101 response.
  const value = String(header).split(",")[0].trim();
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(value)) return null;
  return value;
}

function decodeEarlyData(header) {
  if (!header || header.length > MAX_EARLY_HEADER) return null;
  const token = header.split(",")[0].trim();
  if (!token || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+={0,2}$/.test(token)) return null;
  try {
    const b64 = token.replace(/=+$/, "").replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/");
    const bin = atob(b64 + "=".repeat((4 - (b64.length % 4)) % 4));
    const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length); for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
    return out.length ? out : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}

function concatChunks(chunks) {
  if (!chunks || !chunks.length) return new Uint8Array(0);
  if (chunks.length === 1) return chunks[0];
  const out = new Uint8Array(chunks.reduce((s, c) => s + c.length, 0));
  let p = 0; for (const c of chunks) { out.set(c, p); p += c.length; }
  return out;
}

// Bounded, timer-assisted coalescing. It limits queue growth and preserves stream order.
function createChunkCoalescer(write, options = {}) {
  const targetBytes = Math.max(1, Number(options.targetBytes) || UPSTREAM_BUNDLE_TARGET_BYTES);
  const maxBytes = Math.max(targetBytes, Number(options.maxBytes) || UPSTREAM_BUNDLE_MAX_BYTES);
  const batchLimit = Math.min(targetBytes, maxBytes);
  const delayMs = Math.max(1, Number(options.delayMs) || UPSTREAM_BUNDLE_DELAY_MS);
  const onError = typeof options.onError === "function" ? options.onError : null;
  let chunks = [], size = 0, timer = null, tail = Promise.resolve(), closed = false;
  let failure = null;
  const clearTimer = () => { if (timer) { clearTimeout(timer); timer = null; } };
  const flush = () => {
    clearTimer();
    if (failure) return Promise.reject(failure);
    if (!size) return tail;
    const batch = concatChunks(chunks);
    chunks = []; size = 0;
    const job = tail.then(() => {
      if (closed) throw new Error("stream queue closed");
      return write(batch);
    });
    tail = job.catch(error => {
      failure = error;
      if (onError) { try { onError(error); } catch {} }
    });
    return job;
  };
  const enqueue = async chunk => {
    if (closed) throw new Error("stream queue closed");
    if (failure) throw failure;
    if (!chunk || !chunk.byteLength) return;
    // Split oversized frames so the queue itself never retains an unbounded
    // single WebSocket message while waiting for the timer/remote writer.
    let offset = 0;
    while (offset < chunk.byteLength) {
      if (closed) throw new Error("stream queue closed");
      if (failure) throw failure;
      const room = Math.max(1, batchLimit - size);
      const count = Math.min(room, chunk.byteLength - offset);
      chunks.push(chunk.subarray(offset, offset + count));
      size += count; offset += count;
      if (size >= batchLimit) await flush();
    }
    if (size && !timer) timer = setTimeout(() => { timer = null; void flush().catch(() => {}); }, delayMs);
  };
  const drain = async () => { await flush(); await tail; if (failure) throw failure; };
  const cancel = () => { closed = true; clearTimer(); chunks = []; size = 0; };
  return { enqueue, flush, drain, cancel, get pendingBytes() { return size; } };
}

// Compactable byte queue: avoids concatenating and slicing the entire Shadowsocks
// handshake buffer for every WebSocket message. Memory grows only when needed.
function createByteQueue(maxCapacity = 256 * 1024, initialCapacity = 16 * 1024) {
  const max = Math.max(1, Number(maxCapacity) || 256 * 1024);
  let buffer = new Uint8Array(Math.min(max, Math.max(1024, initialCapacity)));
  let head = 0, tail = 0;
  const available = () => tail - head;
  const append = chunk => {
    if (!chunk || !chunk.byteLength) return;
    const current = available();
    if (current + chunk.byteLength > max) throw new Error("Shadowsocks input buffer limit exceeded");
    if (buffer.length - tail < chunk.byteLength && head > 0) {
      buffer.copyWithin(0, head, tail); tail -= head; head = 0;
    }
    const needed = tail + chunk.byteLength;
    if (needed > buffer.length) {
      let capacity = buffer.length;
      while (capacity < needed) capacity = Math.min(max, Math.max(capacity * 2, needed));
      const grown = new Uint8Array(capacity);
      grown.set(buffer.subarray(head, tail));
      tail -= head; head = 0; buffer = grown;
    }
    buffer.set(chunk, tail); tail += chunk.byteLength;
  };
  const take = length => {
    const count = Number(length);
    if (!Number.isInteger(count) || count < 0 || count > available()) throw new Error("byte queue underflow");
    // Return a view instead of allocating/copying every frame. Message handling
    // is serialized; the next append/compaction occurs after the caller has
    // finished its awaited decrypt operation on this view.
    const result = buffer.subarray(head, head + count);
    head += count;
    if (head === tail) { head = 0; tail = 0; }
    return result;
  };
  return { append, take, get available() { return available(); } };
}

function waitForBackpressure(ws, isStopped = () => false) {
  if (typeof ws?.bufferedAmount !== "number") return Promise.resolve();
  return (async () => {
    let value = Number(ws.bufferedAmount) || 0;
    if (value <= DOWNSTREAM_BACKPRESSURE_BYTES) return;
    const started = Date.now();
    while (value > DOWNSTREAM_BACKPRESSURE_RESUME_BYTES) {
      if (isStopped() || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) throw new Error("WebSocket closed during downstream backpressure");
      if (Date.now() - started > 30000) throw new Error("WebSocket downstream remained backpressured");
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 5));
      value = Number(ws.bufferedAmount) || 0;
    }
  })();
}

function adjustSmartBuffer(ws, current) {
  const amount = typeof ws?.bufferedAmount === "number" ? Number(ws.bufferedAmount) || 0 : 0;
  if (amount > 2 * 1024 * 1024) return Math.max(DOWNSTREAM_MIN_BUFFER_BYTES, Math.floor(current / 2));
  if (amount < 512 * 1024) return Math.min(DOWNSTREAM_MAX_BUFFER_BYTES, Math.max(current + DOWNSTREAM_MIN_BUFFER_BYTES, current * 2));
  return current;
}

function createDownstreamSender(ws, isStopped = () => false) {
  let targetBytes = DOWNSTREAM_MIN_BUFFER_BYTES;
  let chunks = [], size = 0, timer = null, tail = Promise.resolve(), failure = null, closed = false;
  const clearTimer = () => { if (timer) { clearTimeout(timer); timer = null; } };
  const flush = () => {
    clearTimer();
    if (failure) return Promise.reject(failure);
    if (!size) return tail;
    const payload = concatChunks(chunks); chunks = []; size = 0;
    const job = tail.then(async () => {
      if (closed || isStopped() || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) throw new Error("downstream WebSocket is closed");
      await waitForBackpressure(ws, isStopped);
      if (closed || isStopped() || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) throw new Error("downstream WebSocket is closed");
      ws.send(payload);
      targetBytes = adjustSmartBuffer(ws, targetBytes);
    });
    tail = job.catch(error => { failure = error; });
    return job;
  };
  const enqueue = chunk => {
    if (closed) return Promise.reject(new Error("downstream sender closed"));
    if (failure) return Promise.reject(failure);
    if (!chunk || !chunk.byteLength) return Promise.resolve();
    chunks.push(chunk); size += chunk.byteLength;
    if (size >= targetBytes || size >= DOWNSTREAM_MAX_BUFFER_BYTES) return flush();
    if (!timer) timer = setTimeout(() => { timer = null; void flush().catch(() => {}); }, DOWNSTREAM_FLUSH_DELAY_MS);
    return Promise.resolve();
  };
  const drain = async () => { await flush(); await tail; if (failure) throw failure; };
  const cancel = () => { closed = true; clearTimer(); chunks = []; size = 0; };
  return { enqueue, flush, drain, cancel };
}

function createWorkerReadableReader(readable, bufferSize = 64 * 1024) {
  try {
    const reader = readable.getReader({ mode: "byob" });
    let buffer = new Uint8Array(bufferSize);
    return {
      async read() {
        const result = await reader.read(buffer);
        if (!result.done) {
          const returnedBuffer = result.value?.buffer;
          buffer = returnedBuffer && returnedBuffer.byteLength >= 1024
            ? new Uint8Array(returnedBuffer)
            : new Uint8Array(bufferSize);
        }
        return result;
      },
      releaseLock() { try { reader.releaseLock(); } catch {} },
      byob: true
    };
  } catch {
    const reader = readable.getReader();
    return { read: () => reader.read(), releaseLock() { try { reader.releaseLock(); } catch {} }, byob: false };
  }
}

function createShadowsocksDownstreamSender(ws, key, nonce, isStopped = () => false) {
  const frameBuffer = new Uint8Array(0x3fff);
  let used = 0, timer = null, tail = Promise.resolve(), failure = null, closed = false;
  const clearTimer = () => { if (timer) { clearTimeout(timer); timer = null; } };
  const flush = () => {
    clearTimer();
    if (failure) return Promise.reject(failure);
    if (!used) return tail;
    const payload = frameBuffer.slice(0, used); used = 0;
    const job = tail.then(async () => {
      if (closed || isStopped() || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) throw new Error("Shadowsocks downstream WebSocket is closed");
      await sendShadowsocksEncrypted(ws, key, nonce, payload);
    });
    tail = job.catch(error => { failure = error; });
    return job;
  };
  const enqueue = async data => {
    if (closed) throw new Error("Shadowsocks downstream sender closed");
    if (failure) throw failure;
    let offset = 0;
    while (offset < data.byteLength) {
      const count = Math.min(frameBuffer.length - used, data.byteLength - offset);
      frameBuffer.set(data.subarray(offset, offset + count), used);
      used += count; offset += count;
      if (used === frameBuffer.length) await flush();
    }
    if (used && !timer) timer = setTimeout(() => { timer = null; void flush().catch(() => {}); }, DOWNSTREAM_FLUSH_DELAY_MS);
  };
  const cancel = () => { closed = true; clearTimer(); used = 0; };
  return { enqueue, flush, cancel };
}

async function pumpRemote(readable, ws, hooks, gen) {
  let receivedAny = false, pipeFailed = false;
  const sender = createDownstreamSender(ws, () => hooks.isStale(gen));
  const sink = new WritableStream({
    async write(value) {
      if (hooks.isStale(gen)) throw new Error("stale outbound stream");
      if (!value || !value.byteLength) return;
      if (!hooks.onRemoteBytes(value.byteLength)) throw new Error("traffic quota reached");
      if (!receivedAny) { receivedAny = true; hooks.onFirstByte(); }
      if (ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) throw new Error("downstream WebSocket is not open");
      await sender.enqueue(value);
    },
    async close() { await sender.flush(); },
    abort() { sender.cancel(); }
  });
  try { await readable.pipeTo(sink); }
  catch { pipeFailed = true; }
  if (pipeFailed && receivedAny && !hooks.isStale(gen)) { try { await sender.flush(); } catch {} }
  else if (pipeFailed && !receivedAny) sender.cancel();
  if (!receivedAny) { let recovered = false; try { recovered = await hooks.fallback(gen); } catch {} if (recovered) return; }
  if (hooks.isStale(gen)) return;
  hooks.closeAll();
}

function isTelegramDatacenterIp(host) {
  const value = String(host || "").replace(/^\[|\]$/g, "").toLowerCase();
  return /^(?:149\.154\.|91\.108\.|185\.76\.151\.|2001:b28:f23[dcef]:|2001:67c:4e8:)/i.test(value);
}

function buildOutboundRoutes(settings, preferredExitId = "") {
  const chains = [...(Array.isArray(settings.chainProxies) ? settings.chainProxies : []), settings.chainProxy].filter(Boolean);
  const proxies = [...(Array.isArray(settings.proxies) ? settings.proxies : []), settings.proxyIP].filter(Boolean);
  const routes = [];
  const exits = getActiveExitNodes(settings);
  if (preferredExitId === "auto") {
    exits.forEach(node => routes.push({ type: node.kind, value: node.uri, id: node.id, label: `${node.country} · ${node.name}` }));
  } else if (preferredExitId) {
    const selected = exits.find(node => node.id === preferredExitId);
    if (selected) routes.push({ type: selected.kind, value: selected.uri, id: selected.id, label: `${selected.country} · ${selected.name}` });
  }
  chains.forEach(chain => routes.push({ type: "chain", value: chain, label: "Chain Proxy" }));
  routes.push({ type: "direct", value: "", label: "Direct" });
  proxies.forEach(proxy => routes.push({ type: "proxy", value: proxy, label: "Proxy IP" }));
  return routes;
}

async function openOutbound(settings, host, port, initialData, afterRouteIndex = -1, preferredExitId = "") {
  const routes = buildOutboundRoutes(settings, preferredExitId);
  let lastError = null;
  const startIndex = Number.isInteger(Number(afterRouteIndex)) ? Number(afterRouteIndex) + 1 : 0;
  const directIndex = routes.findIndex(route => route.type === "direct");
  const telegramDirect = Number(port) === 443 && isTelegramDatacenterIp(host);
  // Telegram DC IPs on TCP/443 are forwarded immediately, without waiting for any
  // SNI/domain sniffing. This Worker does not otherwise sniff TLS; the destination
  // IP is already supplied by the inbound protocol. If direct egress fails, regular
  // configured routes remain available as fallback.
  if (telegramDirect && Number(afterRouteIndex) < directIndex) {
    try {
      const direct = await openDirect(host, port, initialData);
      return { ...direct, routeIndex: directIndex < 0 ? routes.length - 1 : directIndex, routeType: "telegram-direct", viaProxyIP: false };
    } catch (err) { lastError = err; }
  }
  for (let index = Math.max(0, startIndex); index < routes.length; index++) {
    const route = routes[index];
    if (telegramDirect && route.type === "direct") continue;
    try {
      let tunnel;
      if (route.type === "chain") tunnel = await openChainedWebSocket(route.value, host, port, initialData);
      else if (route.type === "socks5") tunnel = await openSocks5Exit(route.value, host, port, initialData);
      else if (route.type === "http") tunnel = await openHttpConnectExit(route.value, host, port, initialData);
      else if (route.type === "direct") tunnel = await openDirect(host, port, initialData);
      else {
        const target = parseHostPort(route.value, port);
        tunnel = await openDirect(target.host, target.port, initialData);
      }
      return { ...tunnel, routeIndex: index, routeType: route.type, viaProxyIP: route.type === "proxy" };
    } catch (err) { lastError = err; }
  }
  throw lastError || new Error("No outbound route is configured");
}

function makeBufferedSocketReader(reader) {
  let buffer = new Uint8Array(0);
  return {
    async readExact(length, timeoutMs = 8000) {
      const started = Date.now();
      while (buffer.length < length) {
        const left = timeoutMs - (Date.now() - started);
        if (left <= 0) throw new Error("proxy handshake timeout");
        let timer;
        let item;
        try {
          item = await Promise.race([reader.read(), new Promise((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("proxy handshake timeout")), left); })]);
        } finally { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); }
        if (item.done || !item.value?.byteLength) throw new Error("proxy closed during handshake");
        buffer = concatChunks([buffer, item.value]);
        if (buffer.length > 65536) throw new Error("proxy handshake response too large");
      }
      const result = buffer.slice(0, length);
      buffer = buffer.slice(length);
      return result;
    },
    async readUntil(marker, maxBytes = 16384, timeoutMs = 8000) {
      const needle = enc.encode(marker);
      const started = Date.now();
      while (true) {
        for (let i = 0; i <= buffer.length - needle.length; i++) {
          let same = true;
          for (let j = 0; j < needle.length; j++) if (buffer[i + j] !== needle[j]) { same = false; break; }
          if (same) {
            const end = i + needle.length;
            const result = buffer.slice(0, end);
            buffer = buffer.slice(end);
            return result;
          }
        }
        if (buffer.length >= maxBytes) throw new Error("proxy response header too large");
        const left = timeoutMs - (Date.now() - started);
        if (left <= 0) throw new Error("proxy handshake timeout");
        let timer; let item;
        try {
          item = await Promise.race([reader.read(), new Promise((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("proxy handshake timeout")), left); })]);
        } finally { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); }
        if (item.done || !item.value?.byteLength) throw new Error("proxy closed during handshake");
        buffer = concatChunks([buffer, item.value]);
      }
    },
    takeRemainder() { const value = buffer; buffer = new Uint8Array(0); return value; }
  };
}

function encodeSocks5Address(host, port) {
  let addr;
  if (isIPv4Text(host)) addr = new Uint8Array([1, ...host.split(".").map(Number)]);
  else if (isIPv6Text(host)) {
    const groups = expandIPv6(host), raw = new Uint8Array(17); raw[0] = 4;
    for (let i = 0; i < groups.length; i++) { raw[1 + i * 2] = (groups[i] >> 8) & 255; raw[2 + i * 2] = groups[i] & 255; }
    addr = raw;
  } else {
    const bytes = enc.encode(String(host));
    if (!bytes.length || bytes.length > 255) throw new Error("invalid SOCKS5 target domain");
    addr = new Uint8Array(2 + bytes.length); addr[0] = 3; addr[1] = bytes.length; addr.set(bytes, 2);
  }
  const out = new Uint8Array(addr.length + 2); out.set(addr); out[out.length - 2] = (port >> 8) & 255; out[out.length - 1] = port & 255;
  return out;
}

function socketReadableWithPrefix(prefix, readable) {
  if (!prefix || !prefix.length) return readable;
  const reader = readable.getReader(); let sent = false;
  return new ReadableStream({
    async pull(controller) {
      if (!sent) { sent = true; controller.enqueue(prefix); return; }
      try { const item = await reader.read(); if (item.done) controller.close(); else if (item.value?.byteLength) controller.enqueue(item.value); }
      catch (error) { controller.error(error); }
    },
    async cancel(reason) { try { await reader.cancel(reason); } catch {} }
  });
}

function safeDecodeUriComponent(value) {
  const raw = String(value || "");
  try { return decodeURIComponent(raw); }
  catch { return raw; }
}

function parseExitProxyUri(uri) {
  const normalizedUri = normalizeTelegramProxy(uri);
  const u = new URL(String(normalizedUri));
  const protocol = u.protocol.toLowerCase();
  if (!(["socks5:", "socks:", "http:"].includes(protocol))) throw new Error("unsupported Exit Node protocol");
  return {
    kind: protocol === "http:" ? "http" : "socks5",
    hostname: String(u.hostname || "").replace(/^\[|\]$/g, ""),
    port: Number(u.port || (protocol === "http:" ? 8080 : 1080)),
    username: safeDecodeUriComponent(u.username || ""),
    password: safeDecodeUriComponent(u.password || "")
  };
}

async function openSocks5Exit(proxyUri, targetHost, targetPort, initialData) {
  const proxy = parseExitProxyUri(proxyUri);
  const socket = connect({ hostname: proxy.hostname, port: proxy.port }, { secureTransport: "off", allowHalfOpen: true });
  const close = () => { try { Promise.resolve(socket.close()).catch(() => {}); } catch {} };
  let reader, writer, connectTimer;
  try {
    try {
      await Promise.race([socket.opened, new Promise((_, reject) => { connectTimer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("SOCKS5 connect timeout")), 10000); })]);
    } finally { if (connectTimer) clearTimeout(connectTimer); }
    reader = socket.readable.getReader(); writer = socket.writable.getWriter();
    const useAuth = !!(proxy.username || proxy.password);
    await writer.write(new Uint8Array(useAuth ? [5, 1, 2] : [5, 1, 0]));
    const buffered = makeBufferedSocketReader(reader);
    const methodReply = await buffered.readExact(2);
    if (methodReply[0] !== 5 || methodReply[1] === 255) throw new Error("SOCKS5 authentication method rejected");
    if (methodReply[1] === 2) {
      const user = enc.encode(proxy.username), pass = enc.encode(proxy.password);
      if (!user.length || user.length > 255 || pass.length > 255) throw new Error("SOCKS5 username/password length invalid");
      await writer.write(new Uint8Array([1, user.length, ...user, pass.length, ...pass]));
      const authReply = await buffered.readExact(2);
      if (authReply[0] !== 1 || authReply[1] !== 0) throw new Error("SOCKS5 authentication failed");
    } else if (methodReply[1] !== 0 || useAuth) throw new Error("SOCKS5 proxy did not accept configured authentication");
    await writer.write(concatChunks([new Uint8Array([5, 1, 0]), encodeSocks5Address(targetHost, targetPort)]));
    const head = await buffered.readExact(4);
    if (head[0] !== 5 || head[1] !== 0) throw new Error(`SOCKS5 CONNECT failed (${head[1]})`);
    const atyp = head[3];
    if (atyp === 1) await buffered.readExact(4);
    else if (atyp === 3) { const len = (await buffered.readExact(1))[0]; await buffered.readExact(len); }
    else if (atyp === 4) await buffered.readExact(16);
    else throw new Error("SOCKS5 returned an invalid address type");
    await buffered.readExact(2);
    if (initialData?.length) await writer.write(initialData);
    writer.releaseLock();
    const remainder = buffered.takeRemainder();
    // Release the handshake reader before creating the long-lived tunnel reader.
    reader.releaseLock();
    return { readable: socketReadableWithPrefix(remainder, socket.readable), writable: socket.writable, close };
  } catch (error) { try { writer?.releaseLock(); } catch {} try { reader?.releaseLock(); } catch {} close(); throw error; }
}

async function openHttpConnectExit(proxyUri, targetHost, targetPort, initialData) {
  const proxy = parseExitProxyUri(proxyUri);
  const socket = connect({ hostname: proxy.hostname, port: proxy.port }, { secureTransport: "off", allowHalfOpen: true });
  const close = () => { try { Promise.resolve(socket.close()).catch(() => {}); } catch {} };
  let reader, writer, connectTimer;
  try {
    try {
      await Promise.race([socket.opened, new Promise((_, reject) => { connectTimer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("HTTP proxy connect timeout")), 10000); })]);
    } finally { if (connectTimer) clearTimeout(connectTimer); }
    reader = socket.readable.getReader(); writer = socket.writable.getWriter();
    const authority = (isIPv6Text(targetHost) ? `[${targetHost}]` : targetHost) + ":" + targetPort;
    const headers = [`CONNECT ${authority} HTTP/1.1`, `Host: ${authority}`, "Proxy-Connection: Keep-Alive"];
    if (proxy.username || proxy.password) headers.push("Proxy-Authorization: Basic " + base64Utf8Standard(proxy.username + ":" + proxy.password));
    await writer.write(enc.encode(headers.join("\r\n") + "\r\n\r\n"));
    const buffered = makeBufferedSocketReader(reader);
    const response = dec.decode(await buffered.readUntil("\r\n\r\n", 16384));
    const statusLine = response.split("\r\n", 1)[0];
    if (!/^HTTP\/1\.[01] 2\d\d(?:\s|$)/.test(statusLine)) throw new Error("HTTP CONNECT rejected: " + statusLine.slice(0, 100));
    if (initialData?.length) await writer.write(initialData);
    writer.releaseLock();
    const remainder = buffered.takeRemainder();
    // Release the handshake reader before creating the long-lived tunnel reader.
    reader.releaseLock();
    return { readable: socketReadableWithPrefix(remainder, socket.readable), writable: socket.writable, close };
  } catch (error) { try { writer?.releaseLock(); } catch {} try { reader?.releaseLock(); } catch {} close(); throw error; }
}

async function openDirect(host, port, initialData) {
  const socket = connect({ hostname: host, port }, { secureTransport: "off", allowHalfOpen: true });
  const close = () => { try { Promise.resolve(socket.close()).catch(()=>{}); } catch {} };
  try {
    let timer; const timeout = new Promise((_, r) => { timer = setTimeout(() => r(new Error("timeout")), 10000); });
    try { await Promise.race([socket.opened, timeout]); } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    if (initialData && initialData.length) { const w = socket.writable.getWriter(); try { await w.write(initialData); } finally { w.releaseLock(); } }
  } catch (err) { close(); throw err; }
  return { readable: socket.readable, writable: socket.writable, close };
}

function handleUdpDnsPackets(chunk, state, dohUrl, ws) {
  state.pending = concatBytes(state.pending, chunk);
  while (state.pending.length >= 2) {
    const len = (state.pending[0] << 8) | state.pending[1];
    if (state.pending.length < 2 + len) break;
    const packet = state.pending.slice(2, 2 + len); state.pending = state.pending.slice(2 + len);
    if (!len) continue;
    if (state.inflight >= UDP_MAX_INFLIGHT) continue;
    state.inflight++;
    forwardDnsQuery(packet, state, dohUrl, ws).catch(()=>{}).finally(() => { state.inflight--; });
  }
}

function handleTrojanUdpDnsPackets(chunk, state, dohUrl, ws) {
  if (state.pending.length + chunk.length > 262144) throw new Error("Trojan UDP buffer limit exceeded");
  state.pending = concatBytes(state.pending, chunk);
  while (state.pending.length) {
    const data = state.pending;
    const type = data[0];
    let addressMinimum;
    if (type === 1) addressMinimum = 5;
    else if (type === 3) {
      if (data.length < 2) break;
      if (!data[1]) throw new Error("invalid Trojan UDP domain length");
      addressMinimum = 2 + data[1];
    } else if (type === 4) addressMinimum = 17;
    else throw new Error("invalid Trojan UDP address type");
    if (data.length < addressMinimum) break;

    const address = parseTrojanAddress(data, 0);
    const p = address.next;
    if (data.length < p + 6) break;
    const port = (data[p] << 8) | data[p + 1];
    const len = (data[p + 2] << 8) | data[p + 3];
    if (data[p + 4] !== 13 || data[p + 5] !== 10) throw new Error("invalid Trojan UDP CRLF");
    const total = p + 6 + len;
    if (data.length < total) break;
    const packet = data.slice(p + 6, total);
    state.pending = data.slice(total);
    if (!packet.length) continue;
    if (port !== 53) throw new Error("Trojan UDP only supports DNS on port 53");
    if (state.inflight >= UDP_MAX_INFLIGHT) continue;
    state.inflight++;
    forwardDnsQuery(packet, state, dohUrl, ws, { host: address.host, port }).catch(()=>{}).finally(() => { state.inflight--; });
  }
}

function encodeTrojanUdpPacket(host, port, payload) {
  let address;
  if (isIPv4Text(host)) {
    address = new Uint8Array([1, ...host.split(".").map(Number)]);
  } else if (isIPv6Text(host)) {
    const groups = expandIPv6(host), raw = new Uint8Array(17);
    raw[0] = 4;
    for (let i = 0; i < groups.length; i++) { raw[1 + i * 2] = (groups[i] >> 8) & 255; raw[2 + i * 2] = groups[i] & 255; }
    address = raw;
  } else {
    const bytes = enc.encode(String(host));
    if (!bytes.length || bytes.length > 255) throw new Error("invalid Trojan UDP response host");
    address = new Uint8Array(2 + bytes.length); address[0] = 3; address[1] = bytes.length; address.set(bytes, 2);
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535 || !payload.length || payload.length > 65535) throw new Error("invalid Trojan UDP response");
  const out = new Uint8Array(address.length + 6 + payload.length);
  out.set(address, 0);
  const p = address.length;
  out[p] = (port >> 8) & 255; out[p + 1] = port & 255;
  out[p + 2] = (payload.length >> 8) & 255; out[p + 3] = payload.length & 255;
  out[p + 4] = 13; out[p + 5] = 10; out.set(payload, p + 6);
  return out;
}

async function fetchDohAnswer(packet, state, dohUrl) {
  // A Worker invocation may issue only a limited number of subrequests (50 on the Free
  // plan). Long-lived tunnels that keep resolving names would otherwise hit the cap and
  // throw; instead the budget is tracked, the condition is logged once, and further
  // lookups are dropped (clients simply retry/fail over) while the tunnel stays open.
  if (state.dohBudget === undefined) state.dohBudget = DNS_DOH_SUBREQUEST_LIMIT;
  if (state.dohBudget <= 0) {
    if (!state.dohBudgetLogged) { state.dohBudgetLogged = true; console.warn("DoH subrequest budget exhausted; dropping further DNS queries for this tunnel."); }
    return null;
  }
  state.dohBudget--;
  const ctrl = new AbortController(); const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 8000);
  let ans;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(dohUrl || DEFAULT_DOH_URL, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/dns-message", "accept": "application/dns-message" }, body: packet, signal: ctrl.signal });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error("DoH HTTP " + res.status);
    ans = new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer());
  } catch (err) {
    if (/subrequest/i.test(String(err && err.message || err))) {
      state.dohBudget = 0;
      if (!state.dohBudgetLogged) { state.dohBudgetLogged = true; console.warn("Worker subrequest limit reached while resolving DNS:", String(err.message || err).slice(0, 120)); }
      return null;
    }
    throw err;
  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
  return ans;
}

async function forwardDnsQuery(packet, state, dohUrl, ws, trojanSource = null) {
  const cacheKey = dohCacheKey(packet, dohUrl);
  let ans = cacheKey ? dohCacheGet(cacheKey) : null;
  if (ans) {
    // Cache hit: no subrequest, just restore this query's transaction ID.
    ans[0] = packet[0]; ans[1] = packet[1];
  } else {
    ans = await fetchDohAnswer(packet, state, dohUrl);
    if (!ans) return;
    if (cacheKey && ans.length && ans.length <= 65535) dohCachePut(cacheKey, ans);
  }
  if (!ans.length || ans.length > 65535) throw new Error();
  if (ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;
  if (typeof state.onTraffic === "function" && !state.onTraffic(ans.byteLength)) return;
  if (state.mode === "trojan") {
    if (!trojanSource) return;
    ws.send(encodeTrojanUdpPacket(trojanSource.host, trojanSource.port, ans));
    return;
  }
  const head = state.header; state.header = null; const headLen = head ? head.length : 0;
  const out = new Uint8Array(headLen + 2 + ans.length);
  if (head) out.set(head, 0);
  out[headLen] = (ans.length >> 8) & 255; out[headLen + 1] = ans.length & 255;
  out.set(ans, headLen + 2); ws.send(out);
}

function parseVlessRequest(bytes) {
  if (!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array) || bytes.length < 24) throw new Error("invalid VLESS header");
  const version = bytes[0], gotUuid = bytesToUuid(bytes.slice(1, 17));
  if (!UUID_RE.test(gotUuid)) throw new Error("invalid VLESS UUID");
  let p = 18 + bytes[17];
  if (p + 4 > bytes.length) throw new Error("truncated VLESS header");
  const command = bytes[p++];
  if (command !== 1 && command !== 2) throw new Error("unsupported VLESS command");
  const port = (bytes[p++] << 8) | bytes[p++];
  if (!port) throw new Error("invalid destination port");
  const addr = parseAddress(bytes, p); p = addr.next;
  if (command === 2 && port !== 53) throw new Error("Only DNS UDP on port 53 is supported");
  return { version, uuid: gotUuid, host: addr.host, port, data: bytes.slice(p), udp: command === 2 };
}

function parseTrojanRequest(bytes) {
  if (!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array) || bytes.length < 65) throw new Error("invalid Trojan header");
  const passwordHash = dec.decode(bytes.slice(0, 56)).toLowerCase();
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{56}$/.test(passwordHash)) throw new Error("invalid Trojan credential hash");
  if (bytes[56] !== 13 || bytes[57] !== 10) throw new Error("invalid Trojan CRLF");
  let p = 58;
  const command = bytes[p++];
  if (command !== 1 && command !== 3) throw new Error("unsupported Trojan command");
  const addr = parseTrojanAddress(bytes, p); p = addr.next;
  if (p + 4 > bytes.length) throw new Error("truncated Trojan destination");
  const port = (bytes[p++] << 8) | bytes[p++];
  if (command === 1 && !port) throw new Error("invalid destination port");
  if (bytes[p] !== 13 || bytes[p + 1] !== 10) throw new Error("invalid Trojan trailing CRLF");
  p += 2;
  return { passwordHash, host: addr.host, port, data: bytes.slice(p), udp: command === 3 };
}

function parseTrojanAddress(bytes, position) {
  if (!Number.isInteger(position) || position < 0 || position >= bytes.length) throw new Error("missing Trojan address type");
  let p = position;
  const type = bytes[p++];
  if (type === 1) {
    if (p + 4 > bytes.length) throw new Error("truncated Trojan IPv4 address");
    return { host: Array.from(bytes.slice(p, p + 4)).join("."), next: p + 4 };
  }
  if (type === 3) {
    if (p >= bytes.length) throw new Error("missing Trojan domain length");
    const len = bytes[p++];
    if (len < 1 || p + len > bytes.length) throw new Error("truncated Trojan domain address");
    const host = dec.decode(bytes.slice(p, p + len));
    if (!isDomainName(host) && !isIPv4Text(host)) throw new Error("invalid Trojan domain address");
    return { host, next: p + len };
  }
  if (type === 4) {
    if (p + 16 > bytes.length) throw new Error("truncated Trojan IPv6 address");
    const groups = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i += 2) groups.push(((bytes[p + i] << 8) | bytes[p + i + 1]).toString(16));
    return { host: groups.join(":"), next: p + 16 };
  }
  throw new Error("unsupported Trojan address type");
}

function parseAddress(bytes, position) {
  if (!Number.isInteger(position) || position < 0 || position >= bytes.length) throw new Error("missing address type");
  let p = position;
  const type = bytes[p++];
  if (type === 1) {
    if (p + 4 > bytes.length) throw new Error("truncated IPv4 address");
    return { host: Array.from(bytes.slice(p, p + 4)).join("."), next: p + 4 };
  }
  if (type === 2) {
    if (p >= bytes.length) throw new Error("missing domain length");
    const len = bytes[p++];
    if (len < 1 || p + len > bytes.length) throw new Error("truncated domain address");
    const host = dec.decode(bytes.slice(p, p + len));
    if (!isDomainName(host) && !isIPv4Text(host)) throw new Error("invalid domain address");
    return { host, next: p + len };
  }
  if (type === 3) {
    if (p + 16 > bytes.length) throw new Error("truncated IPv6 address");
    const groups = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i += 2) groups.push(((bytes[p + i] << 8) | bytes[p + i + 1]).toString(16));
    return { host: groups.join(":"), next: p + 16 };
  }
  throw new Error("unsupported address type");
}

function bytesToUuid(bytes) {
  const h = Array.from(bytes).map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
  return h.slice(0, 8) + "-" + h.slice(8, 12) + "-" + h.slice(12, 16) + "-" + h.slice(16, 20) + "-" + h.slice(20);
}

async function openChainedWebSocket(chainUri, targetHost, targetPort, initialData) {
  validateChainUri(chainUri);
  const u = new URL(chainUri), scheme = u.protocol === "trojan:" ? "trojan" : "vless", security = (u.searchParams.get("security") || "tls").toLowerCase();
  const port = Number(u.port || (security === "tls" ? 443 : 80));
  const wsPath = u.searchParams.get("path") || "/ws";
  const wsUrl = (security === "tls" ? "wss://" : "ws://") + uriHost(u.hostname) + ":" + port + (wsPath.startsWith("/") ? wsPath : "/" + wsPath);
  const handshake = scheme === "vless" ? buildVlessUpstreamHeader(decodeURIComponent(u.username), targetHost, targetPort) : buildTrojanUpstreamHeader(decodeURIComponent(u.username), targetHost, targetPort);
  const ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl); ws.binaryType = "arraybuffer";
  let controller, openedResolve, openedReject, opened = false, closed = false, firstResponse = scheme === "vless", responsePrefix = new Uint8Array(0);
  const openedPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => { openedResolve = resolve; openedReject = reject; });
  const readable = new ReadableStream({ start(c) { controller = c; }, cancel() { try { ws.close(); } catch {} } });
  ws.addEventListener("open", () => { try { ws.send(handshake); if (initialData && initialData.length) ws.send(initialData); opened = true; openedResolve(); } catch (err) { openedReject(err); } });
  ws.addEventListener("message", async ev => {
    try {
      let b = await toBytes(ev.data);
      if (firstResponse) { responsePrefix = concatBytes(responsePrefix, b); if (responsePrefix.length < 2) return; const hSize = 2 + responsePrefix[1]; if (responsePrefix.length < hSize) return; b = responsePrefix.slice(hSize); responsePrefix = new Uint8Array(0); firstResponse = false; }
      if (b.length && controller) controller.enqueue(b);
    } catch (err) { try { controller?.error(err); } catch {} }
  });
  ws.addEventListener("error", () => { if (!opened) openedReject(new Error("err")); });
  ws.addEventListener("close", () => { closed = true; try { controller?.close(); } catch {} });
  const timeout = new Promise((_, r) => setTimeout(() => r(new Error("to")), 10000));
  await Promise.race([openedPromise, timeout]);
  if (!opened || closed) throw new Error("fail");
  const writable = new WritableStream({ write(chunk) { if (ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) ws.send(chunk); }, close() { try { ws.close(); } catch {} } });
  return { readable, writable, close: () => { try { ws.close(); } catch {} } };
}

function buildVlessUpstreamHeader(uuid, host, port) {
  const u = new Uint8Array(16); const hex = uuid.replace(/-/g, ""); for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) u[i] = parseInt(hex.slice(i * 2, i * 2 + 2), 16);
  const addr = encodeAddress(host), out = new Uint8Array(1 + 16 + 1 + 1 + 2 + addr.length);
  let p = 0; out[p++] = 0; out.set(u, p); p += 16; out[p++] = 0; out[p++] = 1; out[p++] = (port >> 8) & 255; out[p++] = port & 255; out.set(addr, p);
  return out;
}
function buildTrojanUpstreamHeader(password, host, port) {
  const hash = enc.encode(sha224Hex(password) + "\r\n"), addr = encodeTrojanAddress(host), out = new Uint8Array(hash.length + 1 + addr.length + 2 + 2);
  let p = 0; out.set(hash, p); p += hash.length; out[p++] = 1; out.set(addr, p); p += addr.length; out[p++] = (port >> 8) & 255; out[p++] = port & 255; out[p++] = 13; out[p++] = 10;
  return out;
}
// Trojan uses SOCKS5 address types: 1 = IPv4, 3 = domain, 4 = IPv6 (VLESS uses 1/2/3).
function encodeTrojanAddress(host) {
  const value = String(host || "");
  if (isIPv4Text(value)) return new Uint8Array([1, ...value.split(".").map(Number)]);
  if (value.includes(":")) {
    const groups = expandIPv6(value.replace(/^\[|\]$/g, "")), out = new Uint8Array(17);
    out[0] = 4;
    groups.forEach((n, i) => { out[1 + i * 2] = (n >> 8) & 255; out[2 + i * 2] = n & 255; });
    return out;
  }
  const data = enc.encode(value);
  if (!data.length || data.length > 255) throw new Error("invalid Trojan upstream domain");
  return new Uint8Array([3, data.length, ...data]);
}
function encodeAddress(host) {
  if (/^\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,3}){3}$/.test(host)) return new Uint8Array([1, ...host.split(".").map(Number)]);
  if (host.includes(":")) { const groups = expandIPv6(host); const out = new Uint8Array(17); out[0] = 3; groups.forEach((n, i) => { out[1 + i * 2] = (n >> 8) & 255; out[2 + i * 2] = n & 255; }); return out; }
  const data = enc.encode(host); return new Uint8Array([2, data.length, ...data]);
}
function sha224HexUncached(text) {
  const bytes = enc.encode(String(text)), bitLen = bytes.length * 8, total = Math.ceil((bytes.length + 9) / 64) * 64, msg = new Uint8Array(total);
  msg.set(bytes); msg[bytes.length] = 0x80; const dv = new DataView(msg.buffer); dv.setUint32(total - 8, Math.floor(bitLen / 0x100000000)); dv.setUint32(total - 4, bitLen >>> 0);
  const K = SHA256_K, H = [0xc1059ed8,0x367cd507,0x3070dd17,0xf70e5939,0xffc00b31,0x68581511,0x64f98fa7,0xbefa4fa4], w = new Uint32Array(64), rotr = (x,n) => (x >>> n) | (x << (32-n));
  for (let off = 0; off < total; off += 64) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) w[i] = dv.getUint32(off + i * 4);
    for (let i = 16; i < 64; i++) { const x = w[i-15], y = w[i-2], s0 = rotr(x,7) ^ rotr(x,18) ^ (x >>> 3), s1 = rotr(y,17) ^ rotr(y,19) ^ (y >>> 10); w[i] = (w[i-16] + s0 + w[i-7] + s1) >>> 0; }
    let [a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h] = H;
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) { const S1 = rotr(e,6) ^ rotr(e,11) ^ rotr(e,25), ch = (e & f) ^ (~e & g), t1 = (h + S1 + ch + K[i] + w[i]) >>> 0, S0 = rotr(a,2) ^ rotr(a,13) ^ rotr(a,22), maj = (a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c), t2 = (S0 + maj) >>> 0; h = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) >>> 0; d = c; c = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) >>> 0; }
    H[0]=(H[0]+a)>>>0; H[1]=(H[1]+b)>>>0; H[2]=(H[2]+c)>>>0; H[3]=(H[3]+d)>>>0; H[4]=(H[4]+e)>>>0; H[5]=(H[5]+f)>>>0; H[6]=(H[6]+g)>>>0; H[7]=(H[7]+h)>>>0;
  }
  return H.slice(0,7).map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")).join("");
}
function sha224Hex(text) {
  const key = String(text);
  const cached = trojanHashCache.get(key);
  if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) return cached.value;
  const value = sha224HexUncached(key);
  trojanHashCache.set(key, { value, expiresAt: Date.now() + 30 * 60 * 1000 });
  while (trojanHashCache.size > TROJAN_HASH_CACHE_MAX) trojanHashCache.delete(trojanHashCache.keys().next().value);
  return value;
}

const SHA256_K = [0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2];

Worker برای هر کانفیگ Exit Node از مسیر مشخص‌شده استفاده می‌کند و در صورت خرابی آن، مسیرهای قدیمی Direct/Chain Proxy همچنان به‌عنوان fallback باقی می‌مانند.</p>

            <label for="cfBootstrapToken">Bootstrap Token (برای ساخت API Token دائمی)</label>
            <div class="cf-token-row"><input id="cfBootstrapToken" type="password" dir="ltr" maxlength="512" autocomplete="new-password" placeholder="Paste Cloudflare Bootstrap Token"><button type="button" class="secondary" id="toggleCfBootstrapTokenVisibilityBtn">نمایش</button></div>
            <p class="small-note">می‌توانید این مقدار را فقط در همین صفحه وارد کنید؛ به Worker، KV یا D1 ذخیره نمی‌شود. در صورت خالی بودن، Worker ابتدا Secret با نام <code>CF_BOOTSTRAP_TOKEN</code> و سپس <code>CF_API_TOKEN</code> را امتحان می‌کند؛ توکن جایگزین باید مجوز <code>Account → Account API Tokens → Edit</code> روی همین حساب داشته باشد.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="cf-placement-actions">
            <button type="button" class="secondary" id="refreshCfCredentialsBtn">تشخیص خودکار مشخصات</button>
            <button type="button" class="secondary" id="createCfApiTokenBtn">ساخت API Token دائمی با مجوز Workers Scripts Write</button>
          </div>
          <div id="cfApiTokenResult" class="cf-api-token-result hidden">
            <label for="cfNewApiToken">API Token دائمی (Secret فقط هنگام ساخت نمایش داده می‌شود)</label>
            <div class="cf-token-row"><input id="cfNewApiToken" type="password" dir="ltr" readonly autocomplete="off"><button type="button" class="secondary" id="toggleCfTokenVisibilityBtn">نمایش</button><button type="button" id="copyCfApiTokenBtn">کپی Token</button></div>
            <p class="small-note" id="cfApiTokenExpiryNote">Token دائمی است و تا زمان لغو دستی در Cloudflare اعتبار دارد. برای استفاده پس از بستن صفحه، آن را به‌عنوان Secret با نام CF_API_TOKEN در تنظیمات Worker ذخیره کنید.</p>
          </div>
          <p class="small-note">Bootstrap Token باید برای ساخت Account-owned Token مجوز <code>Account &gt; Account API Tokens &gt; Edit</code> روی همین حساب داشته باشد. برای ساخت Token Placement، مجوز <code>Workers Scripts Write</code> فقط روی همین Account اعمال می‌شود. Cloudflare مقدار Secret را فقط هنگام ساخت برمی‌گرداند؛ آن را کپی کرده و در Worker → Settings → Variables and Secrets با نام <code>CF_API_TOKEN</code> به‌صورت Secret ذخیره کنید. Worker نمی‌تواند Secretهای محیط خودش را از داخل کد تغییر دهد. تا وقتی صفحه باز است، Token ساخته‌شده برای Apply نیز در حافظهٔ همان صفحه استفاده می‌شود و در KV/D1 ذخیره نمی‌شود. اگر Account ID به‌صورت خودکار پیدا نشد، آن را دستی وارد کنید؛ قالب Bootstrap معمولاً مجوز Account Read برای فهرست‌کردن حساب‌ها ندارد.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="user-form-actions"><button type="button" id="applyWorkerPlacementBtn">ذخیره و اعمال محل اجرا</button></div>
      </div>

    <section id="sec-telegram" class="panel-section">
      <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(41,98,255,.14),rgba(21,54,109,.10));border-color:rgba(56,189,248,.34)">
        <div class="section-head">
          <div>
            <h2>Telegram Web Proxy (TWP-CF)</h2>
            <p class="small-note">TWP-CF یک WebSocket Web Proxy برای کلاینت تلگرامی است که WebView Bridge بومی را پیاده‌سازی کرده باشد (Desktop و برخی buildهای Android سازگار). این لینک پروکسی معمولی MTProto یا SOCKS5 نیست و در صفحهٔ تنظیمات Proxy هر نسخهٔ تلگرام موبایل کار نمی‌کند؛ برای استفادهٔ عادی در اپلیکیشن موبایل، از SOCKS5 پایین همین صفحه استفاده کنید. نشست‌ها و وضعیت relay فقط در حافظهٔ isolate نگهداری می‌شوند.</p>
            <div class="notice-box" style="margin-top:10px">راهنمای استفاده: لینک «صفحهٔ Bridge» را فقط در Telegram client دارای WebView Bridge باز کنید. صفحهٔ Bridge خودش دکمهٔ Import عمومی ندارد؛ نمایش/تأیید اتصال را کلاینت سازگار انجام می‌دهد. اگر تلگرام لینک را به‌عنوان Web Proxy نشناخت یا صفحه را در WebView اختصاصی باز نکرد، این قابلیت روی آن build در دسترس نیست و باید از SOCKS5 استفاده کنید. تغییر server به IP دلخواه پشتیبانی نمی‌شود، چون امضای Bridge و TLS به hostname دامنه متصل است.</div>
          </div>
          <span class="pill off" id="twpStatusPill">در انتظار بررسی</span>
        </div>
        <div class="field" style="margin-top:14px">
          <label for="twpBridgeSecret">BRIDGE_SECRET (۳۲ کاراکتر hex)</label>
          <div class="inline-control">
            <input id="twpBridgeSecret" type="text" maxlength="32" minlength="32" dir="ltr" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="با فعال‌سازی به‌صورت امن تولید می‌شود">
            <button type="button" class="secondary" id="twpCopySecretBtn">کپی</button>
          </div>
          <p class="small-note" style="margin-top:6px">کلید محرمانه در جدول مستقل D1 پنل (یا KV در نبود D1) ذخیره می‌شود؛ مقدار BOOT جداگانه و غیرقابل‌نمایش تولید می‌شود.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="user-form-actions" style="margin-top:12px">
          <button type="button" id="twpEnableBtn">فعال‌سازی</button>
          <button type="button" class="secondary" id="twpDisableBtn">غیرفعال‌سازی</button>

        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card telegram-hero">
        <div class="section-head"><div><h2>پروکسی تلگرام (Telegram Proxy)</h2><p class="small-note">برای هر Exit Node فعال از نوع SOCKS5، لینک استاندارد tg://socks و لینک جایگزین t.me ساخته می‌شود. خود تلگرام مستقیماً به سرور SOCKS5 واقعی متصل می‌شود؛ دامنه Worker فقط صفحه راهنمای اتصال را ارائه می‌کند.</p></div><span class="pill">SOCKS5</span></div>
        <div class="telegram-toolbar"><select id="telegramQuickSelect" aria-label="انتخاب پروکسی تلگرام"></select><button type="button" id="telegramQuickConnect">اتصال مستقیم به تلگرام</button><a class="secondary" id="telegramQuickWeb" href="https://t.me/socks" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Open in Telegram Web</a><button type="button" class="secondary" id="telegramTestActiveBtn">تست اتصال پروکسی‌های فعال</button></div>

<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
function assertSafeUrl(value) {
  try {
    const u = new URL(String(value || ""));
    return u.protocol === "https:" || u.protocol === "http:";
  } catch { return false; }
}
function normalizeTelegramProxy(rawUri) {
  const original = String(rawUri == null ? "" : rawUri);
  const input = original.trim().replace(/&amp;/gi, "&");
  const match = /^(?:tg:\/\/socks|https?:\/\/t\.me\/socks|t\.me\/socks)\/?(?:\?([^#]*))?(?:#.*)?$/i.exec(input);
  if (!match || match[1] === undefined) return original;

  // URLSearchParams performs exactly one standards-compliant decode. Do not call
  // decodeURIComponent on these values again: doing so corrupts literal '%' data.
  let params;
  try { params = new URLSearchParams(match[1]); } catch { return original; }
  const serverValue = String(params.get("server") || "").trim();
  const portText = String(params.get("port") || "").trim();
  const username = String(params.get("user") || "");
  const password = String(params.get("pass") || "");
  if (!serverValue || !/^\d{1,5}$/.test(portText)) return original;
  const port = Number(portText);
  if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) return original;

  // Accept bracketed or accidentally multiply-bracketed IPv6 input, normalize it
  // to the single bracket pair required in a URI authority.
  const server = serverValue.replace(/^\[+/, "").replace(/\]+$/, "");
  if (!server || /[\s\/?#@\\]/.test(server)) return original;
  let host = server;
  if (server.includes(":")) {
    try { new URL("http://[" + server + "]/" ); } catch { return original; }
    host = "[" + server + "]";
  } else {
    if (!/^[a-z0-9.-]+$/i.test(server) || server.startsWith(".") || server.endsWith(".") || server.includes("..")) return original;
  }
  const auth = (username !== "" || password !== "")
    ? encodeURIComponent(username) + ":" + encodeURIComponent(password) + "@"
    : "";
  return "socks5://" + auth + host + ":" + port;
}

function updatePanelTitle(targetId) {
  const username = String(globalSettings.username || 'admin').trim() || 'admin';
  const tabTitle = TAB_TITLES[targetId] || 'پنل مدیریت';
  $('pageTitle').textContent = username + ' — ' + tabTitle;
  document.title = username + ' — ' + tabTitle + ' | VISION UI Panel';
}

function switchTab(targetId) {
  const section = $(targetId);
  if (!section || !section.classList.contains('panel-section')) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('.panel-section').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
  section.classList.add('active');
  const menuItem = document.querySelector('.menu-item[data-target="' + targetId + '"]');
  if (menuItem) menuItem.classList.add('active');
  updatePanelTitle(targetId);
}

document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(item => {
  item.onclick = () => switchTab(item.getAttribute('data-target'));
});

async function api(path, opts = {}) {
  const res = await fetch(path, { credentials: 'same-origin', ...opts, headers: { ...(opts.headers||{}), ...(opts.body?{'content-type':'application/json'}:{}) }});
  let body = {}; try { body = await res.json(); } catch {}
  if (!res.ok) {
    const errInfo = ERROR_DICT[body.code];
    const msg = body.code === 'ERR_VALIDATION' && body.error
      ? String(body.error)
      : (errInfo ? errInfo.msg + " " + errInfo.action : (body.error || 'خطای ناشناخته'));
    const error = new Error(msg);
    error.status = res.status;
    error.code = body.code || '';
    throw error;
  }
  return body;
}

let toastTimer = null;
function showStatus(msg, isError = false) {
  const el = $('globalStatus');
  el.textContent = (isError ? "✕ " : "✓ ") + msg;
  el.className = 'status-msg ' + (isError ? 'error' : '');
  el.classList.remove('hidden');
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => el.classList.add('hidden'), 4500);
}

// -------------------- TWP-CF panel UI --------------------
let twpProxyState = { enabled: false, status: 'not_created', secret: '', host: '', links: {} };
async function refreshTwpProxy() {
  try {
    twpProxyState = await api('/api/twp/config');
    renderTwpProxy();
  } catch (err) {
    const status = $('twpStatusPill');
    if (status) { status.textContent = 'خطا در دریافت وضعیت'; status.className = 'pill off'; }
    if (err?.message) showStatus(err.message, true);
  }
}
function renderTwpProxy() {
  const pill = $('twpStatusPill');
  if (pill) {
    pill.textContent = twpProxyState.status === 'active' ? 'فعال' : twpProxyState.status === 'stopped' ? 'غیرفعال' : 'ایجاد نشده';
    pill.className = 'pill' + (twpProxyState.enabled ? '' : ' off');
  }
  const secretInput = $('twpBridgeSecret');
  if (secretInput && twpProxyState.secret) secretInput.value = twpProxyState.secret;
  const links = $('twpLinks');
  if (!links) return;
  links.replaceChildren();
  if (!twpProxyState.enabled || !twpProxyState.links?.tg || !twpProxyState.links?.webBridge) {
    const note = document.createElement('p'); note.className = 'small-note';
    note.textContent = 'برای نمایش لینک‌های اتصال، ابتدا TWP-CF را فعال کنید.';
    links.appendChild(note); return;
  }
  const add = (label, value, href, openable) => {
    const row = document.createElement('div'); row.className = 'linkrow'; row.style.marginTop = '8px';
    const title = document.createElement('label'); title.textContent = label;
    const code = document.createElement('code'); code.textContent = value; code.style.direction = 'ltr'; code.style.textAlign = 'left'; code.style.overflowWrap = 'anywhere';
    row.append(title, code);
    const copy = document.createElement('button'); copy.type = 'button'; copy.className = 'secondary'; copy.textContent = 'کپی';
    copy.addEventListener('click', async () => { try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value); showStatus('لینک کپی شد.'); } catch { showStatus('کپی خودکار مجاز نیست.', true); } });
    row.appendChild(copy);
    if (openable && href) {
      const open = document.createElement('a'); open.className = 'secondary'; open.href = href; open.target = '_blank'; open.rel = 'noopener noreferrer'; open.textContent = 'باز کردن'; row.appendChild(open);
    }
    links.appendChild(row);
  };
  add('صفحهٔ Bridge برای WebView پشتیبانی‌شده', twpProxyState.links.webBridge, twpProxyState.links.webBridge, true);
  add('لینک افزودن WEB Proxy (فقط کلاینت سازگار)', twpProxyState.links.tg, twpProxyState.links.tg, true);
  const guide = document.createElement('p'); guide.className = 'small-note';
  guide.textContent = 'ابتدا لینک Bridge را در کلاینتی باز کنید که TelegramWebProxy / WebView carrier را پشتیبانی می‌کند. این قابلیت را به‌عنوان SOCKS5 یا MTProto معمولی وارد نکنید؛ برای کلاینت موبایل فاقد این قابلیت، لینک SOCKS5 را انتخاب کنید.';
  links.appendChild(guide);
}
async function twpProxyAction(action) {
  try {
    const secret = $('twpBridgeSecret')?.value.trim() || '';
    const data = await api('/api/twp/config', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ action, secret }) });
    twpProxyState = data; renderTwpProxy();
    showStatus(action === 'disable' ? 'Telegram Web Proxy غیرفعال شد.' : action === 'regenerate' ? 'Secret جدید ساخته شد؛ لینک‌های قبلی باطل شدند.' : 'Telegram Web Proxy فعال شد.');
  } catch (err) { showStatus(err.message || 'عملیات TWP-CF ناموفق بود.', true); }
}
$('twpEnableBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => void twpProxyAction('enable'));
$('twpDisableBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => void twpProxyAction('disable'));
$('twpRegenerateBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (confirm('Secret جدید تولید شود؟ لینک‌های قبلی دیگر کار نخواهند کرد.')) void twpProxyAction('regenerate');
});
$('twpRefreshBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => { void refreshTwpProxy(); });
$('twpCopySecretBtn')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const value = $('twpBridgeSecret')?.value || '';
  if (!value) return showStatus('BRIDGE_SECRET هنوز تولید نشده است.', true);
  try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value); showStatus('BRIDGE_SECRET کپی شد.'); }
  catch { showStatus('کپی خودکار مجاز نیست.', true); }
});
